#!/bin/bash
# Build the given apps and print a one-screen summary each. Usage (from work/): tools/build_all.sh App:prefix ...
for t in "$@"; do
  a=${t%%:*}; p=${t##*:}
  python3 -I tools/build_graph.py graphs/$a $p > /tmp/_b_$a.txt 2>&1
  echo "=== $a: $(grep -E '^methods' /tmp/_b_$a.txt)"
  grep -E '^verdicts|contradictions|^errors|^warnings' /tmp/_b_$a.txt | cut -c1-200
  grep -E "^Traceback|Error" /tmp/_b_$a.txt | head -3
done

FILE	tools/build_graph.py	38050	240e59a15d7835aad09bbe491ea2f723f370bca9856b402fccd0113d05f44cb6	utf8
"""Build, validate and export a trigger graph from an app's graph_def.py (output schema 2, see handoff/SCHEMA_V2.md).

Usage: python3 -I build_graph.py <graph_dir> <file_prefix>

<graph_dir> must contain:
  graph_def.py          edge definitions (see below)
  method_list.json      output of list_methods.py --json (the method universe)
  verification.csv      optional audit log: type,item,result,confirmed_by,ref,edges,note (see SCHEMA_V2.md)
  coverage_baseline.json  optional; used only for the one-way sanity check (a method a tool ran cannot be UNREACHABLE)

graph_def.py is executed with these names predefined:
  e(src, dst, kind, trigger, gui=(), guard=None, reads=(), writes=(), note=None, needs_state=None, evidence=None,
    env=None, cls=None, via=None, env_kinds=(), state_kinds=())      -> appends one edge
  SCREENS = {}  TITLE = ''  NOTES = {}
  UNREACHABLE = {method: (kind, reason[, source_ref[, confirmed_by]])}   kind in UNREACHABLE_KINDS
  DEAD = {}  FRAMEWORK_ONLY = {}     legacy: {method: reason}; read as UNREACHABLE with kind 'unclassified'
  ENV_KINDS_OF = {env text: [kinds]}  STATE_KINDS_OF = {needs_state text: [kinds]}   kinds of the gates, by exact text
  CLASSIFY = None       optional callable(edge) -> edge class or (edge class, via); applied to edges without cls
  EDIT = None           optional callable(edge): may change any field of every edge (e.g. add an ENV gate) before ids are made
  STRICT = False        True: anything still unclassified is an error (set it once an app is converted)
  HTML = True           write the viewer page if the template exists
  PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = False   # True: a method that needs in-app state but no environment gate is preferred
                                  # over a route that needs the environment
`env` marks an edge that needs the device or the outside world; `needs_state` an edge that needs earlier in-app actions.
NOTES maps a base method name to a free-text note for methods.csv.
USE_AUTO = True adds the call edges from <graph_dir>/auto_calls.json (tools/extract_calls.py) for every
caller/callee pair that no hand-written edge already covers; SUPPRESS = {(src, dst), ...} or {src, ...}
drops auto edges (e.g. calls inside branches that can never run). Lambda creations become REGISTER edges.

Writes into <graph_dir>/out/:
  <prefix>_trigger_graph.json, methods.csv, <prefix>_trigger_graph.html (if the template exists),
  sanity_check.csv (tool coverage vs labels, never used to set a label), build_report.txt.
methods.csv has route_edge_ids: the edge ids of the derived best route (what a verification.csv path row must repeat).
"""
import collections
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import sys

KINDS = {'ENTRY', 'LIFECYCLE', 'GUI', 'REGISTER', 'CALL', 'INTENT', 'RESULT', 'ASYNC',
         'FRAMEWORK', 'SYSTEM', 'ENTERS'}
EVIDENCE = ('SRC', 'FW-SRC', 'DYN', 'FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED', 'UNVERIFIED')
JVM = {'I': 'int', 'J': 'long', 'Z': 'boolean', 'F': 'float', 'D': 'double', 'B': 'byte',
       'C': 'char', 'S': 'short', 'V': 'void'}
TEMPLATE = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'viewer_template.html')

# schema 2 vocabularies
EDGE_CLASSES = ('explicit', 'standard', 'non_standard', 'page')
DEFAULT_CLASS = {'CALL': 'explicit', 'ENTRY': 'standard', 'LIFECYCLE': 'standard', 'GUI': 'standard', 'REGISTER': 'standard',
                 'INTENT': 'standard', 'RESULT': 'standard', 'ASYNC': 'standard', 'FRAMEWORK': 'standard', 'SYSTEM': 'standard'}
ENV_KINDS = ('network', 'tls_chain', 'permission', 'hardware', 'other_app', 'system_event', 'time', 'other')
STATE_KINDS = ('data', 'settings', 'account', 'file', 'session')
UNREACHABLE_KINDS = ('no_trigger_in_app', 'branch_never_taken', 'framework_never_calls', 'closed_by_configuration', 'closed_by_api_level')
CONFIRMED = ('DEVICE', 'SOURCE', 'INFERRED')
RESULTS = ('correct', 'fixed', 'wrong', 'unclear')
VERIFY_TYPES = ('label', 'edge', 'path')
# words that suggest a non-standard implicit edge (library or reflection makes the link); only used to print a review list
NONSTD_HINT = re.compile(r'EventBus|ButterKnife|@OnClick|@BindView|Dagger|@Inject|RxJava|android:onClick|reflect|getIdentifier|'
                         r'Class\.forName|data ?binding|ViewBinding|implicit (?:intent|broadcast)|by action|setAction\(|'
                         r'Intent\((?:\w+\.)?ACTION_', re.I)


def base(n):
    return n.split('@')[0]


def is_screen(n):
    return n.startswith('S_')


def param_names(desc):
    """'(Landroid/content/Context;I)Z' -> 'Context,int'"""
    args, i, out = desc[1:desc.index(')')], 0, []
    while i < len(args):
        dims = 0
        while args[i] == '[':
            dims += 1
            i += 1
        if args[i] == 'L':
            j = args.index(';', i)
            name = args[i + 1:j].rsplit('/', 1)[-1].split('$')[-1]
            i = j + 1
        else:
            name = JVM[args[i]]
            i += 1
        out.append(name + '[]' * dims)
    return ','.join(out)


def class_prefixes(rows):
    """{outer class: 'pkgtail.'} for outer classes whose simple name is shared by another class (e.g. two TagsAdapter)."""
    simple = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for r in rows:
        outer = r['class'].split('$')[0]
        simple[outer.rsplit('/', 1)[-1]].add(outer)
    pre = {}
    for sn, fulls in simple.items():
        if len(fulls) < 2:
            continue
        for k in range(1, 8):
            tails = {f: '.'.join(f.split('/')[-1 - k:-1]) for f in fulls}
            if len(set(tails.values())) == len(fulls):
                break
        for f in fulls:
            pre[f] = tails[f] + '.'
    return pre


def method_names(rows):
    """Base node name per method; overloads get '(params)'; classes that share a simple name get a package prefix."""
    pre = class_prefixes(rows)
    shorts = [pre.get(r['class'].split('$')[0], '') + r['short'] for r in rows]
    count = collections.Counter(shorts)
    names, by_name = [], {}
    for r, sh in zip(rows, shorts):
        n = sh if count[sh] == 1 else '%s(%s)' % (sh, param_names(r['desc']))
        k = 2
        while n in by_name:   # same class, overloads whose parameter simple names coincide
            n = '%s#%d' % (n.split('#')[0], k)
            k += 1
        names.append(n)
        by_name[n] = r
    return names, by_name


def bfs(out, allow_setup, allow_env=True, classes=None):
    """Breadth-first reachability from S_ENTRY; classes: only follow edges of these edge classes."""
    prev, q = {'S_ENTRY': None}, ['S_ENTRY']
    for n in q:
        for x in out.get(n, []):
            if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER' or x['src'] == x['dst'] or x['dst'] in prev:
                continue
            if x['needs_state'] and not allow_setup:
                continue
            if x.get('env') and not allow_env:
                continue
            if classes is not None and x['edge_class'] not in classes:
                continue
            prev[x['dst']] = x
            q.append(x['dst'])
    return prev


def cheapest(out, allow_setup):
    """Like bfs, but each reachable node keeps the path with the fewest environment-gated edges, then the fewest
    earlier-setup (needs_state) edges, then the fewest steps.
    The returned dict is in settlement order (cheapest first), so the first variant found for a method is its best one."""
    import heapq
    best = {'S_ENTRY': (0, 0, 0)}
    via = {'S_ENTRY': None}
    done, prev = set(), {}
    heap, tick = [(0, 0, 0, 0, 'S_ENTRY')], 0
    while heap:
        env, setup, steps, _t, n = heapq.heappop(heap)
        if n in done:
            continue
        done.add(n)
        prev[n] = via[n]
        for x in out.get(n, []):
            if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER' or x['src'] == x['dst'] or x['dst'] in done:
                continue
            if x['needs_state'] and not allow_setup:
                continue
            cost = (env + (1 if x.get('env') else 0), setup + (1 if x['needs_state'] else 0), steps + 1)
            if x['dst'] not in best or cost < best[x['dst']]:
                best[x['dst']] = cost
                via[x['dst']] = x
                tick += 1
                heapq.heappush(heap, (cost[0], cost[1], cost[2], tick, x['dst']))
    return prev


def path_to(n, prev):
    p = []
    while prev.get(n):
        p.append(prev[n])
        n = prev[n]['src']
    return p[::-1]


def menu_label(trigger):
    m = re.search(r'(android\.R\.id\.home)|R\.id\.(\w+)', trigger or '')
    if not m:
        return None
    return 'Up arrow (top-left)' if m.group(1) else re.sub(r'^(action|menu|item)_', '', m.group(2)).replace('_', ' ')


def gui_steps(path):
    steps = []
    for i, x in enumerate(path):
        if not x['gui']:
            continue
        if i + 1 < len(path) and re.search(r'case (android\.)?R\.id\.', path[i + 1]['trigger'] or ''):
            lab = menu_label(path[i + 1]['trigger'])
            if lab:
                steps.append('Tap menu item: ' + lab)
                continue
        steps.append(x['gui'][0])
    return steps


def add_auto_edges(gdir, edges, e, suppress):
    auto = json.load(open(os.path.join(gdir, 'auto_calls.json')))
    manual = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
    manual_reg = {(base(x['src']), base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER'}
    receiver = set(auto.get('receiver_variants', ()))
    variants = collections.defaultdict(set)  # hand-written context variants (not receiver variants)
    for x in edges:
        for n in (x['src'], x['dst']):
            if '@' in n and not is_screen(n) and n not in receiver:
                variants[base(n)].add(n)
    manual_nodes = {(x['src'], base(x['dst'])) for x in edges if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'}
    for c in auto['calls']:
        p = (c['src'], c['dst'])
        if p in suppress or c['src'] in suppress or c['dst'] in suppress:
            continue
        if c['src'] in receiver:  # receiver variants: skip only if that exact node already has a hand edge
            if (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual_nodes:
                continue
        elif (c['src'], base(c['dst'])) in manual or (base(c['src']), base(c['dst'])) in manual:
            continue
        trig = 'Direct call' if c['dispatch'] == 'exact' else 'Virtual/interface call that can dispatch to this implementation'
        if c['via']:
            trig += ' (through ' + ', '.join(c['via']) + ')'
        for s in [c['src']] + sorted(variants.get(c['src'], ())):
            e(s, c['dst'], 'CALL', trig, evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (c['file'], c['line']), auto=True)
    for l in auto['lambdas']:
        p = (l['src'], l['dst'])
        if p in manual or p in manual_reg or p in suppress or l['src'] in suppress:
            continue
        e(l['src'], l['dst'], 'REGISTER', 'Creates the lambda / method reference (its body runs when invoked)',
          evidence='SRC %s:%s (bytecode)' % (l['file'], l['line']), auto=True)


def assign_ids(edges):
    """Stable edge ids: a hash of the edge's content, so adding or removing other edges never renumbers an edge
    (stored paths and the audit log refer to these ids); an edge whose own text changes gets a new id."""
    seen = collections.Counter()
    for x in edges:
        key = '|'.join(str(x.get(k) or '') for k in ('src', 'dst', 'kind', 'trigger', 'guard', 'evidence', 'env', 'needs_state'))
        h = 'E' + hashlib.sha1(key.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()[:8]
        seen[h] += 1
        x['id'] = h if seen[h] == 1 else '%s-%d' % (h, seen[h])


def evidence_labels(ev):
    """'SRC A.java:1; B.java:2; FW-MEMORY x' -> ['SRC', 'FW-MEMORY'] (a segment without a label continues the previous one)"""
    return [seg.split()[0] for seg in (ev or '').split(';') if seg.strip() and seg.split()[0] in EVIDENCE]


def edge_sourced(x):
    """True when every claim of the edge's evidence cites a source line (app source or AOSP source)."""
    labs = evidence_labels(x['evidence'])
    return bool(labs) and all(l in ('SRC', 'FW-SRC') for l in labs)


def evidence_ref(ev):
    """'SRC Main.java:646-695; FW-MEMORY ...' -> 'Main.java:646-695' (first source reference), else None."""
    m = re.search(r'(?:^|;\s*)(?:FW-)?SRC\s+([^\s;]+)', ev or '')
    return m.group(1) if m else None


def gate_items(path, key, kinds_key):
    """Distinct gates (text + kinds) on a path, in path order."""
    seen, res = set(), []
    for x in path:
        t = x.get(key)
        if t and t not in seen:
            seen.add(t)
            res.append({'kinds': x[kinds_key], 'text': t})
    return res


def norm_unreachable(m, v, legacy=None):
    """UNREACHABLE entry -> {'kind', 'text', 'ref', 'confirmed_by', 'legacy'}"""
    if isinstance(v, str):
        v = ('unclassified', v)
    kind, text = v[0], v[1]
    ref = v[2] if len(v) > 2 else None
    cb = v[3] if len(v) > 3 else ('SOURCE' if ref else 'INFERRED')
    return {'kind': kind, 'text': text, 'ref': ref, 'confirmed_by': cb, 'legacy': legacy}


def load_verification(gdir):
    path = os.path.join(gdir, 'verification.csv')
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return []
    with open(path, encoding='utf-8', newline='') as f:
        return [r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if (r.get('type') or '').strip() and not r['type'].strip().startswith('#')]


def main(argv):
    gdir, prefix = argv[1], argv[2]
    outdir = os.path.join(gdir, 'out')
    os.makedirs(outdir, exist_ok=True)
    rows = json.load(open(os.path.join(gdir, 'method_list.json')))
    methods, by_name = method_names(rows)

    edges = []

    def e(src, dst, kind, trigger, gui=(), guard=None, reads=(), writes=(), note=None,
          needs_state=None, evidence=None, env=None, auto=False, cls=None, via=None, env_kinds=(), state_kinds=()):
        x = {'src': src, 'dst': dst, 'kind': kind, 'trigger': trigger, 'gui': list(gui),
             'guard': guard, 'reads': list(reads), 'writes': list(writes), 'note': note,
             'needs_state': needs_state, 'evidence': evidence, 'env': env,
             'edge_class': cls, 'via': via, 'env_kinds': list(env_kinds), 'state_kinds': list(state_kinds)}
        if auto:
            x['auto'] = True
        edges.append(x)

    auto_path = os.path.join(gdir, 'auto_calls.json')
    auto_data = json.load(open(auto_path)) if os.path.isfile(auto_path) else None

    def impl(cls, name, desc=None):
        """Node that runs when method `name` is invoked on an instance of class `cls` (short name)."""
        tbl = (auto_data or {}).get('impl', {}).get(cls, {}).get(name)
        if not tbl:
            raise KeyError('impl: %s has no method %s' % (cls, name))
        if desc:
            return tbl[desc]
        if len(set(tbl.values())) != 1:
            raise KeyError('impl: %s.%s is overloaded, pass desc: %s' % (cls, name, sorted(tbl)))
        return next(iter(tbl.values()))

    ns = {'e': e, 'SCREENS': {}, 'DEAD': {}, 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY': {}, 'UNREACHABLE': {}, 'TITLE': '', 'NOTES': {},
          'USE_AUTO': False, 'SUPPRESS': set(), 'AUTO': auto_data, 'impl': impl, 'METHODS': list(methods),
          'GDIR': os.path.abspath(gdir), 'TOOLS': os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
          'AUTO_DEAD': None, 'COVERAGE': None, 'CLASSIFY': None, 'EDIT': None, 'ENV_KINDS_OF': {}, 'STATE_KINDS_OF': {},
          'STRICT': False, 'HTML': True, 'FRAMEWORK_NODES': {}}
    src_path = os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def.py')
    exec(compile(open(src_path, encoding='utf-8').read(), src_path, 'exec'), ns)
    screens, dead, fwo, notes = ns['SCREENS'], ns['DEAD'], ns['FRAMEWORK_ONLY'], ns['NOTES']
    strict = bool(ns['STRICT'])
    for name, rec in ns['FRAMEWORK_NODES'].items():
        if name in by_name:
            raise KeyError('FRAMEWORK_NODES: %s is already in the method list' % name)
        methods.append(name)
        by_name[name] = rec
    if ns['USE_AUTO']:
        add_auto_edges(gdir, edges, e, set(ns['SUPPRESS']))
    if ns['EDIT']:
        for x in edges:
            ns['EDIT'](x)
    assign_ids(edges)

    errors, warnings = [], []
    todo = []     # things still to classify (warnings, or errors in strict mode)
    known = set(methods) | set(screens)
    for x in edges:
        for end in ('src', 'dst'):
            if base(x[end]) not in known:
                errors.append('%s: unknown %s node %s' % (x['id'], end, x[end]))
        if x['kind'] not in KINDS:
            errors.append('%s: bad kind %s' % (x['id'], x['kind']))
        if x['kind'] == 'GUI' and (not is_screen(x['src']) or is_screen(x['dst'])):
            errors.append('%s: GUI edge must go page -> method' % x['id'])
        if x['kind'] == 'ENTERS' and not is_screen(x['dst']):
            errors.append('%s: ENTERS edge must end on a page' % x['id'])
        if not x['evidence'] or x['evidence'].split()[0] not in EVIDENCE:
            errors.append('%s: missing/invalid evidence label' % x['id'])

    # reachability labels that are decided by hand: UNREACHABLE (new) plus the legacy DEAD / FRAMEWORK_ONLY lists
    unreach = {m: norm_unreachable(m, v) for m, v in ns['UNREACHABLE'].items()}
    for m, t in dead.items():
        unreach.setdefault(m, norm_unreachable(m, t, 'DEAD'))
    for m, t in fwo.items():
        unreach.setdefault(m, norm_unreachable(m, t, 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY'))
    for m, u in unreach.items():
        if m not in methods:
            errors.append('UNREACHABLE entry for unknown method ' + m)
        if u['kind'] not in UNREACHABLE_KINDS:
            if u['kind'] == 'unclassified':
                todo.append('%s: unreachable reason has no kind (legacy DEAD / FRAMEWORK_ONLY entry): classify it' % m)
            else:
                errors.append('%s: bad unreachable kind %s' % (m, u['kind']))
        if u['confirmed_by'] not in CONFIRMED:
            errors.append('%s: bad confirmed_by %s' % (m, u['confirmed_by']))

    if ns.get('AUTO_DEAD'):   # drop pages (and their edges) that nothing reaches
        o0 = collections.defaultdict(list)
        for x in edges:
            o0[x['src']].append(x)
        reach = bfs(o0, allow_setup=True)
        gone = {s_ for s_ in screens if s_ != 'S_ENTRY' and not any(base(n) == s_ for n in reach)}
        if gone:
            edges[:] = [x for x in edges if base(x['src']) not in gone and base(x['dst']) not in gone]
            for s_ in gone:
                del screens[s_]
            assign_ids(edges)

    # edge classes (explicit / standard / non_standard / page) and the kinds of the ENV / STATE gates
    classify = ns.get('CLASSIFY')
    env_of, state_of = ns['ENV_KINDS_OF'], ns['STATE_KINDS_OF']
    hint_review = []
    for x in edges:
        x['class_basis'] = 'authored'
        if not x['edge_class'] and classify:
            r = classify(x)
            if r:
                x['edge_class'], x['via'] = r if isinstance(r, tuple) else (r, x['via'])
        if not x['edge_class']:
            x['edge_class'] = 'page' if is_screen(x['dst']) else DEFAULT_CLASS.get(x['kind'], 'standard')
            x['class_basis'] = 'default'
            if x['class_basis'] == 'default' and NONSTD_HINT.search(' '.join(str(x.get(k) or '') for k in ('trigger', 'note', 'guard', 'evidence'))):
                hint_review.append(x['id'])
        if x['edge_class'] not in EDGE_CLASSES:
            errors.append('%s: bad edge class %s' % (x['id'], x['edge_class']))
        if x['edge_class'] == 'non_standard' and not x['via']:
            todo.append('%s: non_standard edge without via (name the library or mechanism)' % x['id'])
        for key, kinds_key, table, vocab, what in (('env', 'env_kinds', env_of, ENV_KINDS, 'ENV'),
                                                   ('needs_state', 'state_kinds', state_of, STATE_KINDS, 'STATE')):
            if not x[key]:
                if x[kinds_key]:
                    errors.append('%s: %s kinds without a %s gate text' % (x['id'], what, key))
                continue
            kinds = list(x[kinds_key]) or list(table.get(x[key], ()))
            if not kinds:
                x[kinds_key] = ['unclassified']
                todo.append('%s: %s gate has no kinds: %s' % (x['id'], what, x[key][:80]))
                continue
            bad = [k for k in kinds if k not in vocab]
            if bad:
                errors.append('%s: bad %s kind %s' % (x['id'], what, ', '.join(bad)))
            x[kinds_key] = kinds
    if strict and ns.get('AUTO_DEAD'):
        errors.append('STRICT: AUTO_DEAD is not allowed (every UNREACHABLE needs an authored reason)')

    out, inn = collections.defaultdict(list), collections.defaultdict(list)
    for x in edges:
        out[x['src']].append(x)
        inn[base(x['dst'])].append(x)

    prev = bfs(out, allow_setup=False)
    prev_setup = bfs(out, allow_setup=True)
    prev_noenv = bfs(out, allow_setup=True, allow_env=False)
    prev_expl = bfs(out, allow_setup=True, classes={'explicit', 'standard', 'page'})
    prefer = bool(ns.get('PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV'))
    prev_clean = bfs(out, allow_setup=False, allow_env=False) if prefer else None
    best_first = cheapest(out, allow_setup=False)     # path choice: fewest environment gates, then fewest steps
    best_setup = cheapest(out, allow_setup=True)

    def first_variant(m, pv):
        for n in pv:  # dict keeps BFS discovery order, so the first hit is the shortest
            if base(n) == m:
                return n
        return None

    verdict, info = {}, {}
    auto_dead = set()
    for m in methods:
        mode = None     # 'clean': a route without any needs_state edge; 'setup': a route that needs earlier in-app actions
        if m in unreach:
            verdict[m] = 'UNREACHABLE'
        elif prefer and first_variant(m, prev_clean):
            verdict[m], mode = 'REACHABLE', 'clean'
        elif prefer and first_variant(m, prev_noenv):
            verdict[m], mode = 'REACHABLE', 'setup'   # in-app setup only: better than a route that needs the environment
        elif first_variant(m, prev):
            verdict[m], mode = 'REACHABLE', 'clean'
        elif first_variant(m, prev_setup):
            verdict[m], mode = 'REACHABLE', 'setup'
        elif ns.get('AUTO_DEAD'):
            verdict[m] = 'UNREACHABLE'
            auto_dead.add(m)
            unreach[m] = norm_unreachable(m, ns['AUTO_DEAD'] if isinstance(ns['AUTO_DEAD'], str) else 'No route found from the entry points', 'AUTO_DEAD')
            todo.append('%s: no route found, labelled UNREACHABLE by AUTO_DEAD: read the source and give a reason' % m)
        else:
            verdict[m] = 'UNRESOLVED'
            errors.append('method has no path and no UNREACHABLE entry: ' + m)
        if verdict[m] == 'UNREACHABLE' and m not in auto_dead:
            live = [x['id'] for x in inn.get(m, []) if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER']
            if live:
                warnings.append('%s is UNREACHABLE but has incoming edges %s' % (m, live))
        # best route. Converted graphs (STRICT): of the cheapest route without any needs_state edge and the cheapest route over all edges, take
        # the one with the fewest ENV kinds, then the fewest STATE kinds, then the fewest steps. Schema-1 graphs: a route without any
        # needs_state edge first.
        v, pv, p = None, None, []
        if mode and strict:
            cands = []
            for cand in (best_first, best_setup):
                v = first_variant(m, cand)
                if v:
                    cands.append(path_to(v, cand))
            p = min(cands, key=lambda q: (len({k for x in q for k in x['env_kinds']}), len({k for x in q for k in x['state_kinds']}), len(q)))
        elif mode:
            for cand in ([best_first] if mode == 'clean' else [best_setup]):
                v = first_variant(m, cand)
                if v:
                    pv = cand
                    break
            p = path_to(v, pv) if v else []
        info[m] = {'path': p, 'env_gated': any(x.get('env') for x in p)}

    pages = set(screens)
    for s in pages:
        if s not in ('S_ENTRY',) and not any(base(n) == s for n in prev_setup):
            errors.append('page not reachable from S_ENTRY: ' + s)

    # per-method labels
    labels = {}
    for m in methods:
        p = info[m]['path']
        if verdict[m] == 'UNREACHABLE':
            u = unreach[m]
            lab = {'unreachable': {'kind': u['kind'], 'text': u['text']}, 'confirmed_by': u['confirmed_by'], 'source_ref': u['ref'],
                   'path_status': None}
        else:
            lab = {'env': gate_items(p, 'env', 'env_kinds'), 'state': gate_items(p, 'needs_state', 'state_kinds'),
                   'route_edge_classes': sorted({x['edge_class'] for x in p} - {'page'}),
                   'explicit_standard_route': bool(first_variant(m, prev_expl)),
                   'confirmed_by': 'SOURCE' if p and all(edge_sourced(x) for x in p) else 'INFERRED',
                   'source_ref': evidence_ref(p[-1]['evidence']) if p else None,
                   'path_status': 'UNCHECKED'}
        labels[m] = lab

    # audit log (verification.csv): confirmations, path status, stored paths, error rates
    vrows = load_verification(gdir)
    edge_ids = {x['id'] for x in edges}
    counts = {t: collections.Counter() for t in VERIFY_TYPES}
    stored_paths = []
    for r in vrows:
        t, item, res = r['type'].strip(), (r.get('item') or '').strip(), (r.get('result') or '').strip()
        cb = (r.get('confirmed_by') or 'INFERRED').strip()
        if t not in VERIFY_TYPES or res not in RESULTS or cb not in CONFIRMED:
            errors.append('verification.csv: bad row %s' % dict(r))
            continue
        if t == 'edge':
            if item not in edge_ids:
                warnings.append('verification.csv: edge %s no longer exists (the edge text changed): re-verify it' % item)
                continue
        elif item not in labels:
            errors.append('verification.csv: unknown method %s' % item)
            continue
        counts[t][res] += 1
        if t == 'label' and res in ('correct', 'fixed'):
            labels[item]['confirmed_by'] = cb
            if r.get('ref'):
                labels[item]['source_ref'] = r['ref'].strip()
        elif t == 'path' and verdict[item] == 'REACHABLE':
            ids = (r.get('edges') or '').split()
            current = [x['id'] for x in info[item]['path']]
            if res == 'wrong':
                labels[item]['path_status'] = 'WRONG'
            elif res == 'unclear':
                labels[item]['path_status'] = 'UNCHECKED'
            elif ids != current:
                labels[item]['path_status'] = 'STALE'
                warnings.append('verification.csv: the verified path of %s is no longer the best route: re-verify' % item)
            else:
                labels[item]['path_status'] = {'DEVICE': 'DEVICE_OK', 'SOURCE': 'SOURCE_OK'}.get(cb, 'UNCHECKED')
                if cb in ('DEVICE', 'SOURCE'):
                    p = info[item]['path']
                    cls = sorted({x['edge_class'] for x in p} - {'page'})
                    stored_paths.append({'method': item, 'edges': current, 'edge_classes': cls,
                                         'explicit_standard_only': 'non_standard' not in cls,
                                         'env': labels[item]['env'], 'state': labels[item]['state'],
                                         'verified_by': cb, 'ref': (r.get('ref') or '').strip() or None})
    verification = None
    if vrows:
        verification = {}
        for t in VERIFY_TYPES:
            c = counts[t]
            checked = c['correct'] + c['fixed'] + c['wrong']
            verification[t] = {'checked': checked, 'correct': c['correct'], 'errors_found': c['fixed'] + c['wrong'], 'unclear': c['unclear'],
                               'error_rate': round((c['fixed'] + c['wrong']) / checked, 3) if checked else None}
        verification['reachable_methods'] = sum(1 for m in methods if verdict[m] == 'REACHABLE')

    if todo:
        (errors if strict else warnings).extend(['TO CLASSIFY ' + s for s in todo[:200]] + (['TO CLASSIFY ... %d more' % (len(todo) - 200)] if len(todo) > 200 else []))
    if hint_review:
        warnings.append('REVIEW %d edges on the default class mention a library/reflection hint (EventBus, ButterKnife, android:onClick, ...): '
                        'check they are not non_standard: %s' % (len(hint_review), ' '.join(hint_review[:40]) + (' ...' if len(hint_review) > 40 else '')))

    graph = {'schema': 2, 'title': ns['TITLE'], 'screens': screens, 'methods': methods, 'verdict': verdict, 'labels': labels,
             'edges': edges, 'paths': stored_paths, 'verification': verification}
    jpath = os.path.join(outdir, prefix + '_trigger_graph.json')
    with open(jpath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(graph, f, indent=1, ensure_ascii=False)

    cov = {}
    cpath = os.path.join(gdir, 'coverage_baseline.json')
    if os.path.isfile(cpath):
        for r in json.load(open(cpath)):
            cov[(r.get('class'), r['short'], r['desc'])] = r

    def tool_hit(m):
        c = cov.get((by_name[m]['class'], by_name[m]['short'], by_name[m]['desc']), {})
        return bool(c.get('monkey') or c.get('ape'))

    # optional: frontier of the baseline coverage (where the nearest tool-run method leads into code no tool ran).
    # Not a label and off by default: it uses tool coverage, so it never goes into methods.csv.
    frontier = {}
    if cov and ns.get('FRONTIER', False):
        hit_set = {m for m in methods if tool_hit(m)}
        nodes_all = {x['src'] for x in edges} | {x['dst'] for x in edges}
        prevf = {}
        dq = collections.deque()
        for n in sorted(nodes_all):
            if not is_screen(n) and base(n) in hit_set:
                prevf[n] = None
                dq.append(n)
        while dq:
            n = dq.popleft()
            for x in out.get(n, []):
                if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER' or x['dst'] in prevf:
                    continue
                prevf[x['dst']] = x
                dq.append(x['dst'])
        first = {}
        for n in prevf:
            first.setdefault(base(n), n)
        for m in methods:
            if m in hit_set or verdict[m] != 'REACHABLE' or m not in first:
                continue
            chain, n = [], first[m]
            while prevf.get(n):
                chain.append(prevf[n])
                n = prevf[n]['src']
            chain.reverse()      # edges from the hit method (n) to the target
            k = None
            for i, x in enumerate(chain):
                if not is_screen(x['dst']) and base(x['dst']) not in hit_set:
                    k = i
                    break
            if k is None:
                continue
            fx = chain[k]
            ev = [t.strip() for t in (fx['evidence'] or '').split(';')]
            frontier[m] = (base(fx['src']), fx['dst'], fx['kind'], next((t for t in ev if t.startswith('SRC')), ev[0]), len(chain) - k)
    if frontier:
        with open(os.path.join(outdir, 'frontier.csv'), 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(['method', 'frontier_from', 'frontier_first_unrun', 'frontier_edge_kind', 'frontier_edge_evidence', 'steps_after_frontier'])
            for m in methods:
                if m in frontier:
                    w.writerow([m] + list(frontier[m]))

    def kinds_of(items):
        return ';'.join(sorted({k for it in items for k in it['kinds']}))

    with open(os.path.join(outdir, 'methods.csv'), 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        w = csv.writer(f)
        w.writerow(['method', 'verdict', 'unreachable_kind', 'unreachable_reason', 'env_kinds', 'env', 'state_kinds', 'state',
                    'route_edge_classes', 'explicit_standard_route', 'confirmed_by', 'source_ref', 'path_status',
                    'steps_from_launch', 'gui_steps_on_shortest_path', 'shortest_path', 'route_edge_ids', 'incoming_edges', 'note', 'jvm_descriptor'])
        for m in methods:
            p, lab = info[m]['path'], labels[m]
            un = lab.get('unreachable') or {}
            r = by_name[m]
            w.writerow([m, verdict[m], un.get('kind', ''), un.get('text', ''),
                        kinds_of(lab.get('env', [])), ' | '.join(it['text'] for it in lab.get('env', [])),
                        kinds_of(lab.get('state', [])), ' | '.join(it['text'] for it in lab.get('state', [])),
                        ';'.join(lab.get('route_edge_classes', [])),
                        '' if 'explicit_standard_route' not in lab else ('yes' if lab['explicit_standard_route'] else 'no'),
                        lab['confirmed_by'], lab['source_ref'] or '', lab['path_status'] or '',
                        len(p) if p else '', ' ; '.join(gui_steps(p)),
                        ' > '.join([p[0]['src']] + [x['dst'] for x in p]) if p else '', ' '.join(x['id'] for x in p),
                        sum(1 for x in inn.get(m, []) if x['kind'] != 'REGISTER'),
                        notes.get(m) or '', r['desc']])

    if cov:    # one-way sanity check: a method a tool ran cannot be UNREACHABLE; never used to set a label
        with open(os.path.join(outdir, 'sanity_check.csv'), 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            w.writerow(['method', 'verdict', 'monkey_hit', 'ape_hit', 'flag'])
            for m in methods:
                c = cov.get((by_name[m]['class'], by_name[m]['short'], by_name[m]['desc']), {})
                flag = ''
                if tool_hit(m) and verdict[m] == 'UNREACHABLE':
                    flag = 'CONTRADICTION'
                elif tool_hit(m) and info[m]['env_gated']:
                    flag = 'ENV_GATED_BUT_HIT'
                w.writerow([m, verdict[m], int(bool(c.get('monkey'))) if c else '', int(bool(c.get('ape'))) if c else '', flag])

    if ns.get('HTML', True) and os.path.isfile(TEMPLATE):
        data = json.dumps(graph, ensure_ascii=False).replace('</', '<\\/')
        html = open(TEMPLATE, encoding='utf-8').read().replace('/*DATA*/', data)
        with open(os.path.join(outdir, prefix + '_trigger_graph.html'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(html)

    # report
    rep = []
    vc = collections.Counter(verdict.values())
    rep.append('methods %d, pages %d, edges %d' % (len(methods), len(screens), len(edges)))
    rep.append('verdicts: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(vc.items())))
    rep.append('unreachable by kind: ' + (', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(u['kind'] for m, u in unreach.items() if m in methods).items())) or 'none'))
    rep.append('edge kinds: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(x['kind'] for x in edges).items())))
    rep.append('edge classes: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(x['edge_class'] for x in edges).items()))
               + '; class set by hand: %d of %d' % (sum(1 for x in edges if x['class_basis'] == 'authored'), len(edges)))
    rep.append('auto edges (from bytecode): %d of %d' % (sum(1 for x in edges if x.get('auto')), len(edges)))
    rep.append('evidence: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(x['evidence'].split()[0] for x in edges).items())))
    rep.append('edges with a claim that cites no source line (FW-MEMORY, INFERRED, DYN, UNVERIFIED): %d of %d' % (sum(1 for x in edges if not edge_sourced(x)), len(edges)))
    rep.append('needs_state edges: %d; env edges: %d; methods with ENV: %d, with STATE: %d' % (
        sum(1 for x in edges if x['needs_state']), sum(1 for x in edges if x.get('env')),
        sum(1 for m in methods if labels[m].get('env')), sum(1 for m in methods if labels[m].get('state'))))
    reach = [m for m in methods if verdict[m] == 'REACHABLE']
    rep.append('reachable methods whose best route has a non-standard edge: %d; with no explicit/standard-only route at all: %d' % (
        sum(1 for m in reach if 'non_standard' in labels[m]['route_edge_classes']),
        sum(1 for m in reach if not labels[m]['explicit_standard_route'])))
    rep.append('labels confirmed by: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(l['confirmed_by'] for l in labels.values()).items())))
    rep.append('path status: ' + ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(collections.Counter(l['path_status'] for l in labels.values() if l['path_status']).items())))
    if verification:
        for t in VERIFY_TYPES:
            v = verification[t]
            rep.append('verification %s: checked %d, errors found %d (rate %s), unclear %d' % (t, v['checked'], v['errors_found'], v['error_rate'], v['unclear']))
    if cov:
        contra = [m for m in methods if verdict[m] == 'UNREACHABLE' and tool_hit(m)]
        gated_hit = [m for m in methods if verdict[m] == 'REACHABLE' and tool_hit(m) and info[m]['env_gated']]
        unreported = [m for m in methods if (by_name[m]['class'], by_name[m]['short'], by_name[m]['desc']) not in cov]
        rep.append('sanity check (tool coverage; never used to set a label): %d methods ran in a tool run; %d UNREACHABLE but ran (contradictions); '
                   '%d ENV-gated on the best route but ran' % (sum(1 for m in methods if tool_hit(m)), len(contra), len(gated_hit)))
        for m in contra:
            rep.append('  CONTRADICTION: %s is UNREACHABLE but ran in a tool run: read the source, do not just flip the label' % m)
        for m in gated_hit:
            rep.append('  ENV-GATED BUT RAN: %s' % m)
        rep.append('not in the JaCoCo report (filtered trivial methods): %s' % (', '.join(unreported) or 'none'))
    rep.append('errors: %d' % len(errors))
    rep += ['  ERROR ' + s for s in errors]
    rep.append('warnings: %d' % len(warnings))
    rep += ['  WARN ' + s for s in warnings]
    text = '\n'.join(rep)
    with open(os.path.join(outdir, 'build_report.txt'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(text + '\n')
    print(text)
    return 1 if errors else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

"""Run the generator on the five hand-written apps and compare with the hand-written verdicts (tools/tests/_t_<App>/).
Writes tools/tests/calibration.json and prints a table. Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/calibrate.py [--rebuild-inputs]
The test folders hold copies of method_list.json / coverage_baseline.json of graphs/<App> and a stub graph_def.py that only
calls gen_edges.apply(); auto_calls.json is regenerated with the current tools/extract_calls.py when missing or --rebuild-inputs."""
import collections, glob, json, os, subprocess, sys

APPS = [('Currency', 'currency'), ('Diary', 'diary'), ('hash-checker', 'hash-checker'), ('openlauncher', 'openlauncher'), ('Omni-Notes', 'Omni-Notes')]
ROOT = '/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang'
STUB = '''TITLE = '%s generated (calibration run)'
USE_AUTO = True
PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = True
AUTO_DEAD = 'No route found by the generator'
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, TOOLS)
import gen_edges
gen_edges.apply(globals(), GDIR, '%s', {})
'''
res = {}
for app, pfx in APPS:
    d = 'tools/tests/_t_%s' % app
    os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
    for f in ('method_list.json', 'coverage_baseline.json'):
        subprocess.run(['cp', 'graphs/%s/%s' % (app, f), d])
    if '--rebuild-inputs' in sys.argv or not os.path.exists(d + '/auto_calls.json'):
        b = '%s/final_java_only/%s/app' % (ROOT, app)
        (k, v), = json.load(open(b + '/class_files.json')).items()
        subprocess.run(['python3', '-I', 'tools/extract_calls.py', d] + [os.path.join(b, p) for p in v['classfiles']], capture_output=True)
    open(d + '/graph_def.py', 'w').write(STUB % (app, app))
    out = subprocess.run(['python3', '-I', 'tools/build_graph.py', d, 't'], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    a = json.load(open('graphs/%s/out/%s_trigger_graph.json' % (app, pfx)))['verdict']
    b = json.load(open('%s/out/t_trigger_graph.json' % d))['verdict']
    common = [m for m in a if m in b]
    dead = lambda x: x in ('DEAD', 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY')
    n = len(common)
    res[app] = {'methods': n,
                'dead_vs_alive': round(100.0 * sum(1 for m in common if dead(a[m]) == dead(b[m])) / n, 1),
                'exact': round(100.0 * sum(1 for m in common if a[m] == b[m]) / n, 1),
                'contradictions': int(next((l.split(' DEAD')[0].split()[-1] for l in out.split('\n') if 'contradictions' in l), -1))}
json.dump(res, open('tools/tests/calibration.json', 'w'), indent=1)
print('%-14s %8s %14s %8s %14s' % ('app', 'methods', 'dead-vs-alive', 'exact', 'contradictions'))
for app, r in res.items():
    print('%-14s %8d %13.1f%% %7.1f%% %14d' % (app, r['methods'], r['dead_vs_alive'], r['exact'], r['contradictions']))

FILE	tools/cases.py	17344	5fa548f6e23e7aee3c5da041dc130a3f8cde7effcf553c176d95920bd27cffaf	utf8
"""Source helpers for graph_def.py of hand-written graphs: split a big switch / if-chain method into its branches, find who sends a Handler message.

    import sys; sys.path.insert(0, TOOLS); import cases
    CS = cases.Cases('Vanilla-Music')                       # indexes the app sources once (flavor aware, like show_route.py)
    CS.branches('LibraryActivity.java', 'onFancyItemSelected')
        -> [('CTX_MENU_EXPAND', 746, 752), ('CTX_MENU_ENQUEUE', 753, 755), ...]   labels of the first switch (case X: / default:) or of the
           first if / else-if chain of the method body, with the first and last source line of each branch (fall-through labels share a range)
    CS.method_lines('LibraryActivity.java', 'onFancyItemSelected')  -> (first_line, last_line) of the method (declaration to closing brace)
    CS.enclosing('LibraryActivity.java', 775)                -> 'onFancyItemSelected' (name of the method that contains the line, or None)
    CS.uses(r'\\bMSG_DELETE\\b')                              -> [(file, line, method)] every use in the app sources (comments / strings skipped)
    CS.calls_in(src_node, a, b, AUTO)                        -> the bytecode call edges of src_node whose line is in [a, b]

The parser blanks comments, strings and char literals, then matches braces; it understands `switch (x) { case A: case B: ... default: ... }`,
`case A: { ... }` and `if (c) {...} else if (c) {...} else {...}` (also braceless single statements). It does not understand a switch inside a
lambda body or a ternary chain; for those the caller gets an empty list and writes the branches by hand.
"""
import collections
import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import show_route  # noqa: E402


def blank(text):
    """Replace comments, string and char literals by spaces (newlines kept) so braces and keywords can be searched."""
    out, i, n = [], 0, len(text)
    while i < n:
        c = text[i]
        two = text[i:i + 2]
        if two == '//':
            j = text.find('\n', i)
            j = n if j < 0 else j
            out.append(' ' * (j - i)); i = j
        elif two == '/*':
            j = text.find('*/', i + 2)
            j = n if j < 0 else j + 2
            out.append(re.sub(r'[^\n]', ' ', text[i:j])); i = j
        elif c in '"\'':
            q, j = c, i + 1
            while j < n and text[j] != q:
                j += 2 if text[j] == '\\' else 1
            j = min(j + 1, n)
            out.append(re.sub(r'[^\n]', ' ', text[i:j])); i = j
        else:
            out.append(c); i += 1
    return ''.join(out)


class Cases:
    def __init__(self, app):
        self.app = app
        self.idx = show_route.source_index(app)
        self.fl = show_route.built_flavors(app)
        self._src = {}

    def _load(self, fname):
        p = show_route.find(self.idx, fname, self.fl)
        if not p:
            raise KeyError(fname)
        if p not in self._src:
            raw = open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read()
            self._src[p] = (raw.split('\n'), blank(raw))
        return self._src[p]

    # ---- positions
    @staticmethod
    def _offsets(text):
        starts, pos = [0], 0
        for line in text.split('\n'):
            pos += len(line) + 1
            starts.append(pos)
        return starts

    @staticmethod
    def _line_of(starts, off):
        lo, hi = 0, len(starts) - 1
        while lo < hi:
            mid = (lo + hi + 1) // 2
            if starts[mid] <= off:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid - 1
        return lo + 1

    @staticmethod
    def _match(text, i, op='{', cl='}'):
        depth = 0
        for j in range(i, len(text)):
            if text[j] == op:
                depth += 1
            elif text[j] == cl:
                depth -= 1
                if depth == 0:
                    return j
        return -1

    def method_lines(self, fname, name, nth=0, params=None, any_depth=False):
        """(first_line, last_line) of the nth declaration of `name` whose parameter list matches the regex `params` (default any); declarations
        nested deeper than the shallowest one (methods of inner / anonymous classes) are skipped unless any_depth."""
        lines, text = self._load(fname)
        starts = self._offsets(text)
        pat = re.compile(r'(?:(?:public|private|protected|static|final|synchronized|abstract|native)\s+)*[\w<>\[\],.? ]+\s+%s\s*\(([^)]*)\)\s*(?:throws[\w\s,.]+)?\{' % re.escape(name))
        pat2 = re.compile(r'\b%s\s*\(([^)]*)\)\s*(?:throws[\w\s,.]+)?\{' % re.escape(name))   # constructors
        hits = []
        for m in list(pat.finditer(text)) + [m for m in pat2.finditer(text) if m.start() not in {x.start() for x in pat.finditer(text)}]:
            head = text[max(0, m.start() - 40):m.start()]
            if re.search(r'\b(new|return|else)\s*$', head) or re.search(r'[=.]\s*$', head):
                continue
            if params is not None and not re.search(params, m.group(1)):
                continue
            hits.append(m)
        hits.sort(key=lambda m: m.start())
        if not any_depth and hits:
            def depth_at(off):
                d = 0
                for ch in text[:off]:
                    if ch == '{':
                        d += 1
                    elif ch == '}':
                        d -= 1
                return d
            ds = [depth_at(m.start()) for m in hits]
            lo = min(ds)
            hits = [m for m, d in zip(hits, ds) if d == lo]
        if nth >= len(hits):
            raise KeyError('%s.%s #%d' % (fname, name, nth))
        m = hits[nth]
        ob = m.end() - 1
        cb = self._match(text, ob)
        return self._line_of(starts, m.start()), self._line_of(starts, cb)

    def enclosing(self, fname, line):
        lines, text = self._load(fname)
        starts = self._offsets(text)
        best = None
        for m in re.finditer(r'\b([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(([^;{}]*)\)\s*(?:throws[\w\s,.]+)?\{', text):
            if m.group(1) in ('if', 'for', 'while', 'switch', 'catch', 'synchronized', 'return', 'new', 'else'):
                continue
            ob = m.end() - 1
            cb = self._match(text, ob)
            a, b = self._line_of(starts, m.start()), self._line_of(starts, cb)
            if a <= line <= b and (best is None or a >= best[1]):
                best = (m.group(1), a)
        return best[0] if best else None

    def uses(self, regex):
        out = []
        rx = re.compile(regex)
        for fname, paths in sorted(self.idx.items()):
            if not fname.endswith('.java'):
                continue
            try:
                lines, text = self._load(fname)
            except KeyError:
                continue
            for i, ln in enumerate(text.split('\n'), 1):
                if rx.search(ln):
                    out.append((fname, i, self.enclosing(fname, i)))
        return out

    def calls_in(self, src, a, b, AUTO):
        return [c for c in AUTO['calls'] if c['src'] == src and a <= c['line'] <= b]


    # ---- the conditions around a line
    def guard_of(self, fname, line, depth=3):
        """The source conditions that enclose `line`, outermost first, as a list of short strings: `cond` of an if, `not (cond)` of the matching else,
        `case X`, `for (...)` / `while (...)`, `catch (E)`, `finally`, `synchronized`. Lambdas and anonymous classes end the search (their body runs
        when the library calls it, not where it is written). Returns [] when the line is not inside any condition."""
        lines, text = self._load(fname)
        raw = '\n'.join(lines)
        starts = self._offsets(text)
        a, b = None, None
        try:
            name = self.enclosing(fname, line)
        except KeyError:
            return []
        # method body start: the nearest method declaration that contains the line
        best = None
        for m in re.finditer(r'\b([A-Za-z_]\w*)\s*\(([^;{}]*)\)\s*(?:throws[\w\s,.]+)?\{', text):
            if m.group(1) in ('if', 'for', 'while', 'switch', 'catch', 'synchronized', 'return', 'new', 'else'):
                continue
            ob = m.end() - 1
            cb = self._match(text, ob)
            la, lb = self._line_of(starts, m.start()), self._line_of(starts, cb)
            if la <= line <= lb and (best is None or la >= best[0]):
                best = (la, lb, ob)
        if not best:
            return []
        target = starts[line - 1] + len(lines[line - 1]) - len(lines[line - 1].lstrip())
        body = text[best[2]:target]
        stack = []                 # (header, kind, cond)
        closed = {}                # depth -> (kind, cond) of the last block closed at that depth
        last = best[2] + 1
        i = best[2]
        n = len(text)
        j = best[2] + 1
        seg_start = j
        depth = 0
        labels = {}                # depth -> last case label seen at that depth (switch body)
        def classify(header):
            h = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', header).strip()
            m = re.search(r'(?:^|\})\s*else\s+if\s*\((.*)\)$', h)
            if m:
                return ('elseif', m.group(1))
            m = re.search(r'^(?:.*?[;{}:]\s*)?if\s*\((.*)\)$', h)
            if m:
                return ('if', m.group(1))
            if re.search(r'(?:^|\})\s*else$', h):
                return ('else', None)
            m = re.search(r'(for|while)\s*\((.*)\)$', h)
            if m:
                return (m.group(1), m.group(2))
            m = re.search(r'catch\s*\(([^)]*)\)$', h)
            if m:
                return ('catch', m.group(1))
            if re.search(r'(^|[;{}])\s*finally$', h):
                return ('finally', None)
            if re.search(r'synchronized\s*\(.*\)$', h):
                return ('sync', None)
            if re.search(r'switch\s*\(.*\)$', h):
                return ('switch', None)
            if re.search(r'->\s*$', h) or re.search(r'\bnew\s+[\w.<>]+\s*\([^)]*\)$', h) or re.search(r'\)\s*->$', h):
                return ('lambda', None)
            if re.search(r'(?:class|interface|enum)\s+\w+.*$', h):
                return ('lambda', None)
            return ('block', None)
        pos = best[2] + 1
        while pos < target:
            ch = text[pos]
            if ch == '{':
                header = text[seg_start:pos]
                kind, cond = classify(header)
                if kind in ('if', 'elseif', 'for', 'while', 'catch'):
                    rk, rc = classify(raw[seg_start:pos])
                    if rk == kind and rc:
                        cond = rc
                if kind == 'else':
                    prev = closed.get(len(stack))
                    cond = prev[1] if prev and prev[0] in ('if', 'elseif') else None
                    kind = 'else'
                stack.append((kind, cond))
                seg_start = pos + 1
            elif ch == '}':
                if stack:
                    k, c = stack.pop()
                    closed[len(stack)] = (k, c)
                    for d_ in [d for d in labels if d > len(stack)]:
                        del labels[d_]
                seg_start = pos + 1
            elif ch == ';':
                seg_start = pos + 1
            else:
                mm = re.match(r'(case\s+[^:;{}]+?|default)\s*:(?!:)', text[pos:pos + 200])
                if mm and (text[pos - 1].isspace() or text[pos - 1] in '{};') and stack and stack[-1][0] == 'switch':
                    rl = re.match(r'(case\s+[^:;{}]+?|default)\s*:(?!:)', raw[pos:pos + 200])
                    labels[len(stack)] = re.sub(r'^case\s+', 'case ', re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', (rl or mm).group(1)).strip())
                    pos += mm.end()
                    seg_start = pos
                    continue
            pos += 1
        out = []
        for d, (k, c) in enumerate(stack):
            if k == 'lambda':
                out = []
                continue
            if k == 'if' or k == 'elseif':
                out.append(re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', c).strip())
            elif k == 'else':
                out.append('not (%s)' % re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', c).strip() if c else 'else')
            elif k in ('for', 'while'):
                out.append('%s (%s)' % (k, re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', c).strip()))
            elif k == 'catch':
                out.append('catch (%s)' % c.strip())
            elif k == 'finally':
                out.append('finally')
            elif k == 'sync':
                pass
            if k == 'switch' and (d + 1) in labels:
                out.append(labels[d + 1])
        # a braceless if right before the call
        tail = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', raw[seg_start:target]).strip()
        mm = re.match(r'(?:else )?if\s*\((.*)\)\s*\S?.*$', tail)
        if mm and tail.count('(') >= 1:
            out.append(mm.group(1)[:200])
        return out[-depth:]

    # ---- branches of a method
    def branches(self, fname, name, nth=0, params=None, any_depth=False):
        lines, text = self._load(fname)
        starts = self._offsets(text)
        a, b = self.method_lines(fname, name, nth, params, any_depth)
        o0 = starts[a - 1]
        o1 = starts[b]
        body_open = text.index('{', o0)
        body_close = self._match(text, body_open)
        body = text[body_open + 1:body_close]
        base = body_open + 1
        # top-level statements: depth 0 of the body
        m = None
        depth = 0
        i = 0
        while i < len(body):
            c = body[i]
            if c == '{':
                depth += 1
            elif c == '}':
                depth -= 1
            elif depth == 0 and body.startswith('switch', i) and re.match(r'switch\s*\(', body[i:]) and (i == 0 or not (body[i - 1].isalnum() or body[i - 1] == '_')):
                m = ('switch', i)
                break
            elif depth == 0 and body.startswith('if', i) and re.match(r'if\s*\(', body[i:]) and (i == 0 or not (body[i - 1].isalnum() or body[i - 1] == '_')) and not re.search(r'else\s*$', body[:i]):
                m = ('if', i)
                break
            i += 1
        if not m:
            return []
        kind, i = m
        if kind == 'switch':
            p = body.index('(', i)
            pe = self._match(body, p, '(', ')')
            ob = body.index('{', pe)
            cb = self._match(body, ob)
            inner = body[ob + 1:cb]
            ibase = base + ob + 1
            labels = []      # (label text, offset of label start, offset of colon end)
            d = 0
            j = 0
            while j < len(inner):
                c = inner[j]
                if c == '{':
                    d += 1
                elif c == '}':
                    d -= 1
                elif d == 0:
                    mm = re.match(r'(case\s+[^:]+?|default)\s*:(?!:)', inner[j:])
                    if mm and (j == 0 or not (inner[j - 1].isalnum() or inner[j - 1] == '_')):
                        labels.append((re.sub(r'^case\s+', '', mm.group(1)).strip(), j, j + mm.end()))
                        j += mm.end()
                        continue
                j += 1
            out = []
            pending = []
            for k, (lab, s0, s1) in enumerate(labels):
                nxt = labels[k + 1][1] if k + 1 < len(labels) else len(inner)
                stmt = inner[s1:nxt]
                pending.append(lab)
                if stmt.strip() == '':
                    continue          # fall-through label: shares the next range
                la = self._line_of(starts, ibase + s0)
                lb = self._line_of(starts, ibase + nxt - 1)
                last = inner[:nxt].rstrip()
                lb = self._line_of(starts, ibase + len(last) - 1)
                for l_ in pending:
                    out.append((l_, la, lb))
                pending = []
            return out
        # if / else-if chain
        out = []
        j = i
        while True:
            mm = re.match(r'if\s*\(', body[j:])
            if not mm:
                break
            p = j + body[j:].index('(')
            pe = self._match(body, p, '(', ')')
            cond = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', body[p + 1:pe]).strip()
            k = pe + 1
            while body[k] in ' \n\t\r':
                k += 1
            if body[k] == '{':
                ke = self._match(body, k)
            else:
                ke = body.index(';', k)
            out.append((cond, self._line_of(starts, base + j), self._line_of(starts, base + ke)))
            t = ke + 1
            while t < len(body) and body[t] in ' \n\t\r':
                t += 1
            if body.startswith('else', t):
                t += 4
                while t < len(body) and body[t] in ' \n\t\r':
                    t += 1
                if re.match(r'if\s*\(', body[t:]):
                    j = t
                    continue
                if body[t] == '{':
                    te = self._match(body, t)
                else:
                    te = body.index(';', t)
                out.append(('else', self._line_of(starts, base + t), self._line_of(starts, base + te)))
            break
        return out


if __name__ == '__main__':
    app, fname, meth = sys.argv[1:4]
    CS = Cases(app)
    print(CS.method_lines(fname, meth))
    for b in CS.branches(fname, meth):
        print(b)

FILE	tools/check_bytecode_lines.py	5020	71c9b930cc8f5db0280a0b7a69eb09abb329428ede42397e65379054a1d63369	utf8
"""Check that the source line cited by an edge that came from the bytecode scan really contains the call.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/check_bytecode_lines.py <App> [--all] [--emit]
For every edge whose evidence has `SRC File.java:N (bytecode)` it looks for the callee's simple name (method name, or the class name for a
constructor) on line N of the source (flavor-aware, same lookup as show_route.py). The name is searched on the cited line first, then up to
2 lines before and after (javac gives a `new X(...)` that sits inside a chained call the line of the previous call, so the line can be off by one).
For a constructor that is nowhere near, it looks up to 30 lines back for `new Name(` (the start of a multi-line expression).
Listed: the edges whose cited line does not show the callee, with the correct line when one was found.
Skipped and counted: lambdas, <clinit>, anonymous classes, implicit constructor calls (a constructor or static initializer calling another
constructor: super(), this(), enum constants) and the Dagger-generated injector calls (*HelperModule.xxxHelper).
--emit also prints a FIX_LINES literal (edge -> corrected line text) that can be pasted into the EDIT hook of graph_def.py.
Exit status 1 when anything is listed.
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import show_route  # noqa: E402

IMPLICIT = 'IMPLICIT'


def callee_token(dst, src=''):
    d = dst.split('@')[0]
    s_ = src.split('@')[0].rpartition('.')[2]
    if '.<init>' in d and s_.startswith(('<init>', '<clinit>')):
        return IMPLICIT                                   # super() / this() / enum constant
    if re.match(r'.*HelperModule\.\w+Helper$', d):
        return IMPLICIT                                   # Dagger-generated injector
    if re.match(r'Dagger\w+\$Builder\.<init>', d):
        return 'builder'
    cls, _, meth = d.rpartition('.')
    if meth.startswith('<clinit>') or 'lambda$' in meth or meth.startswith('access$'):
        return None
    if meth.startswith('<init>'):
        simple = re.sub(r'^\d+', '', cls.split('$')[-1])      # a local class is named Outer$1Name
        return None if not simple else simple
    meth = meth.split('(')[0]
    return meth or None


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
    flavors = show_route.built_flavors(app)
    idx = show_route.source_index(app)
    cache, bad, checked, skipped, implicit, fixes = {}, [], 0, 0, 0, {}
    for x in g['edges']:
        for m in re.finditer(r'SRC\s+([\w./$-]+\.java):(\d+)\s*\(bytecode\)', x['evidence'] or ''):
            fname, n = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))
            tok = callee_token(x['dst'], x['src'])
            if tok == IMPLICIT:
                implicit += 1
                continue
            if not tok:
                skipped += 1
                continue
            path = show_route.find(idx, fname, flavors)
            if not path:
                bad.append((x['id'], x['src'], x['dst'], fname, n, 'file not found'))
                continue
            if path not in cache:
                cache[path] = open(path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
            lines = cache[path]
            checked += 1
            rx = re.compile(r'\b%s\b' % re.escape(tok))
            if 1 <= n <= len(lines) and rx.search(lines[n - 1]):
                continue
            near = [k for k in (n - 1, n + 1, n - 2, n + 2) if 1 <= k <= len(lines) and rx.search(lines[k - 1])]
            start = None
            if not near and '<init>' in x['dst']:
                nrx = re.compile(r'\bnew\s+%s\s*\(' % re.escape(tok))
                for k in range(n - 1, max(0, n - 31), -1):
                    if nrx.search(lines[k - 1]):
                        start = k
                        break
            if near:
                what, new = 'name on line %d' % near[0], '%d' % near[0]
            elif start:
                what, new = 'new %s( starts on line %d' % (tok, start), '%d-%d' % (start, n)
            else:
                what, new = 'name not within 2 lines', None
            bad.append((x['id'], x['src'], x['dst'], fname, n, what))
            if new:
                fixes[(x['src'], x['dst'], 'SRC %s:%d (bytecode)' % (fname, n))] = 'SRC %s:%s (bytecode line was %d)' % (fname, new, n)
    print('%s: %d bytecode-cited edges checked, %d skipped (lambda / clinit / anonymous), %d implicit (super / enum / Dagger), '
          '%d where the cited line does not show the callee' % (app, checked, skipped, implicit, len(bad)))
    for b in bad[:(len(bad) if '--all' in argv else 60)]:
        print('  %s %s -> %s  cited %s:%d  (%s)' % b)
    if '--emit' in argv:
        print('FIX_LINES = {')
        for k, v in sorted(fixes.items()):
            print('    %r: %r,' % (k, v))
        print('}')
    return 1 if bad else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

FILE	tools/check_citations.py	4680	e875d7c9f7665ccd0c50e3725a02c10c59ffc98a176b1f705969b6fb1c5dad89	utf8
"""Check that every framework citation (FW-SRC) of a schema 2 graph points at a source file we hold, at lines inside that file.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/check_citations.py <App> [--show]
Reads the evidence of every edge and the UNREACHABLE reasons in graphs/<App>/out/*_trigger_graph.json. Two citation styles exist:
  core/java/android/app/Activity.java:3001-3010, 3386-3388      path from the Android source root (Diary style)
  android-8.1.0_r1:app/Activity.java:3001-3010                  path tail after the version tag (Currency style)
Files are looked up in work/fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1/ and graphs/<App>/.plan/fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1/ (the tails are matched).
Library citations (lib ..., MPAndroidChart-...) are counted but not checked. It also counts the evidence claims that no source backs
(FW-MEMORY, DYN, UNVERIFIED), which a converted graph must not have. --show prints the first cited line of every distinct citation, to be read.
It checks that the file exists and the lines exist; that a line says what the edge claims is for a person to read.
Exit status 1 when a file is missing or a line is out of range.
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

LINES = r'\d+(?:-\d+)?(?:\s*,\s*\d+(?:-\d+)?)*'
ROOT_STYLE = re.compile(r'((?:core|services|data|libcore|packages)/[\w./\-]+\.(?:java|xml)):(' + LINES + ')')
TAG_STYLE = re.compile(r'android-8\.1\.0_r1:([\w./\-]+\.(?:java|xml)):(' + LINES + ')')
LIB_STYLE = re.compile(r'FW-SRC lib |MPAndroidChart-v[\d.]+:')
UNBACKED = re.compile(r'(?:^|;\s*)(FW-MEMORY|DYN|UNVERIFIED)\b')


def caches(app):
    out = []
    for c in ('fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1', 'graphs/%s/.plan/fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1' % app):
        if os.path.isdir(c):
            out.append(c)
    return out


def files_in(cache):
    res = {}
    for dp, _, fn in os.walk(cache):
        for f in fn:
            full = os.path.join(dp, f)
            res[os.path.relpath(full, cache)] = full
    return res


def resolve(path, style, index):
    if style == 'root':
        for cache, files in index:
            if path in files:
                return files[path]
        return None
    for cache, files in index:
        for rel, full in files.items():
            if rel == path or rel.endswith('/' + path):
                return full
    return None


def line_ranges(spec):
    for part in spec.split(','):
        a, _, b = part.strip().partition('-')
        yield int(a), int(b or a)


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
    index = [(c, files_in(c)) for c in caches(app)]
    texts = [(x['id'], x['evidence'] or '') for x in g['edges']]
    texts += [(m, l['unreachable']['text']) for m, l in g['labels'].items() if l.get('unreachable')]
    seen, problems, libs, unbacked = {}, [], 0, []
    line_cache = {}
    for owner, ev in texts:
        if LIB_STYLE.search(ev):
            libs += 1
        if UNBACKED.search(ev):
            unbacked.append(owner)
        for style, rx in (('root', ROOT_STYLE), ('tag', TAG_STYLE)):
            for m in rx.finditer(ev):
                path, spec = m.group(1), re.sub(r'\s+', '', m.group(2))
                key = (path, spec)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                full = resolve(path, style, index)
                if not full:
                    seen[key] = None
                    problems.append('%s: file not held: %s' % (owner, path))
                    continue
                if full not in line_cache:
                    line_cache[full] = open(full, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
                lines = line_cache[full]
                bad = [r for r in line_ranges(spec) if not 1 <= r[0] <= r[1] <= len(lines)]
                seen[key] = (full, lines, spec)
                if bad:
                    problems.append('%s: %s has %d lines, cited %s' % (owner, path, len(lines), spec))
    print('citations: %d distinct, %d problems; library citations (not checked): %d; evidence with FW-MEMORY / DYN / UNVERIFIED: %d' % (
        len(seen), len(problems), libs, len(unbacked)))
    for p in problems[:40]:
        print('  PROBLEM ' + p)
    if unbacked:
        print('  unbacked evidence on: ' + ', '.join(unbacked[:12]) + (' ...' if len(unbacked) > 12 else ''))
    if '--show' in argv:
        for (path, spec), val in sorted(seen.items()):
            if val:
                first = next(iter(line_ranges(spec)))[0]
                print('%s:%s\n      %s' % (path, spec, val[1][first - 1].strip()[:150]))
    return 1 if problems else 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

#!/bin/bash
# Regression check of the generator: run it on the five hand-written apps (tools/tests/_t_*) and print the agreement with the
# hand-written verdicts (also written to tools/tests/calibration.json). It must keep 0 baseline contradictions.

FILE	tools/check_routes.py	2747	e3671278c593bc38f2ee957a54f4e5f7846796a310baaa08af01ae3ed5abf6b9	utf8
"""Flag derived routes whose guards contradict each other (the derived path ignores guards, SCHEMA_V2.md section 4).

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/check_routes.py <App> [--all]
For every REACHABLE method it walks the best route (methods.csv route_edge_ids) and looks for a step whose guard or ENV gate contradicts an
earlier step of the same route: a RESULT_CANCELED step followed by a RESULT_OK guard, histMap == null followed by histMap != null
(unless the download completes in between), network off followed by network on. It is a heuristic over the guard text:
a hit is a route to read by hand (tools/show_route.py), not proof of an error. Prints one line per flagged method, or all with --all.
"""
import csv
import glob
import json
import re
import sys

PAIRS = [
    ('result', r'RESULT_CANCELED|Cancel tapped|Back closes the picker', r'RESULT_OK|Select tapped|resultCode == RESULT_OK|DISPLAY_MODE: setResult\(RESULT_OK'),
    ('histMap', r'histMap == null', r'histMap != null|history loaded'),
    ('network', r'network off|not \(network connected|not allowed by the Wi-Fi only', r'network connected|ECB download succeeds'),
]
DOWNLOAD_DONE = 'ECB download succeeds'   # an edge with this ENV gate is the download completing, which turns histMap == null into loaded


def text(x, keys):
    return ' '.join(str(x.get(k) or '') for k in keys)


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
    edges = {x['id']: x for x in g['edges']}
    flagged = 0
    for r in csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app, encoding='utf-8')):
        if r['verdict'] != 'REACHABLE':
            continue
        route = [edges[i] for i in r['route_edge_ids'].split()]
        hits = []
        for name, first, second in PAIRS:
            for i, a in enumerate(route):
                ta = text(a, ('trigger', 'guard', 'env'))
                if not re.search(first, ta):
                    continue
                for j, b in enumerate(route[i + 1:], i + 1):
                    tb = text(b, ('guard', 'env'))
                    if name == 'histMap' and any(DOWNLOAD_DONE in (e.get('env') or '') for e in route[i:j + 1]):
                        continue
                    if re.search(second, tb) and not re.search(first, tb):
                        hits.append('%s: %s [%s] then %s [%s]' % (name, a['id'], (a['guard'] or a['trigger'])[:40], b['id'], (b['guard'] or b['env'] or '')[:40]))
                        break
        if hits or '--all' in argv:
            flagged += bool(hits)
            print('%s %s' % (r['method'], '; '.join(hits[:3])))
    print('# %s: %d routes flagged' % (app, flagged))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

FILE	tools/compare_graphs.py	1646	678edf031a149ee86a3c4d3980ac42456ae0335505f1ae47a261f54829dbcff5	utf8
"""Compare the verdicts of two trigger graphs of the same app (hand-written vs generated).

Usage: python3 -I compare_graphs.py <hand.json> <gen.json> [--list N]
Prints a confusion table of verdicts and the first N methods of each disagreement class.
"""
import collections
import json
import sys


def main(argv):
    a = json.load(open(argv[1]))
    b = json.load(open(argv[2]))
    n = int(argv[argv.index('--list') + 1]) if '--list' in argv else 12
    va, vb = a['verdict'], b['verdict']
    common = [m for m in va if m in vb]
    tab = collections.Counter((va[m], vb[m]) for m in common)
    kinds = sorted({k for p in tab for k in p})
    print('methods in both: %d (hand %d, generated %d)' % (len(common), len(va), len(vb)))
    print('%-24s' % 'hand \\ generated' + ''.join('%-24s' % k for k in kinds))
    for ka in kinds:
        print('%-24s' % ka + ''.join('%-24d' % tab.get((ka, kb), 0) for kb in kinds))
    agree = sum(v for (x, y), v in tab.items() if x == y)
    print('exact agreement: %.1f%%' % (100.0 * agree / len(common)))
    dead = lambda v: v in ('DEAD', 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY')
    both_alive = sum(v for (x, y), v in tab.items() if not dead(x) and not dead(y))
    print('agreement on dead-vs-alive: %.1f%%' % (100.0 * sum(v for (x, y), v in tab.items() if dead(x) == dead(y)) / len(common)))
    groups = collections.defaultdict(list)
    for m in common:
        if va[m] != vb[m]:
            groups[(va[m], vb[m])].append(m)
    for (x, y), ms in sorted(groups.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[1])):
        print('\n%s -> %s: %d' % (x, y, len(ms)))
        for m in ms[:n]:
            print('   ', m)


main(sys.argv)

FILE	tools/coverage_methods.py	1527	5333f2f5774657ced2de92caa3ed3a2b6229244fdce1cfafd4aa275be05b3726	utf8
"""Per-method JaCoCo hit status for an app's APE and Monkey runs.

Usage: python3 -I coverage_methods.py <app_dir> [--json OUT] [--grep SUBSTRING]
Prints Class.method(desc) with M (Monkey) / A (APE) hit flags.
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET


def load(path):
    res = {}
    for cls in ET.parse(path).getroot().iter('class'):
        cn = cls.get('name')
        if 'JacocoInstrument' in cn:
            continue
        for m in cls.findall('method'):
            c = {x.get('type'): int(x.get('covered')) for x in m.findall('counter')}
            res[(cn, m.get('name'), m.get('desc'))] = c.get('METHOD', 0) > 0
    return res


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    out_json = argv[argv.index('--json') + 1] if '--json' in argv else None
    pat = argv[argv.index('--grep') + 1] if '--grep' in argv else ''
    mk = load(os.path.join(app, 'monkey/report/report.xml'))
    ap = load(os.path.join(app, 'ape/report/report.xml'))
    rows = []
    for k in sorted(set(mk) | set(ap)):
        cn, name, desc = k
        rows.append({'class': cn, 'short': cn.rsplit('/', 1)[-1] + '.' + name, 'desc': desc,
                     'monkey': bool(mk.get(k)), 'ape': bool(ap.get(k))})
    for r in rows:
        if pat in r['short']:
            print('%s %s  %s%s' % ('M' if r['monkey'] else '-', 'A' if r['ape'] else '-', r['short'], r['desc']))
    if out_json:
        with open(out_json, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(rows, f, indent=1)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)
