               'The ten frontier nodes that unlock the most never-run methods:\n%s\n') % (
        len(fr_rows), ', '.join('%d %s' % (v, k) for k, v in fr_kind.most_common()), '\n'.join(fr_lines))
steps = [int(r['steps_from_launch']) for r in rows if r['steps_from_launch']]
avg_steps = sum(steps) / len(steps) if steps else 0
cal = json.load(open('tools/tests/calibration.json'))
cal_da = ', '.join('%.1f%% (%s)' % (v['dead_vs_alive'], k) for k, v in cal.items())
cal_ex = ', '.join('%.1f%% (%s)' % (v['exact'], k) for k, v in cal.items())
title = g['title'].replace(' trigger graph', '')
review = ''
if os.path.exists(gd + '/review_notes.md'):
    review = '\n## Review notes for this app\n\n' + open(gd + '/review_notes.md', encoding='utf-8').read().strip() + '\n'

txt = '''# %(title)s: page-jump and method-call graph (generated)

A map of how every method of the open-source Android app **%(app)s** (%(prov)s, source commit `%(commit)s`, package `%(pkg)s`, APK sha256 `%(sha)s`) can be triggered: from which page, by which user gesture or system event, through which framework callback or call. It is the build in `final_java_only/%(app)s`.

**How this one differs from Currency, hash-checker, openlauncher and Omni-Notes:** those graphs were written by hand from reading the source. This one was **generated** by `tools/gen_edges.py` from the bytecode, `AndroidManifest.xml` and `res/` XML, and then checked against the baseline coverage and reviewed (see "How much to trust it"). Every number below is recomputed from the files in this folder.

## What is in this folder

| File | What it is |
|---|---|
| `%(prefix)s_trigger_graph.json` | The graph: pages, methods, verdicts, edges. Every edge has an `evidence` label; an edge may carry `env` (needs something outside the app) and `needs_state` (needs an earlier action or setting). |
| `methods.csv` | One row per method: verdict, steps from launch, the user taps on the best path, the path, number of incoming edges, note. Extra columns: `jvm_descriptor`, `monkey_hit`, `ape_hit` (1-hour API 27 baseline runs; empty = JaCoCo does not report the method), and for methods the baseline never ran `frontier_from`, `frontier_first_unrun`, `frontier_edge_kind`, `frontier_edge_evidence`, `steps_after_frontier` (where the chain from code that ran first enters code that never ran). |
| `%(prefix)s_trigger_graph.html` | The same data in the viewer from the Currency example. Optional. |
| `build_report.txt` | Output of the validation script (0 errors, 0 warnings, baseline cross-check, the list of reachable methods the baseline never ran). |

## Numbers

- **%(methods)d methods** (the app's own code, aligned with what JaCoCo reports), **%(pages)d pages** (%(pseudo)d of them `S_NEW_<Class>` anchors: a class whose empty default constructor is not in the JaCoCo method universe is constructed through such a node), **%(nedges)d edges**: %(auto)d from the bytecode call extractor, %(gen)d added by the generator or by hand.
- Edge kinds: %(kinds)s.
- Verdicts: **%(reach)d reachable**, **%(setup)d reachable after setup**, **%(dead)d dead**, %(fwo)d framework-only.
- %(env_edges)d edges are gated by an outside environment, %(need_edges)d need an earlier action or setting; %(env_methods)d methods are reached only through an environment gate, %(setup_methods)d only after setup. Average route length: %(avg).1f edges.
- Evidence labels: %(evid)s.
- Largest dead groups (auto-classified, see limits): %(dead_lines)s.

## Comparison with the baseline coverage (APE and Monkey, 1 hour each, API 27)

- %(n_hit)d of the %(n_obs)d observable methods ran in at least one run (Monkey %(n_mon)d, APE %(n_ape)d, both %(n_both)d). **%(dead_hit)d dead or framework-only methods ran**, so the coverage contradicts no verdict.
- **%(n_unhit)d methods were run by neither tool.** By the graph: %(u_dead)d are dead; %(u_env)d are reachable only through an outside environment; %(u_setup)d need an earlier in-app action or setting; **%(u_clean)d are reachable with no modelled setup and no outside help, yet were never run** (the clearest target list for the paper). Classes holding most of them: %(clean_lines)s.
%(fr_text)s- Harness facts that matter for target selection: `merged.ec` omits the launch dump `00000.ec`, so first-launch code can show `monkey_hit = 0` although it ran; one dump per tool may be unreadable, and a process that dies between 30-second dumps loses its coverage, so "never hit" does not strictly prove "never ran".

## How much to trust it

- **Method.** `gen_edges.py` applies Android framework rules to the compiled classes: process entry (Application, launcher, exported components behind an `env` gate), lifecycle callbacks and one page `S_<Activity|Fragment>` per screen, UI and system callbacks of every constructed class with a framework ancestor, lambdas and method references (creator method -> body, the tap label is read from the source line), `startActivity(new Intent(ctx, X.class))` and custom intent actions, classes named in layout / xml / menu / navigation files and where they are inflated, activity and permission results, `Handler` messages, ButterKnife / EventBus / data binding / Dagger / Glide / Icepick / Parceler / ORMLite / Room glue, static initialisers, and template-method receiver variants (`Base.m@Sub`). Direct calls come from the bytecode (`extract_calls.py`, class-hierarchy analysis for virtual calls). Methods that nothing reaches are reported DEAD (`AUTO_DEAD`).
- **Calibration on the five hand-written apps** (the generator run on the same inputs, compared with the hand-written verdicts; `tools/calibrate.py`): dead-versus-alive agreement %(cal_da)s; exact verdict agreement %(cal_ex)s. The gap is almost entirely `needs_state`: a human reads "needs a note / a folder / a setting" out of the code, the generator only knows list rows. (openlauncher and Omni-Notes were used while the generator rules were developed; Currency, Diary and hash-checker were not.)
- **Checks done on this app:** the validation script (0 errors, 0 warnings: every method has a route or a DEAD verdict, every page is reachable); the baseline cross-check above (0 contradictions after the generator rules were completed by reading the contradictions one by one); random shortest paths were read for shortcuts.
- **Not checked on a device.** Framework rules are `FW-MEMORY` (recalled, not checked against AOSP source); guesses are `INFERRED`.

## Limits (read before using a verdict)

- **`REACHABLE` means "no modelled setup", not "no setup".** Gates beyond list rows (data that must exist, settings, earlier actions, incoming intents, files on the device) are modelled only where listed in the review notes. Adapter row callbacks, row-based listeners and action modes carry `needs_state` = "the list shows at least one row".
- A callback is reachable as soon as an instance of its class is constructed; the real trigger (a tap on one specific control) is shown in the `gui_steps` column only where the source line names the control. Menu and click handlers that switch on `R.id.x` give one labelled edge per case (`case R.id.x`), found by the nearest preceding case label (`INFERRED`).
- `Handler.handleMessage`, EventBus subscribers and Dagger-provided objects are linked from every send / post / graph-build site (`INFERRED`), so their routes can be shorter than the real ones.
- DEAD is automatic: nothing in the bytecode, manifest, XML or the modelled libraries reaches the method. Entries through reflection, JNI, other generated code or libraries that are not modelled would be missed (the baseline contradiction check found and fixed every such case that the 1-hour runs exercised).
- Calls inside catch blocks, data-dependent branches and feature flags are ordinary calls unless the review notes say otherwise.
%(review)s
## JSON shape (short)

```
{ "title", "screens": {page: description}, "methods": [...], "verdict": {method: REACHABLE|REACHABLE_AFTER_SETUP|DEAD|FRAMEWORK_ONLY},
  "dead": {method: reason}, "framework_only": {method: reason},
  "edges": [ {id, src, dst, kind, trigger, gui[], guard, reads[], writes[], note, needs_state, evidence, env} ] }
```
`src`/`dst` are methods (`Class.method`, overloads as `Class.method(Type,Type)`, classes that share a simple name get a package prefix) or pages (`S_...`). A suffix `@CONTEXT` is the same method in a specific receiver class (`Base.m@Sub`) or context.
''' % dict(
    title=title, app=app, prov=ds.get('provenance', ''), commit=ds.get('source_commit', '')[:8], pkg=ds.get('package', ''),
    sha=ds.get('sha256', '')[:8] + '…' + ds.get('sha256', '')[-8:], prefix=prefix, methods=len(g['methods']), pages=pages,
    pseudo=pseudo, nedges=len(edges), auto=auto, gen=len(edges) - auto,
    kinds=', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(kinds.items())),
    reach=verdict['REACHABLE'], setup=verdict['REACHABLE_AFTER_SETUP'], dead=verdict['DEAD'], fwo=verdict['FRAMEWORK_ONLY'],
    env_edges=env_edges, need_edges=need_edges, env_methods=env_methods, setup_methods=setup_methods, avg=avg_steps,
    evid=', '.join('%d `%s`' % (v, k) for k, v in sorted(evid.items())), dead_lines=', '.join(dead_lines) or 'none',
    n_hit=n_hit, n_obs=len(observable), n_mon=n_mon, n_ape=n_ape, n_both=n_both, dead_hit=len(dead_hit),
    n_unhit=len(unhit), u_dead=len(u_dead), u_env=len(u_env), u_setup=len(u_setup), u_clean=len(u_clean),
    clean_lines=', '.join(clean_lines) or 'none', review=review, fr_text=fr_text, cal_da=cal_da, cal_ex=cal_ex)
open(out + '/README.md', 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(txt)
print('wrote %s/README.md (%d lines); unhit %d: dead %d, env %d, setup %d, clean %d' % (out, txt.count('\n'), len(unhit), len(u_dead), len(u_env), len(u_setup), len(u_clean)))

"""Write graphs/<App>/out/README.md for a schema 2 graph from its build outputs.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/gen_readme_v2.py <App>
Reads out/*_trigger_graph.json, out/methods.csv, out/sanity_check.csv, verification.csv (through the JSON), the dataset notes
(final_java_only/<App>/app/DATASET.txt) and the optional hand-written graphs/<App>/readme_notes.md, whose text is appended
under its own headings. All numbers come from the files, none is typed in.
"""
import collections
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import build_graph  # noqa: E402


def dataset(app):
    d = {}
    path = 'final_java_only/%s/app/DATASET.txt' % app
    if os.path.isfile(path):
        text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read()
        for key in ('provenance', 'source_commit', 'sha256', 'apk', 'package', 'min_sdk', 'target_sdk'):
            m = re.search(r'^%s\s*:\s*(\S+)' % key, text, re.M)   # one line has a carriage return before the colon
            if m:
                d[key] = m.group(1)
    return d


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    gdir = 'graphs/%s' % app
    jp = glob.glob('%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % gdir)[0]
    prefix = os.path.basename(jp).replace('_trigger_graph.json', '')
    g = json.load(open(jp))
    assert g.get('schema') == 2, 'not a schema 2 build'
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('%s/out/methods.csv' % gdir, encoding='utf-8')))
    labels, edges, verdict = g['labels'], g['edges'], g['verdict']
    ds = dataset(app)
    reach = [r for r in rows if r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE']
    unre = [r for r in rows if r['verdict'] == 'UNREACHABLE']
    kinds = collections.Counter(r['unreachable_kind'] for r in unre)
    env_k, state_k = collections.Counter(), collections.Counter()
    for r in reach:
        for k in filter(None, r['env_kinds'].split(';')):
            env_k[k] += 1
        for k in filter(None, r['state_kinds'].split(';')):
            state_k[k] += 1
    cls = collections.Counter(x['edge_class'] for x in edges)
    nonstd = [x for x in edges if x['edge_class'] == 'non_standard']
    conf = collections.Counter(l['confirmed_by'] for l in labels.values())
    unsourced = [x for x in edges if not build_graph.edge_sourced(x)]
    pages = len(g['screens'])
    no_es = [r['method'] for r in reach if r['explicit_standard_route'] == 'no']
    nonstd_route = [r['method'] for r in reach if 'non_standard' in r['route_edge_classes']]
    ver = g.get('verification') or {}
    pstat = collections.Counter(l['path_status'] for l in labels.values() if l['path_status'])
    sanity = None
    sp = '%s/out/sanity_check.csv' % gdir
    if os.path.isfile(sp):
        s = list(csv.DictReader(open(sp, encoding='utf-8')))
        sanity = (sum(1 for r in s if r['monkey_hit'] == '1' or r['ape_hit'] == '1'), sum(1 for r in s if r['flag'] == 'CONTRADICTION'))
    L = []
    title = g['title'].replace(' trigger graph', '')
    L.append('# %s: method-level trigger graph (schema 2)' % title)
    L.append('')
    L.append('How every method of **%s** is triggered, in the standard configuration: the instrumented debug build%s, an API 27 emulator, permissions and fixtures as in `final_java_only`. '
             'Labels follow `handoff/SCHEMA_V2.md`: REACHABLE or UNREACHABLE, ENV and STATE gates, edge classes (explicit, standard, non-standard), and how each label was confirmed.' % (
                 app, ' (`%s`, commit `%s`, APK sha256 `%s`)' % (ds.get('provenance', ''), ds.get('source_commit', '')[:12], ds.get('sha256', '')[:8] + '...') if ds else ''))
    L.append('')
    L.append('## What is in this folder')
    L.append('')
    L.append('| File | What it is |')
    L.append('|---|---|')
    L.append('| `%s_trigger_graph.json` | pages, methods, `verdict`, `labels` (per-method fields), `edges` (with `edge_class`, `env_kinds`, `state_kinds`, stable ids), `paths` (verified paths only), `verification` (counts) |' % prefix)
    L.append('| `methods.csv` | one row per method: verdict, UNREACHABLE reason, ENV and STATE kinds and texts, edge classes on the best route, whether an explicit/standard-only route exists, how the label was confirmed, path status, and the derived best route (`shortest_path`, `route_edge_ids`) |')
    L.append('| `../verification.csv` | the audit log: one row per label, edge or path that was checked, with result, how, and where (file:line) |')
    L.append('| `sanity_check.csv` | tool coverage (Monkey / APE, API 27) next to the labels; a one-way check only, it never sets a label |')
    L.append('| `%s_trigger_graph.html`, `build_report.txt` | the viewer and the validation report |' % prefix)
    L.append('')
    L.append('## Numbers')
    L.append('')
    L.append('- **%d methods**, **%d pages** (including pseudo pages), **%d edges**. Verdicts: **%d REACHABLE**, **%d UNREACHABLE**.' % (len(rows), pages, len(edges), len(reach), len(unre)))
    L.append('- UNREACHABLE by reason: %s.' % (', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(kinds.items())) or 'none'))
    L.append('- ENV gates (methods): %s. STATE gates (methods): %s. Methods with no gate: %d.' % (
        ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(env_k.items())) or 'none', ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(state_k.items())) or 'none',
        sum(1 for r in reach if not r['env_kinds'] and not r['state_kinds'])))
    L.append('- Edge classes: %s. Non-standard edges: %d%s.' % (', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(cls.items())), len(nonstd),
                                                              ' (' + '; '.join(sorted({x['via'] for x in nonstd if x['via']}))[:300] + ')' if nonstd else ''))
    L.append('- %d reachable methods have a non-standard edge on their best route; %d have no explicit-or-standard-only route at all.' % (len(nonstd_route), len(no_es)))
    L.append('- Labels confirmed by: %s (SOURCE: every edge of the best route cites a source line, in the app or in the Android 8.1 / library source; INFERRED: some edge rests on inference or on another app).' % ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(conf.items())))
    L.append('')
    L.append('## How much to trust it')
    L.append('')
    if ver:
        L.append('Verification log (`verification.csv`), counts of what was checked and found:')
        L.append('')
        L.append('| Checked | Rows | Correct | Errors found (fixed) | Unclear |')
        L.append('|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|')
        for t, name in (('label', 'labels'), ('edge', 'edges (framework and other claims read against source)'), ('path', 'paths (stratified sample, read step by step)')):
            v = ver[t]
            L.append('| %s | %d | %d | %d | %d |' % (name, v['checked'] + v['unclear'], v['correct'], v['errors_found'], v['unclear']))
        L.append('')
        L.append('Path status over all reachable methods: %s.' % ', '.join('%s %d' % kv for kv in sorted(pstat.items())))
        L.append('The rows are the items that were individually checked, not a random sample of all labels; see the notes below for how they were chosen and what the unclear rows wait for.')
    else:
        L.append('No verification log yet: every label is `UNCHECKED` and nothing is claimed beyond what the evidence column says.')
    L.append('')
    if unsourced:
        L.append('Edges whose evidence is not a source line (%d of %d): %s' % (len(unsourced), len(edges), '; '.join(
            '%s (%s -> %s)' % (x['id'], x['src'], x['dst']) for x in unsourced[:12]) + (' ...' if len(unsourced) > 12 else '')))
        L.append('')
    if sanity:
        L.append('Sanity check against tool coverage (never used to set a label): %d methods ran in the 1-hour Monkey / APE runs on API 27; **%d** of them are labelled UNREACHABLE.' % sanity)
        L.append('')
    notes = '%s/readme_notes.md' % gdir
    if os.path.isfile(notes):
        L.append(open(notes, encoding='utf-8').read().rstrip())
        L.append('')
    L.append('## JSON shape (short)')
    L.append('')
    L.append('```')
    L.append('{ "schema": 2, "title", "screens": {page: description}, "methods": [...], "verdict": {method: REACHABLE|UNREACHABLE},')
    L.append('  "labels": {method: {unreachable: {kind, text}, env: [{kinds, text}], state: [{kinds, text}], route_edge_classes, explicit_standard_route,')
    L.append('                      confirmed_by, source_ref, path_status}},')
    L.append('  "edges": [ {id, src, dst, kind, edge_class, via, trigger, gui[], guard, reads[], writes[], note, needs_state, state_kinds, env, env_kinds, evidence, class_basis} ],')
    L.append('  "paths": [ {method, edges[], edge_classes, explicit_standard_only, env, state, verified_by, ref} ], "verification": {...} }')
    L.append('```')
    L.append('')
    L.append('`src`/`dst` are methods (`Class.method`, overloads as `Class.method(Type,Type)`) or pages (`S_...`). A suffix `@CONTEXT` is the same method or page in a specific context. '
             'Edge ids are a hash of the edge content, so they stay stable when other edges change.')
    open('%s/out/README.md' % gdir, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(L) + '\n')
    print('wrote %s/out/README.md (%d lines)' % (gdir, len(L)))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

FILE	tools/hubs.py	764	26925c493ad8f20a06029bf39be3ce41acfb330a25ceebcd66b4a72082bd16f2	utf8
"""Nodes that appear on the most shortest paths (hubs = likely shortcuts) and paths without any tap.
Usage: python3 -I hubs.py <App> [N]"""
import csv, collections, sys
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % sys.argv[1])))
n = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 25
cnt = collections.Counter(); notap = 0; total = 0
for r in rows:
    if not r['verdict'].startswith('REACHABLE') or not r['shortest_path']:
        continue
    total += 1
    nodes = r['shortest_path'].split(' > ')
    for x in nodes[1:-1]:
        cnt[x] += 1
    if not r['gui_steps_on_shortest_path'].strip():
        notap += 1
print('%d reachable with a path; %d have no tap at all' % (total, notap))
for x, c in cnt.most_common(n):
    print('%5d %s' % (c, x))

"""Classes that look environment- or fixture-dependent, with their method counts and baseline hits (to choose cfg GROUPS).
Usage: python3 -I keyword_clusters.py <App>"""
import csv, collections, re, sys
rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % sys.argv[1])))
KW = {
 'network': r'(http|url|download|upload|sync|cloud|dropbox|oauth|ftp|smb|sftp|webdav|network|socket|retrofit|okhttp|request|fetch|torrent|tracker|feed|rss|peer)',
 'account': r'(login|auth|token|account|credential|password|passcode|biometric|fingerprint|keystore|crypt)',
 'hardware': r'(camera|record|audio|bluetooth|nfc|gps|location|sensor|vibrat|flash|torch|tts|speech|voice|microphone|scanner|qr|barcode)',
 'files': r'(import|export|backup|restore|zip|archive|csv|json|xml|qif|ofx|pdf|storage|document|media|folder)',
 'system': r'(notification|widget|shortcut|alarm|boot|receiver|service|job|scheduler|worker|provider)',
}
cls = collections.defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0, 0, 0])
for r in rows:
    c = r['method'].split('(')[0].rsplit('.', 1)[0]
    top = c.split('$')[0]
    h = r['monkey_hit'] == '1' or r['ape_hit'] == '1'
    d = cls[top]
    d[0] += 1; d[1] += 1 if h else 0; d[2] += 1 if r['verdict'] == 'DEAD' else 0
for k, rx in KW.items():
    print('== %s' % k)
    out = []
    for c, (n, h, dd, _x) in sorted(cls.items()):
        if re.search(rx, c, re.I) and n - h - dd > 0:
            out.append('%s %d/%d/%d' % (c, n - h - dd, h, dd))
    print('  (unhit-alive / hit / dead):', '; '.join(out[:60]))

FILE	tools/list_methods.py	5651	c35c803818665e61d15c8c601357b32cafd35a74078b760ce543f75db86e38a7	utf8
"""List every method with a body in a directory of .class files (a javap -p substitute).

Usage: python3 -I list_methods.py <classes_dir> [<classes_dir> ...] [--exclude SUBSTRING ...] [--json OUT]
                                   [--jacoco report.xml]

--jacoco keeps only methods that the JaCoCo report lists, plus private no-arg constructors (JaCoCo
filters the empty ones), so the universe matches the coverage data (drops @Generated code such as
Dagger, enum values/valueOf, synthetic switch maps). Dropped methods are listed on stderr.

Drops: abstract/native methods, synthetic or bridge methods (access$NNN, bridges),
and synthetic inner-class constructors. Keeps lambdas (lambda$m$0), constructors,
static initialisers and inner/anonymous class methods.
Prints one line per method: Class.method<TAB>descriptor<TAB>flags.
"""
import json
import os
import struct
import sys

ACC_STATIC, ACC_BRIDGE, ACC_NATIVE = 0x0008, 0x0040, 0x0100
ACC_ABSTRACT, ACC_SYNTHETIC = 0x0400, 0x1000


def parse_class(path):
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        data = f.read()
    if data[:4] != b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbe':
        raise ValueError('not a class file: ' + path)
    pos = 10
    (count,) = struct.unpack_from('>H', data, 8)
    pool = [None] * count
    i = 1
    while i < count:
        tag = data[pos]
        pos += 1
        if tag == 1:  # Utf8
            (n,) = struct.unpack_from('>H', data, pos)
            pool[i] = data[pos + 2:pos + 2 + n].decode('utf-8', 'replace')
            pos += 2 + n
        elif tag in (3, 4):
            pos += 4
        elif tag in (5, 6):  # long/double take two slots
            pos += 8
            i += 1
        elif tag == 7:  # Class
            pool[i] = ('class', struct.unpack_from('>H', data, pos)[0])
            pos += 2
        elif tag in (8, 16, 19, 20):
            pos += 2
        elif tag in (9, 10, 11, 12, 17, 18):
            pos += 4
        elif tag == 15:
            pos += 3
        else:
            raise ValueError('bad constant tag %d in %s' % (tag, path))
        i += 1
    _access, this_idx = struct.unpack_from('>HH', data, pos)
    pos += 6
    (n_ifaces,) = struct.unpack_from('>H', data, pos)
    pos += 2 + 2 * n_ifaces

    def skip_members(p):
        (n,) = struct.unpack_from('>H', data, p)
        p += 2
        out = []
        for _ in range(n):
            acc, name_i, desc_i, n_attr = struct.unpack_from('>HHHH', data, p)
            p += 8
            for _ in range(n_attr):
                (alen,) = struct.unpack_from('>I', data, p + 2)
                p += 6 + alen
            out.append((acc, pool[name_i], pool[desc_i]))
        return p, out

    pos, _fields = skip_members(pos)
    pos, methods = skip_members(pos)
    cls = pool[pool[this_idx][1]]
    return cls, methods


def short_name(internal):
    return internal.rsplit('/', 1)[-1]


def main(argv):
    roots, excludes, out_json, jacoco = [], [], None, None
    rest = argv[1:]
    while rest:
        a = rest.pop(0)
        if a == '--exclude':
            excludes.append(rest.pop(0))
        elif a == '--json':
            out_json = rest.pop(0)
        elif a == '--jacoco':
            jacoco = rest.pop(0)
        else:
            roots.append(a)
    rows = []
    for root in roots:
      for dirpath, _dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        for fn in files:
            if not fn.endswith('.class'):
                continue
            path = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
            if any(x in path for x in excludes):
                continue
            cls, methods = parse_class(path)
            if any(x in cls for x in excludes):
                continue
            for acc, name, desc in methods:
                if acc & (ACC_ABSTRACT | ACC_NATIVE):
                    continue
                if acc & ACC_BRIDGE or name.startswith('access$'):
                    continue
                if acc & ACC_SYNTHETIC and not name.startswith('lambda$'):
                    continue
                flags = []
                if acc & ACC_STATIC:
                    flags.append('static')
                if acc & ACC_SYNTHETIC:
                    flags.append('synthetic')
                if acc & 0x0002:
                    flags.append('private')
                rows.append({'class': cls, 'short': short_name(cls) + '.' + name,
                             'name': name, 'desc': desc, 'flags': flags})
    if jacoco:
        import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
        rep, rep_classes = set(), set()
        for c in ET.parse(jacoco).getroot().iter('class'):
            rep_classes.add(c.get('name'))
            for m in c.findall('method'):
                rep.add((c.get('name'), m.get('name'), m.get('desc')))
        keep, dropped = [], []
        for r in rows:
            k = (r['class'], r['name'], r['desc'])
            if k in rep or (r['name'] == '<init>' and r['desc'] == '()V' and 'private' in r['flags']
                            and r['class'] in rep_classes):
                keep.append(r)
            else:
                dropped.append(r)
        print('# --jacoco: kept %d, dropped %d not in the report' % (len(keep), len(dropped)), file=sys.stderr)
        for r in dropped:
            print('#   dropped %s%s' % (r['short'], r['desc']), file=sys.stderr)
        rows = keep
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r['class'], r['name'], r['desc']))
    for r in rows:
        print('%s\t%s\t%s' % (r['short'], r['desc'], ','.join(r['flags'])))
    print('# %d methods' % len(rows), file=sys.stderr)
    if out_json:
        with open(out_json, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(rows, f, indent=1)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

FILE	tools/make_test_manifest.py	9103	0925701bc8687911114235e79af2adb77f448ae464d60f48e2392e0a6ec7965c	utf8
"""Build the emulator test manifests: what to test for every app, with the expected outcome for each target.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/make_test_manifest.py
Reads graphs/<App>/out/<prefix>_trigger_graph.json and methods.csv (verdicts, paths, baseline hits) and writes
  emulator_tests/<App>/targets.csv    one row per target method
  emulator_tests/SUMMARY.md           counts per app and category
Categories (expected = what the graph predicts when the emulator is run):
  CONTROL_HIT    the baseline ran it            -> HIT   (checks that the new harness reproduces the baseline)
  FRONTIER       first never-run node behind which many never-run methods sit (where the baseline stopped) -> HIT after crossing it
  CLEAN_SAMPLE   reachable, no setup, no env, never run   -> HIT when the path is followed
  SETUP_SAMPLE   reachable after setup, never run         -> HIT with the setup, NOT_HIT without it
  ENV_ENTRY      edge gated by another app / adb / the system that adb can inject (am start, am broadcast) -> HIT when injected
  ENV_SAMPLE     reachable only through another environment gate (network, picker, root, hardware) -> UNKNOWN (record what happens)
  DEAD_SAMPLE    graph says DEAD / FRAMEWORK_ONLY         -> NOT_HIT (any hit is a contradiction)
Every run also checks ALL dead methods passively (a hit on any of them is a contradiction).
Random samples use a fixed seed, so the manifest is reproducible.
"""
import collections
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
import random
import re

SEED = 20261025
APPS = [('Currency', 'hand'), ('Diary', 'hand'), ('hash-checker', 'hand'), ('openlauncher', 'hand'), ('Omni-Notes', 'hand'),
        ('Vanilla-Music', 'generated'), ('AnyMemo', 'generated'), ('Markor', 'generated'), ('Aegis', 'generated'),
        ('AmazeFileManager', 'generated'), ('AnkiDroid', 'generated'), ('MMEX', 'generated'), ('LibreTorrent', 'generated')]
N_FRONTIER, N_CONTROL, N_CLEAN, N_SETUP, N_ENV, N_DEAD = 10, 10, 20, 10, 10, 20


def base(n):
    return n.split('@')[0]


def frontier_nodes(rows, edges, hit):
    """{first never-run node: (count of never-run clean methods behind it, example chain info, [dependents])}"""
    out = collections.defaultdict(list)
    nodes = set()
    for x in edges:
        out[x['src']].append(x)
        nodes.add(x['src'])
        nodes.add(x['dst'])
    prev, dq = {}, collections.deque()
    for n in sorted(nodes):
        if not n.startswith('S_') and base(n) in hit:
            prev[n] = None
            dq.append(n)
    while dq:
        n = dq.popleft()
        for x in out.get(n, []):
            if x['kind'] == 'REGISTER' or x['dst'] in prev:
                continue
            prev[x['dst']] = x
            dq.append(x['dst'])
    first = {}
    for n in prev:
        first.setdefault(base(n), n)
    res = collections.defaultdict(lambda: {'n': 0, 'dep': [], 'edge': None})
    for r in rows:
        m = r['method']
        if r['verdict'] != 'REACHABLE' or m in hit or r['monkey_hit'] == '' or 'needs environment' in (r['note'] or '') or m not in first:
            continue
        chain, n = [], first[m]
        while prev.get(n):
            chain.append(prev[n])
            n = prev[n]['src']
        chain.reverse()
        k = next((i for i, x in enumerate(chain) if not x['dst'].startswith('S_') and base(x['dst']) not in hit), None)
        if k is None:
            continue
        fx = chain[k]
        key = base(fx['dst'])
        d = res[key]
        d['n'] += 1
        if len(d['dep']) < 5 and m != key:
            d['dep'].append(m)
        d['edge'] = (base(fx['src']), fx['kind'], (fx['evidence'] or '').split(';')[0])
    return res


def main():
    rng = random.Random(SEED)
    os.makedirs('emulator_tests', exist_ok=True)
    summary = []
    for app, how in APPS:
        jp = glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]
        g = json.load(open(jp))
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app)))
        by = {r['method']: r for r in rows}
        hit = {r['method'] for r in rows if r['monkey_hit'] == '1' or r['ape_hit'] == '1'}
        observable = [r for r in rows if r['monkey_hit'] != '']
        targets = []

        def add(cat, m, expected, extra=None):
            r = by[m]
            note = r['note'] or ''
            setup = '; '.join(re.findall(r'needs earlier setup: ([^|]*)', note))
            env = '; '.join(re.findall(r'needs environment: ([^|]*)', note))
            row = {'category': cat, 'method': m, 'verdict': r['verdict'], 'expected': expected,
                   'baseline_hit': 1 if m in hit else 0, 'steps_from_launch': r['steps_from_launch'],
                   'taps': r['gui_steps_on_shortest_path'], 'path': r['shortest_path'], 'setup_needed': setup, 'env_needed': env,
                   'extra': extra or ''}
            targets.append(row)

        # frontier
        fr = frontier_nodes(rows, g['edges'], hit)
        top = sorted(fr.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1]['n'])[:N_FRONTIER]
        used = set()
        for node, d in top:
            if node in by:
                used.add(node)
                add('FRONTIER', node, 'HIT', 'behind it: %d never-run methods; entered from %s by %s (%s); examples: %s' % (
                    d['n'], d['edge'][0], d['edge'][1], d['edge'][2], ', '.join(d['dep'][:4])))
        # controls
        ctl = [r['method'] for r in observable if r['method'] in hit and r['steps_from_launch'] and int(r['steps_from_launch']) >= 3 and r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE']
        for m in rng.sample(ctl, min(N_CONTROL, len(ctl))):
            add('CONTROL_HIT', m, 'HIT')
        # clean never run
        clean = [r['method'] for r in observable if r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE' and r['method'] not in hit and r['method'] not in used and 'needs environment' not in (r['note'] or '')]
        for m in rng.sample(clean, min(N_CLEAN, len(clean))):
            add('CLEAN_SAMPLE', m, 'HIT')
        # setup
        setup = [r['method'] for r in observable if r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE_AFTER_SETUP' and r['method'] not in hit and 'needs environment' not in (r['note'] or '')]
        for m in rng.sample(setup, min(N_SETUP, len(setup))):
            add('SETUP_SAMPLE', m, 'HIT with setup; NOT_HIT without it')
        # env entries that adb can inject
        inj = {}
        for x in g['edges']:
            e = x.get('env') or ''
            if e and x['kind'] in ('ENTRY', 'INTENT', 'FRAMEWORK', 'CALL') and re.search(r'another app|adb|system sends|incoming|share|launch intent|intent', e, re.I) and base(x['dst']) in by:
                inj.setdefault(base(x['dst']), (x['src'], x['kind'], e[:140]))
        inj_list = [m for m in inj if by[m]['verdict'].startswith('REACHABLE')]
        for m in rng.sample(inj_list, min(N_ENV, len(inj_list))):
            add('ENV_ENTRY', m, 'HIT when injected (am start / am broadcast)', 'gate on %s via %s: %s' % inj[m])
        # other env
        envm = [r['method'] for r in observable if r['verdict'].startswith('REACHABLE') and 'needs environment' in (r['note'] or '') and r['method'] not in inj and r['method'] not in hit]
        for m in rng.sample(envm, min(N_ENV, len(envm))):
            add('ENV_SAMPLE', m, 'UNKNOWN (record what happens)')
        # dead
        dead = [r['method'] for r in rows if r['verdict'] in ('DEAD', 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY') and not r['method'].startswith('S_')]
        for m in rng.sample(dead, min(N_DEAD, len(dead))):
            add('DEAD_SAMPLE', m, 'NOT_HIT')
        os.makedirs('emulator_tests/%s' % app, exist_ok=True)
        with open('emulator_tests/%s/targets.csv' % app, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            w = csv.writer(f)
            cols = ['id', 'category', 'method', 'verdict', 'expected', 'baseline_hit', 'steps_from_launch', 'taps', 'setup_needed', 'env_needed', 'extra', 'path']
            w.writerow(cols)
            for i, t in enumerate(targets, 1):
                t['id'] = '%s-%03d' % (app, i)
                w.writerow([t[c] for c in cols])
        cnt = collections.Counter(t['category'] for t in targets)
        summary.append((app, how, len(rows), len(hit), sum(1 for r in rows if r['verdict'] in ('DEAD', 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY')), cnt, len(targets), len(fr)))
    cats = ['CONTROL_HIT', 'FRONTIER', 'CLEAN_SAMPLE', 'SETUP_SAMPLE', 'ENV_ENTRY', 'ENV_SAMPLE', 'DEAD_SAMPLE']
    lines = ['# Emulator test targets (generated by tools/make_test_manifest.py)', '',
             '| App | Methods | Baseline-run | Dead (all checked passively) | ' + ' | '.join(cats) + ' | Targets |',
             '|---|---:|---:|---:|' + '---:|' * len(cats) + '---:|']
    tot = collections.Counter()
    for app, how, n, h, d, cnt, nt, nf in summary:
        lines.append('| %s | %d | %d | %d | %s | %d |' % (app, n, h, d, ' | '.join(str(cnt[c]) for c in cats), nt))
        tot.update(cnt)
    lines.append('| **Total** | | | | %s | %d |' % (' | '.join(str(tot[c]) for c in cats), sum(tot.values())))
    open('emulator_tests/SUMMARY.md', 'w').write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
    print('\n'.join(lines))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

"""Write graphs/<App>/KIRO_WORKLIST.md: one row per distinct evidence claim of a schema 2 build that no source line backs.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/make_worklist.py <App>
Reads graphs/<App>/out/*_trigger_graph.json and out/methods.csv (build first). A claim is an evidence segment (split at ';') that starts with
FW-MEMORY, INFERRED, DYN or UNVERIFIED, on an edge whose evidence is not fully sourced. A segment that is only a tag (no text) is grouped by
the edge trigger. Each row gets an action from the rules below (first match wins; the rules of the app come first):
  S = read the cached framework / app lines and cite them, I = keep the claim but write INFERRED and say what was not read, R = delete a DYN segment.
A row with no rule shows '?'; the tool prints those so a rule can be added here. The list ends with the framework files that are on disk.
"""
import collections
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import build_graph as b  # noqa: E402

ANOTHER_APP = ('Another app does this (launcher, document picker, share sheet, receiving app, print service). Keep it as `INFERRED <what the other app does> '
               '(another app, its source was not read)`; the ENV other_app gate already says so.')
WEBVIEW = 'The WebView engine is native code that is not in the Java sources. Keep it as `INFERRED the WebView engine (Chromium, native code outside the Java sources) <what it does>`.'
LIBCORE = 'Java library code (libcore) is not held here. Keep it as `INFERRED java.io / java.util library behaviour (libcore source not read)`.'
ANDROIDX = ('The app uses the androidx library `%s` (not the android.app class), whose source is not held, and nothing may be downloaded. '
            'Keep the claim as `INFERRED androidx %s <what it does> (androidx source not read)`.')
APP_LINE = 'The text already names app lines. Turn it into `SRC File.java:<lines>`: print the lines (`sed -n` on the app source under final_java_only/<App>/app/), check they show what the claim says, keep a short reason in the text.'

COMMON = [
    (r'rotation triggers the relaunch', 'S', 'Delete this segment and add `SRC AndroidManifest.xml:<lines of the activity elements> (no android:configChanges)`. The relaunch itself is cited by the existing ActivityThread lines.'),
    (r'TextView\.onRestoreInstanceState', 'S', 'core/java/android/widget/TextView.java: find `onRestoreInstanceState`; cite the lines where it calls setText for the saved text.'),
    (r'Chromium|WebView|onPageFinished|WebViewClient', 'I', WEBVIEW),

    (r'libcore|Arrays\.sort|FileOutputStream', 'I', LIBCORE),
    (r'UNVERIFIED', 'I', 'Not checked. Reword as `INFERRED <the open point> (not checked)`.'),
    (r'DYN baseline|DYN ', 'R', 'Delete the DYN segment (tool coverage is not evidence). If the edge then has no support, read the code around it and cite the line, or write `INFERRED` with the reason.'),
]

        (r'no text; trigger: (Back in the foreground|The activity returns to the foreground)', 'S/I', 'S: core/java/android/app/ActivityThread.java (cached) handleResumeActivity -> performResumeActivity -> Activity.performResume -> onResume. I for the fragment part: the androidx FragmentActivity.onResume dispatches resume to the added fragments (`INFERRED androidx FragmentActivity ... (androidx source not read)`).'),
        (r'no text; trigger: Class initialisation', 'I', 'JVM class initialisation on first use is a Java language rule, not in the sources read. Write `INFERRED the JVM runs <clinit> on first use of the class (Java language rule, not read from a source)`.'),

def action_for(app, text):
    for rx, act, hint in BY_APP.get(app, []) + COMMON:
        if re.search(rx, text, re.I):
            return act, hint
    return '?', 'No rule: read the edge, then either cite the framework lines (S) or reword it as INFERRED with the reason (I).'


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    gdir = 'graphs/%s' % app
    jp = glob.glob('%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % gdir)[0]
    prefix = os.path.basename(jp).replace('_trigger_graph.json', '')
    g = json.load(open(jp))
    E = {x['id']: x for x in g['edges']}
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('%s/out/methods.csv' % gdir, encoding='utf-8')))

    claims = collections.OrderedDict()
    for x in g['edges']:
        if b.edge_sourced(x):
            continue
        for seg in (x['evidence'] or '').split(';'):
            seg = seg.strip()
            if not seg or seg.split()[0] not in ('FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED', 'DYN', 'UNVERIFIED'):
                continue
            if seg in ('FW-MEMORY', 'INFERRED', 'DYN', 'UNVERIFIED'):          # a bare tag: the trigger says what it is about
                seg = '%s (no text; trigger: %s)' % (seg, (x['trigger'] or '').strip()[:110])
            claims.setdefault(seg, []).append(x['id'])
    affected = collections.defaultdict(set)
    for r in rows:
        if r['verdict'] != 'REACHABLE':
            continue
        for eid in r['route_edge_ids'].split():
            for seg, ids in claims.items():
                if eid in ids:
                    affected[seg].add(r['method'])
    order = sorted(claims.items(), key=lambda kv: (-len(affected[kv[0]]), -len(kv[1])))

    caches = [c for c in ('fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1', '%s/.plan/fw_src/android-8.1.0_r1' % gdir) if os.path.isdir(c)]
    local = []
    for c in caches:
        for dp, _, fn in os.walk(c):
            for f in fn:
                local.append((c, os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dp, f), c)))
    local.sort(key=lambda t: t[1])

    lines = ['# %s: evidence work list for the schema 2 conversion' % app, '',
             'Generated from the current build (`python3 -I tools/build_graph.py %s %s`). One row per distinct claim in `evidence` that no source line backs: **%d claims on %d edges**. '
             'Edit only `%s/graph_def.py`. After each batch rebuild and run `python3 -I tools/check_citations.py %s`.' % (
                 gdir, prefix, len(claims), len({i for ids in claims.values() for i in ids}), gdir, app), '',
             '**Actions.** `S` = read the framework / app lines and cite them as `FW-SRC <path>:<lines>` or `SRC File.java:<lines>`; `I` = keep the claim but make it start with `INFERRED` and say what is not read; '
             '`R` = remove a `DYN` segment (tool coverage is not evidence); `S/I` = two parts, see the row.',
             'A citation counts only if you printed the lines (`sed -n "<a>,<b>p" <file>`) and they show what the claim says. Never cite from memory.', '',
             '| # | Claim (as in the evidence) | Label | Edges | Methods that depend on it | Action | What to do / where to look | Find it with |', '|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|']
    no_rule = []
    for i, (seg, ids) in enumerate(order, 1):
        label, text = seg.split(None, 1) if ' ' in seg else (seg, '')
        act, hint = action_for(app, seg)
        if act == '?':
            no_rule.append(seg)
        words = re.sub(r'[^\w ]', ' ', text.replace('no text; trigger:', '')).split()[:5]
        find = 'grep -n "%s" %s/graph_def.py' % (' '.join(words), gdir) if words else ''
        ex = E[ids[0]]
        lines.append('| %d | %s | %s | %d (e.g. %s %s -> %s) | %d | **%s** | %s | `%s` |' % (
            i, seg.replace('|', '/')[:190], label, len(ids), ex['kind'], ex['src'], ex['dst'], len(affected[seg]), act, hint, find.replace('|', '/')))

    unre = [(m, l['unreachable']) for m, l in g['labels'].items() if l.get('unreachable')]
    lines += ['', '## UNREACHABLE methods (%d): check each once more' % len(unre), '',
              'For each: grep the call sites in the app source (`grep -rn "<name>" final_java_only/%s/app/`), read the cited lines, and write one `label` row in `verification.csv` (result `correct`, or `fixed` after you changed it). '
              'A reason that has an `INFERRED` part stays INFERRED; do not add a citation you did not print.' % app, '',
              '| Method | Kind | Reason | Reference |', '|---|---|---|---|']
    for m, u in sorted(unre):
        lines.append('| %s | %s | %s | %s |' % (m, u['kind'], u['text'].replace('|', '/')[:400], g['labels'][m]['source_ref']))

    lines += ['', '## Framework files already on disk (use only these; do not download anything)', '']
    for c, rel in local:
        lines.append('- `%s/%s`' % (c, rel))
    open('%s/KIRO_WORKLIST.md' % gdir, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write('\n'.join(lines) + '\n')
    print('%s: %d claims; %d without a rule; %d UNREACHABLE rows' % (app, len(claims), len(no_rule), len(unre)))
    for s in no_rule:
        print('  NO RULE:', s[:150])


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

#!/bin/bash
# Write the stub graph_def.py of a generated app (does not overwrite an existing one).
# Usage (from work/): tools/new_graph_def.sh <App> "<Title>"
APP="$1"; TITLE="$2"
F="graphs/$APP/graph_def.py"

cat > "$F" <<PY
# $TITLE
# Generated part: tools/gen_edges.py derives entry points, lifecycle, callbacks, lambdas, intents, XML-instantiated classes
# from the bytecode, AndroidManifest.xml and res/ XML (see its docstring). Direct app->app calls come from
# tools/extract_calls.py. Everything below gen_edges.apply() is hand-written refinement for this app.
# Rebuild: python3 -I tools/build_graph.py graphs/$APP <prefix>

TITLE = '$TITLE'
USE_AUTO = True
PREFER_SETUP_OVER_ENV = True
AUTO_DEAD = ('Generated graph: no route found. Nothing in the app calls it and no entry point (manifest component, lifecycle or '
             'UI callback of a constructed class, lambda, XML reference, class literal, intent action) leads to it. '
             'Probably unused code; entries through reflection, JNI or code generators are not modelled.')

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, TOOLS)
import gen_edges

CFG = {
    'GROUPS': [],           # [(regex on node name, 'needs'|'env'|'guard', text)]
    'STATIC_ADAPTERS': [],  # adapters whose rows exist without user data
}

gen_edges.apply(globals(), GDIR, '$APP', CFG)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ hand-written refinement
PY
echo "wrote $F"

#!/bin/bash

(k,v),=d.items()
print('\n'.join(os.path.join(b,p) for p in v['classfiles']))

FILE	tools/publish_graphs.py	7736	21c846226f6a36900a741d97f7bb3298572b89223cda5b59b39a8f9828b37d23	utf8
"""Publish the finished schema 2 work to the branch `graphs` of the public repo bugfixops/work-emulator (a snapshot, not the emulator runs).

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/publish_graphs.py [--push] [--workdir <folder>]   (default folder: work/publish_tmp, not copied anywhere)
- takes every app that is ticked "- [x]" in handoff/PROGRESS_V2.md (graph outputs, graph_def.py, verification.csv, readme_notes.md, AGENT_NOTES.md, LESSONS.md);
- adds tools/, the schema, the brief, the recipe, the progress file and the worked examples; leaves out the app sources, backups and draft files;
- scrubs local paths and personal names, then refuses to continue if a personal string is still in the copy;
- clones the repo into <workdir>/clone, replaces the content of branch `graphs` with the copy (history is kept: one new commit), commits with the
  neutral identity bugfixops and pushes only with --push. Never touches `main` or `results`.
"""
import argparse
import glob
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

WORK = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
REPO = 'https://github.com/bugfixops/work-emulator.git'
# names are matched exactly, suffixes and prefixes on the file or folder name
TOOLS_SKIP = {'__pycache__', 'auto_resume.sh', 'resume_after_reset.sh', 'rd.sh', 'publish_graphs.py', 'calibration.json'}
TOOLS_SKIP_SUFFIX = ('_backup.py', '_backup.html', '.pyc', '.log')
APP_SKIP = {'__pycache__', '.plan', 'generated_draft_graph_def.py.txt', 'KIRO_WORKLIST.md'}
APP_SKIP_SUFFIX = ('.pyc', '.log')
HANDOFF_FILES = ('SCHEMA_V2.md', 'CONVERSION_RECIPE.md', 'AGENT_BRIEF_V2.md', 'PROGRESS_V2.md', 'HANDOFF_V2.md')
LEAK = re.compile(r'(darshan|khanal|dibya|dibae|[a-z0-9]+@gmail|gmail\.com|uc\.edu|/Users/|claude-501|d045584d)', re.I)
ALLOWED = ('smlnskgmail',)          # a Java package name of hash-checker, not a personal address
SCRUB = [
    (re.compile(r'/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-darshan-Desktop-yang/d045584d-1ca6-4e30-988a-f2f0a8d6b500'), '<scratchpad>'),
    (re.compile(r'/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang'), '<root>'),
    (re.compile(r'-Users-darshan-Desktop-yang'), '<project>'),
    (re.compile(r'\bDarshan\b'), 'the student'),
    (re.compile(r'\bdarshan\b'), 'user'),
    (re.compile(r'\bYang\b'), 'the reviewer'),
    (re.compile(r'\bKiro\b'), 'a helper agent'),
]


def progress_done():
    lines = open(os.path.join(WORK, 'handoff', 'PROGRESS_V2.md'), encoding='utf-8').read().split('\n')
    return [l[6:].split()[0] for l in lines if l.startswith('- [x] ')]


def copy_tree(src, dst, names, suffixes, prefixes=()):
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(src):
        dn[:] = [d for d in dn if d not in names and not d.endswith(suffixes) and not d.startswith(prefixes or ('\0',))]
        for f in fn:
            if f in names or f.endswith(suffixes) or f.startswith(prefixes or ('\0',)):
                continue
            s_ = os.path.join(dp, f)
            d_ = os.path.join(dst, os.path.relpath(s_, src))
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(d_), exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(s_, d_)


def scrub_tree(root):
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root):
        for f in fn:
            p = os.path.join(dp, f)
            if not f.endswith(('.py', '.md', '.csv', '.json', '.sh', '.html', '.txt')):
                continue
            try:
                s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                continue
            t = s
            for rx, rep in SCRUB:
                t = rx.sub(rep, t)
            if t != s:
                open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(t)


def leaks(root):
    out = []
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root):
        for f in fn:
            p = os.path.join(dp, f)
            try:
                s = open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
            except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
                continue
            for m in LEAK.finditer(s):
                line = s[s.rfind('\n', 0, m.start()) + 1: s.find('\n', m.end()) if s.find('\n', m.end()) >= 0 else len(s)]
                if any(a in line for a in ALLOWED):
                    continue
                out.append('%s: %s' % (os.path.relpath(p, root), line.strip()[:160]))
                break
    return out


def run(cmd, cwd=None):
    r = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True)
    if r.returncode != 0:
        sys.exit('command failed: %s\n%s' % (' '.join(cmd), r.stderr or r.stdout))
    return r.stdout.strip()


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--push', action='store_true')
    ap.add_argument('--workdir', default=None)
    ap.add_argument('--message', default='Update the schema 2 ground-truth graphs (snapshot of the finished apps)')
    a = ap.parse_args()
    apps = progress_done()
    if not apps:
        sys.exit('no app is ticked in handoff/PROGRESS_V2.md')
    tmp = a.workdir or os.path.join(WORK, 'publish_tmp')
    os.makedirs(tmp, exist_ok=True)
    export = os.path.join(tmp, 'export')
    shutil.rmtree(export, ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(export)
    for app in apps:
        src = os.path.join(WORK, 'graphs', app)
        if not os.path.isdir(src):
            sys.exit('missing folder for a ticked app: %s' % app)
        copy_tree(src, os.path.join(export, 'graphs', app), APP_SKIP, APP_SKIP_SUFFIX)
    copy_tree(os.path.join(WORK, 'tools'), os.path.join(export, 'tools'), TOOLS_SKIP, TOOLS_SKIP_SUFFIX, prefixes=('_t_',))
    for f in HANDOFF_FILES:
        p = os.path.join(WORK, 'handoff', f)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            os.makedirs(os.path.join(export, 'handoff'), exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copy2(p, os.path.join(export, 'handoff', f))
    copy_tree(os.path.join(WORK, 'handoff', 'examples'), os.path.join(export, 'handoff', 'examples'), {'__pycache__'}, ('.pyc',))
    scrub_tree(export)
    bad = leaks(export)
    if bad:
        print('personal strings left, nothing pushed:')
        print('\n'.join(bad[:40]))
        sys.exit(1)
    clone = os.path.join(tmp, 'clone')
    shutil.rmtree(clone, ignore_errors=True)
    run(['git', 'clone', '--quiet', REPO, clone])
    branches = run(['git', 'branch', '-r'], cwd=clone)
    if 'origin/graphs' in branches:
        run(['git', 'checkout', '--quiet', '-B', 'graphs', 'origin/graphs'], cwd=clone)
    else:
        run(['git', 'checkout', '--quiet', '--orphan', 'graphs'], cwd=clone)
    for entry in os.listdir(clone):
        if entry == '.git':
            continue
        p = os.path.join(clone, entry)
        shutil.rmtree(p) if os.path.isdir(p) and not os.path.islink(p) else os.remove(p)
    for entry in os.listdir(export):
        s = os.path.join(export, entry)
        d = os.path.join(clone, entry)
        shutil.copytree(s, d) if os.path.isdir(s) else shutil.copy2(s, d)
    run(['git', 'add', '-A'], cwd=clone)
    if not run(['git', 'status', '--porcelain'], cwd=clone):
        print('nothing changed since the last publish')
        return
    msg = a.message + '\n\nApps in this snapshot: ' + ', '.join(apps) + '\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Haiku 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>\n'
    mf = os.path.join(tmp, 'msg.txt')
    open(mf, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(msg)
    run(['git', '-c', 'user.name=bugfixops', '-c', 'user.email=bugfixops@users.noreply.github.com', 'commit', '-q', '-F', mf], cwd=clone)
    head = run(['git', 'rev-parse', '--short', 'HEAD'], cwd=clone)
    print('commit %s on branch graphs with %d apps: %s' % (head, len(apps), ', '.join(apps)))
    if a.push:
        run(['git', 'push', '--quiet', 'origin', 'graphs'], cwd=clone)
        print('pushed graphs to %s' % REPO)
    else:
        print('dry run: not pushed (clone and export are in %s)' % tmp)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

#!/bin/bash
# compact source view: strip imports/comments/blank/brace-only lines, trim long lines
for f in "$@"; do echo "=================== $f"; cat -n "$f" | grep -v -E '^\s+[0-9]+\s+(import |package |\s*$|\s*//|\s*\*|\s*/\*|\s*@Override\s*$|\s*[{}]+\s*$|\s*\}\);?\s*$|\s*\}\s*else\s*\{\s*$)' | cut -c1-165; done

FILE	tools/resume_convert.sh	2555	28419ba4a02aa6e754df3fcb3cc74a49ad1b4c484fa9f3d61bdcc6d1a84cb030	utf8
#!/bin/bash
# Overnight convert run for AnkiDroid, MMEX and LibreTorrent (prompt: handoff/RESUME_PROMPT_CONVERT.md).
# Starts a headless Claude Code run and starts another one while handoff/PROGRESS_V2.md still has a "- [C]" app.
# It does not wait for the Aegis run and does not use its stop file.
# Stop: touch /Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/work/handoff/STOP_CONVERT (the run that is going keeps going until it ends).
ROOT=/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang
H=$ROOT/work/handoff
LOCK=$H/WORKING_LOCK
LOG=$H/resume_convert.log
CLAUDE=${CLAUDE:-claude}
MAX_RUN=${MAX_RUN:-172800}
RUN_PATTERN="claude -p You are continuing the convert job"
echo $$ > "$H/resume_convert.pid"
log() { echo "$(date '+%F %T') $*" >> "$LOG"; }
todo() { grep -c '^- \[C\]' "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null; }
model() { tr -d ' \n\r\t' < "$H/MODEL_V2" 2>/dev/null || echo claude-haiku-5-5; }
strikes=0
log "convert runner started (pid $$)"
while true; do
  [ -e "$H/STOP_CONVERT" ] && { log "stop file found"; exit 0; }
  n=$(todo)
  [ "${n:-0}" -eq 0 ] && { log "no [C] app left: done"; exit 0; }
  if pgrep -f "$RUN_PATTERN" >/dev/null 2>&1; then sleep 60; continue; fi
  m=$(model)
  out="$H/resume_convert_run_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).log"
  before=$(md5 -q "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null)
  log "run: $n app(s) left, model $m, output $out"
  t0=$(date +%s)
  ( cd "$ROOT/work" && touch "$LOCK" && export CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS=0 && caffeinate -i "$CLAUDE" -p "$(cat "$H/RESUME_PROMPT_CONVERT.md")" --permission-mode auto --model "$m" --output-format json < /dev/null > "$out" 2>&1 ) &
  pid=$!
  while kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; do
    if [ $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) -gt "$MAX_RUN" ]; then
      log "run longer than $MAX_RUN s: stopping it"
      pkill -P "$pid" 2>/dev/null; kill "$pid" 2>/dev/null
      break
    fi
    sleep 60
  done
  wait "$pid" 2>/dev/null; rc=$?
  dt=$(( $(date +%s) - t0 ))
  rm -f "$LOCK"
  after=$(md5 -q "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null)
  log "run ended rc=$rc after ${dt}s, progress file changed: $([ "$before" != "$after" ] && echo yes || echo no)"
  if grep -qiE 'usage limit|session limit|limit reached|hit your|resets [0-9]|rate limit|try again later|quota' "$out" 2>/dev/null; then
    log "limit message in the output: waiting 20 minutes"; sleep 1200; continue
  fi
  if [ "$before" = "$after" ] && [ "$dt" -lt 300 ]; then
    strikes=$((strikes + 1))
    [ "$strikes" -ge 3 ] && { log "3 quick runs without progress: giving up (check the last output for an error)"; exit 1; }
    sleep 600
  else
    strikes=0
    sleep 60
  fi
done

FILE	tools/resume_v5.sh	4556	75eca8d8070ad2b6b15242cf7998f782e3f75ea7569419eab9de380fbaed0ebd	utf8
#!/bin/bash
# Keeps the schema 2 work going (version 5: records the model each run really used). Starts a fresh headless Claude Code session (prompt: handoff/RESUME_PROMPT_V2.md) while handoff/PROGRESS_V2.md
# still has an unchecked app. The model is read from handoff/MODEL_V2 at the start of every run (claude-haiku-5-5 or claude-sonnet-5-5; the aliases
# "haiku" and "sonnet" are NOT used: in this Claude Code version "haiku" means Haiku 4.5).
# A run lasts until it has finished, including the background helpers it starts (CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS=0). It is stopped after MAX_RUN seconds.
# A usage-limit message is waited out (20 minutes, then a new try).
# Before a run starts it waits (a) while another headless run of this job is still going (RUN_PATTERN), and (b) while there was activity after the end of
# our own last run: an interactive session or helper that touched handoff/WORKING_LOCK in the last 30 minutes, or a Claude transcript of this project
# written in the last 30 minutes.
# Start:  DEADLINE_HOURS=240 nohup caffeinate -i /Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/work/tools/resume_v4.sh <start_epoch_seconds> >/dev/null 2>&1 &
# Cancel: touch /Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/work/handoff/STOP_RESUME_V2   (or kill the pid in handoff/resume_v2.pid)
ROOT=/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang
H=${H:-$ROOT/work/handoff}
START=${1:-0}
DEADLINE=$((START + ${DEADLINE_HOURS:-48} * 3600))
MAX_RUN=${MAX_RUN:-172800}
LOG=$H/resume_v2.log
LOCK=$H/WORKING_LOCK
TDIR=${TDIR:-$HOME/.claude/projects/-Users-darshan-Desktop-yang}
CLAUDE=${CLAUDE:-claude}
TOOLS=${TOOLS:-/Users/darshan/Desktop/yang/work/tools}
RUN_PATTERN=${RUN_PATTERN:-claude -p You are continuing an unattended job}
echo $$ > "$H/resume_v2.pid"
log() { echo "$(date '+%F %T') $*" >> "$LOG"; }
todo() { grep -c '^- \[ \]' "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null; }
mtime() { stat -f %m "$1" 2>/dev/null || echo 0; }
newest() { find "$TDIR" -name '*.jsonl' -exec stat -f %m {} + 2>/dev/null | sort -n | tail -1; }
model() { tr -d ' \n\r\t' < "$H/MODEL_V2" 2>/dev/null || echo claude-sonnet-5-5; }
last_run_end=$START
busy() {
  local now last lk
  now=$(date +%s)
  last=$(newest); last=${last:-0}
  [ "$last" -gt "$last_run_end" ] && [ $((now - last)) -lt 1800 ] && return 0
  lk=$(mtime "$LOCK")
  [ "$lk" -gt "$last_run_end" ] && [ $((now - lk)) -lt 1800 ] && return 0
  return 1
}
log "watcher v5 started (pid $$), start epoch $START ($(date -r "$START" '+%F %T' 2>/dev/null)), deadline in ${DEADLINE_HOURS:-48} h"
while [ "$(date +%s)" -lt "$START" ]; do
  [ -e "$H/STOP_RESUME_V2" ] && { log "stop file found while waiting"; exit 0; }
  sleep 30
done
while pgrep -f "$RUN_PATTERN" >/dev/null 2>&1; do sleep 60; done
last_run_end=$(date +%s)
runs=0; strikes=0
while [ "$(date +%s)" -lt "$DEADLINE" ]; do
  [ -e "$H/STOP_RESUME_V2" ] && { log "stop file found"; exit 0; }
  n=$(todo)
  [ "${n:-1}" -eq 0 ] && { log "no unchecked app left: done"; exit 0; }
  if pgrep -f "$RUN_PATTERN" >/dev/null 2>&1; then sleep 60; continue; fi
  if busy; then sleep 120; continue; fi
  runs=$((runs + 1))
  m=$(model)
  out="$H/resume_v2_run_$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S).log"
  before=$(md5 -q "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null)
  log "run $runs: $n app(s) left, model $m, starting headless claude -> $out"
  t0=$(date +%s)
  ( cd "$ROOT" && touch "$LOCK" && export CLAUDE_CODE_PRINT_BG_WAIT_CEILING_MS=0 && "$CLAUDE" -p "$(cat "$H/RESUME_PROMPT_V2.md")" --permission-mode auto --model "$m" --output-format json < /dev/null > "$out" 2>&1 ) &
  pid=$!
  while kill -0 "$pid" 2>/dev/null; do
    if [ $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) -gt "$MAX_RUN" ]; then
      log "run $runs longer than $MAX_RUN s: stopping it"
      pkill -P "$pid" 2>/dev/null; kill "$pid" 2>/dev/null
      break
    fi
    sleep 60
  done
  wait "$pid" 2>/dev/null; rc=$?
  models=$(python3 -I "$TOOLS/run_models.py" "$out" 2>/dev/null)
  dt=$(( $(date +%s) - t0 ))
  last_run_end=$(date +%s)
  rm -f "$LOCK"
  after=$(md5 -q "$H/PROGRESS_V2.md" 2>/dev/null)
  log "run $runs ended rc=$rc after ${dt}s, progress file changed: $([ "$before" != "$after" ] && echo yes || echo no); $models"
  if grep -qiE 'usage limit|session limit|limit reached|hit your|resets [0-9]|rate limit|try again later|quota' "$out"; then
    log "limit message in the output: waiting 20 minutes"; sleep 1200; continue
  fi
  if [ "$before" = "$after" ] && [ "$dt" -lt 300 ]; then
    strikes=$((strikes + 1))
    [ "$strikes" -ge 3 ] && { log "3 quick runs without progress: giving up"; exit 1; }
    sleep 600
  else
    strikes=0
    sleep 60
  fi

FILE	tools/run_models.py	1042	bb5e115f729658dc085d4867cfb0d540863cc484fb611785a6b80c38b99dba76	utf8
"""Print the model that a headless `claude -p --output-format json` run used for its main work, read from the JSON that the run prints last.
Usage: python3 -I tools/run_models.py <run output file>
The run also makes small calls of its own (for example on Haiku 4.5); they are listed as 'other calls'."""
import json, sys


def summary(path):
    lines = [l for l in open(path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n') if l.startswith('{') and '"modelUsage"' in l]
    if not lines:
        return 'no model usage in the output'
    d = json.loads(lines[-1])
    mu = d.get('modelUsage') or {}
    if not mu:
        return 'no model usage in the output'

    def tot(v):
        return sum(v.get(k, 0) for k in ('inputTokens', 'outputTokens', 'cacheReadInputTokens', 'cacheCreationInputTokens'))
    main = max(mu, key=lambda m: tot(mu[m]))
    side = sorted(m for m in mu if m != main)
    return 'main model %s; other calls: %s' % (main, ', '.join(side) or 'none')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(summary(sys.argv[1]))

FILE	tools/scan_unreachable.py	5716	0d000f5e7648f26a799488477118e9f51d99867a5c017f6a36f7798ed4dccec6	utf8
"""List, for every UNREACHABLE method of a schema 2 build, where it is declared and where its name is still used in the app sources.

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/scan_unreachable.py <App> [--all] [--refs] [--callers]
Reads graphs/<App>/out/*_trigger_graph.json. Searches the .java and .xml files of final_java_only/<App>/app/*/ (generated and build folders skipped).
For a method: the name (a constructor is searched as `new Class(` / `Class(`), the declaring file and line (printed as the reference), and every other line
that mentions the name, comments skipped. Methods with no other mention are printed in one line at the end ("no other mention"): those are the
no-caller cases; the others are listed with up to 4 hits so a person can decide if the hit is a real caller (a call, an XML android:onClick,
a reflection string) or only another method of the same name. --refs prints a python dict {method: 'File.java:line'} for use as source_ref.
--callers instead prints the UNREACHABLE methods that still have a bytecode call edge from a method outside the unreachable set (the callers a SUPPRESS may have hidden); each one must be explained in its reason.
It is a triage tool: a hit is not proof of a caller, and no hit does not rule out reflection by a string built at run time.
"""
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

SRC_ROOT = 'final_java_only'


def sources(app):
    out = {}
    for root in glob.glob('%s/%s/app/*/' % (SRC_ROOT, app)):
        for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root):
            dn[:] = [d for d in dn if d not in ('build', 'androidTest', 'test', '.git', 'JacocoInstrument')]
            for f in fn:
                if f.endswith(('.java', '.xml', '.kt')):
                    p = os.path.join(dp, f)
                    try:
                        out[p] = open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
                    except OSError:
                        pass
    return out


def simple(m):
    base = m.split('@')[0]
    cls, _, meth = base.rpartition('.')
    meth = meth.split('(')[0]
    outer = cls.split('$')[0]
    return cls, outer, meth


def is_comment(line):
    s = line.strip()
    return s.startswith(('//', '*', '/*', '<!--'))


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    if '--callers' in argv:
        g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
        unre = {m for m, l in g['labels'].items() if l.get('unreachable')}
        if not os.path.exists('graphs/%s/auto_calls.json' % app):
            print('graphs/%s/auto_calls.json does not exist: no bytecode edges to compare' % app)
            return
        auto = json.load(open('graphs/%s/auto_calls.json' % app))
        live = {}
        for c in auto['calls']:
            if c['dst'] in unre and c['src'] not in unre:
                live.setdefault(c['dst'], []).append('%s (%s:%s)' % (c['src'], c['file'], c['line']))
        for m in sorted(live):
            print('%s [%s] <- %s' % (m, g['labels'][m]['unreachable']['kind'], '; '.join(live[m][:3])))
        print('%d of %d unreachable methods have a bytecode caller outside the set' % (len(live), len(unre)))
        return
    g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
    files = sources(app)
    by_name = {}
    for p in files:
        by_name.setdefault(os.path.basename(p), []).append(p)
    refs, quiet, shown = {}, [], 0
    for m, l in sorted(g['labels'].items()):
        u = l.get('unreachable')
        if not u:
            continue
        cls, outer, meth = simple(m)
        inner = cls.split('$')[-1]
        ctor = meth == '<init>'
        name = inner if ctor else meth
        if meth == '<clinit>':
            name = inner
        decl_files = by_name.get(outer + '.java', []) + by_name.get(outer + '.kt', [])
        rx = re.compile(r'\bnew\s+%s\s*\(|\b%s\s*\(' % (re.escape(inner), re.escape(inner))) if ctor else re.compile(r'\b%s\b' % re.escape(name))
        decl = None
        hits = []
        for p, lines in files.items():
            for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
                if is_comment(line) or not rx.search(line):
                    continue
                is_decl = p in decl_files and decl is None and (
                    re.search(r'(public|private|protected|static|void|int|boolean|String|class)\b.*\b%s\b' % re.escape(name), line) and '(' in line and not re.search(r'\bnew\b|return |=\s*[\w.]*%s\(' % re.escape(name), line)) if not ctor else None
                if ctor and p in decl_files and decl is None and re.search(r'\b(class|enum)\s+%s\b|\b%s\s*\([^)]*\)\s*(throws [\w, ]+)?\s*\{' % (re.escape(inner), re.escape(inner)), line):
                    is_decl = True
                if is_decl:
                    decl = '%s:%d' % (os.path.basename(p), i)
                    continue
                hits.append('%s:%d %s' % (os.path.relpath(p, '%s/%s/app' % (SRC_ROOT, app)).split('/src/')[-1] if '--full' in argv else os.path.basename(p), i, line.strip()[:70]))
        refs[m] = decl or (decl_files and os.path.basename(decl_files[0]) or '?')
        if not hits and '--all' not in argv:
            quiet.append(m)
            continue
        shown += 1
        print('%s [%s] %s: %s' % (m, u['kind'], refs[m], 'no other mention' if not hits else '%d other mentions' % len(hits)))
        for h in hits[:4]:
            print('      ' + h)
    print('\n%d methods with other mentions shown; %d with no other mention: %s' % (shown, len(quiet), ', '.join(quiet)[:3000]))
    if '--refs' in argv:
        print('REFS = {')
        for m, r in sorted(refs.items()):
            print('    %r: %r,' % (m, r))
        print('}')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

FILE	tools/show_route.py	5314	b91c043a309caea6b106a000c7779bd724dd67485a579f84110866d5e1d6ce12	utf8
"""Print the best route of methods with the source lines every step cites (to read a path against the source).

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/show_route.py <App> <method> [<method> ...] [--max N] [--dedupe]
Needs a schema 2 build (graphs/<App>/out/methods.csv with route_edge_ids). For each method: the label, then every edge of the route
with kind, class, gates, guard and the lines of its evidence (`SRC File.java:12-30`) taken from the app's source tree.
--max N: print at most N lines per snippet (default 14). --dedupe: an edge already printed for an earlier method is listed in one line.
"""
import csv
import glob
import json
import os
import re
import sys

SRC_ROOT = 'final_java_only'
FLAVORS = ()


def source_index(app):
    idx = {}
    for root in glob.glob('%s/%s/app/*/' % (SRC_ROOT, app)):
        for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root):
            dn[:] = [d for d in dn if d not in ('build', 'test', 'androidTest', '.git')]
            if 'JacocoInstrument' in dp:
                continue
            for f in fn:
                if f.endswith(('.java', '.xml', '.kt')):
                    idx.setdefault(f, []).append(os.path.join(dp, f))
    return idx


def built_flavors(app):
    """Flavors the dataset APK was built from: the folders app/build/intermediates/javac/<flavor>Debug of the source tree."""
    out = set()
    for d in glob.glob('%s/%s/app/*/app/build/intermediates/javac/*Debug' % (SRC_ROOT, app)):
        out.add(os.path.basename(d)[:-len('Debug')])
    return out


def find(idx, name, flavors=()):
    cands = idx.get(os.path.basename(name), [])
    if '/' in name:
        tail = [c for c in cands if c.endswith(name)]
        cands = tail or cands
    # the same file name can exist in several flavor folders: take src/main or the flavor that was built, never another flavor
    cands = sorted(cands, key=lambda c: (not ('/src/main/' in c or any('/src/%s/' % f in c for f in flavors)), len(c)))
    return cands[0] if cands else None


def snippet(idx, ref, cap, flavors=()):
    """ref: 'File.java:12-30,45' -> printed lines"""
    m = re.match(r'(.+?):([0-9][0-9,\-]*)$', ref)
    if not m:
        return ['      (%s: no line numbers)' % ref]
    path = find(idx, m.group(1), flavors)
    if not path:
        return ['      (%s not found in the source tree)' % m.group(1)]
    lines = open(path, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
    out = []
    for seg in m.group(2).split(','):
        a, _, b = seg.partition('-')
        a, b = int(a), int(b or a)
        for n in range(a, min(b, a + cap - 1) + 1):
            if 1 <= n <= len(lines):
                out.append('      %5d  %s' % (n, lines[n - 1].rstrip()[:170]))
        if b - a + 1 > cap:
            out.append('      ...  (%d more lines)' % (b - a + 1 - cap))
    return out


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    global FLAVORS
    FLAVORS = built_flavors(app)
    cap = int(argv[argv.index('--max') + 1]) if '--max' in argv else 14
    dedupe = '--dedupe' in argv
    names = [a for i, a in enumerate(argv[2:], 2) if a not in ('--max', '--dedupe') and argv[i - 1] != '--max']
    seen = set()
    jp = glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]
    g = json.load(open(jp))
    edges = {x['id']: x for x in g['edges']}
    rows = {r['method']: r for r in csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app, encoding='utf-8'))}
    idx = source_index(app)
    for m in names:
        r = rows.get(m)
        if not r:
            print('## %s: no such method' % m)
            continue
        lab = g['labels'][m]
        print('## %s  %s  confirmed_by=%s path=%s' % (m, r['verdict'], lab['confirmed_by'], lab['path_status']))
        if r['verdict'] == 'UNREACHABLE':
            print('   %s: %s  [%s]' % (lab['unreachable']['kind'], lab['unreachable']['text'], lab['source_ref']))
            continue
        print('   env=%s state=%s classes=%s explicit_standard_route=%s' % (r['env_kinds'] or '-', r['state_kinds'] or '-', r['route_edge_classes'], r['explicit_standard_route']))
        for i, eid in enumerate(r['route_edge_ids'].split(), 1):
            x = edges[eid]
            if dedupe and eid in seen:
                print('  %2d. %s %s -> %s  (printed above)' % (i, eid, x['src'], x['dst']))
                continue
            seen.add(eid)
            gates = ''.join(' [ENV: %s]' % x['env'] for _ in [0] if x.get('env')) + ''.join(' [STATE: %s]' % x['needs_state'] for _ in [0] if x.get('needs_state'))
            print('  %2d. %s %s/%s%s%s\n      %s -> %s\n      %s' % (i, eid, x['kind'], x['edge_class'] + ('(' + x['via'] + ')' if x.get('via') else '') + ('*' if x.get('class_basis') == 'default' else ''),
                                                                 '', gates, x['src'], x['dst'], x['trigger']))
            if x.get('guard'):
                print('      guard: %s' % x['guard'])
            if x.get('note'):
                print('      note: %s' % x['note'])
            print('      evidence: %s' % x['evidence'])
            for seg in re.findall(r'(?:FW-)?SRC\s+([^\s;]+(?:\s*\([^)]*\))?)', x['evidence'] or ''):
                ref = seg.split(' (')[0]
                for line in snippet(idx, ref, cap, FLAVORS):
                    print(line)
        print()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

        app, how, len(rows), len(g['screens']), len(g['edges']), v['REACHABLE'], v['REACHABLE_AFTER_SETUP'], v['DEAD'],
        v['FRAMEWORK_ONLY'], sum(1 for r in obs if hit(r)), ud, ue, us, uc, contra))
    tot.update({'m': len(rows), 'e': len(g['edges'])})
print('\nTotal: %d methods, %d edges in 13 graphs.' % (tot['m'], tot['e']))

"""Reachable-without-setup methods that neither APE nor Monkey ran, grouped by class (the paper's target list).

FILE	tools/verify_sample.py	2373	2a17ad40b9d7026725606a2d225ff35af9cec29db283351e1d5056ad3aa9acc3	utf8
"""Pick a stratified sample of REACHABLE methods whose best routes are to be verified against the source (SCHEMA_V2.md, section 4).

Usage (from work/): python3 -I tools/verify_sample.py <App> [N] [seed]
Strata (a method may belong to several): ENV gate, STATE gate, a non_standard edge on the route, a label confirmed only by inference,
a long route (12 or more steps), and the rest (PLAIN). Each stratum gets a quota; inside a stratum the method that adds the most edges
not yet covered by the sample comes first, ties are broken by the seed, so the sample is reproducible and spreads over many edges.
Prints one line per picked method: stratum, steps, number of new edges.
"""
import csv
import random
import sys

QUOTA = [('NONSTD', 4), ('INFERRED', 4), ('STATE', 3), ('ENV', 6), ('LONG', 6), ('PLAIN', 7)]


def strata(r):
    s = []
    if r['env_kinds']:
        s.append('ENV')
    if r['state_kinds']:
        s.append('STATE')
    if 'non_standard' in r['route_edge_classes']:
        s.append('NONSTD')
    if r['confirmed_by'] == 'INFERRED':
        s.append('INFERRED')
    if int(r['steps_from_launch'] or 0) >= 12:
        s.append('LONG')
    return s or ['PLAIN']


def main(argv):
    app = argv[1]
    n = int(argv[2]) if len(argv) > 2 else 30
    seed = int(argv[3]) if len(argv) > 3 else 20261025
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    rows = [r for r in csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app, encoding='utf-8')) if r['verdict'] == 'REACHABLE']
    rng.shuffle(rows)
    total = sum(q for _, q in QUOTA)
    picked, covered = [], set()
    for name, q in QUOTA:
        q = max(1, round(q * n / total))
        pool = [r for r in rows if name in strata(r) and r not in [p[0] for p in picked]]
        for _ in range(q):
            if not pool:
                break
            pool.sort(key=lambda r: -len(set(r['route_edge_ids'].split()) - covered))
            r = pool.pop(0)
            new = set(r['route_edge_ids'].split()) - covered
            covered |= new
            picked.append((r, name, len(new)))
    print('# %s: %d of %d reachable methods, %d distinct edges on their routes (seed %d)' % (app, len(picked), len(rows), len(covered), seed))
    for r, name, new in picked:
        print('%-9s steps %-3s new edges %-3d %s' % (name, r['steps_from_launch'], new, r['method']))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main(sys.argv)

<header>
  <h1 id="title">Trigger graph</h1>
  <nav><button id="t-map">Page map</button><button id="t-page">Page → next pages</button><button id="t-meth">Method → trigger path</button></nav>
  <label class="opt"><input type="checkbox" id="setup"> Include "needs prior state" edges</label>
