<script>
"use strict";
const G = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('data').textContent);
const BASE = n => n.split('@')[0];
const isScreen = n => n.startsWith('S_');
const out = {}, inn = {};
G.edges.forEach(e => { (out[e.src] = out[e.src] || []).push(e); (inn[BASE(e.dst)] = inn[BASE(e.dst)] || []).push(e); });
const isNoise = p => p === 'S_EXIT' || /_PAUSED$/.test(p);   // pseudo pages: paused/background and exit
const firstPage = Object.keys(G.screens).map(BASE).find(p => p !== 'S_ENTRY' && !isNoise(p)) || 'S_ENTRY';
const state = {tab: 'map', page: firstPage, meth: G.methods[0], setup: false, bg: false, q: ''};

function el(tag, props, ...kids) {
  const e = document.createElement(tag);
  for (const k in (props || {})) {
    if (k === 'class') e.className = props[k]; else if (k === 'text') e.textContent = props[k];
    else if (k.startsWith('on')) e.addEventListener(k.slice(2), props[k]); else e.setAttribute(k, props[k]);
  }
  kids.flat().forEach(c => { if (c != null) e.append(c.nodeType ? c : document.createTextNode(c)); });
  return e;
}
const svgel = (tag, props, ...kids) => {
  const e = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', tag);
  for (const k in (props || {})) { if (k.startsWith('on')) e.addEventListener(k.slice(2), props[k]); else e.setAttribute(k, props[k]); }
  kids.flat().forEach(c => { if (c != null) e.append(c.nodeType ? c : document.createTextNode(c)); });
  return e;
};

// ---------- graph search -------------------------------------------------------------------
function bfs() {
  const prev = {'S_ENTRY': null}, q = ['S_ENTRY'];
  while (q.length) {
    const n = q.shift();
    for (const e of out[n] || []) {
      if (e.kind === 'REGISTER' || e.src === e.dst || e.dst in prev) continue;
      if (e.needs_state && !state.setup) continue;
      prev[e.dst] = e; q.push(e.dst);
    }
  }
  return prev;
}
function pathTo(n, prev) { const p = []; while (prev[n]) { p.push(prev[n]); n = prev[n].src; } return p.reverse(); }

// next pages reached when edge `e` fires: follow calls/intents/lifecycle/async until a screen is entered
function nextScreens(e) {
  const found = new Set(), seen = new Set();
  const walk = (n, depth) => {
    if (isScreen(n)) { found.add(BASE(n)); return; }
    if (seen.has(n) || depth > 14) return; seen.add(n);
    for (const x of out[n] || []) {
      if (x.kind === 'REGISTER' || x.kind === 'FRAMEWORK') continue;
      if (x.needs_state && !state.setup) continue;
      walk(x.dst, depth + 1);
    }
  };
  walk(e.dst, 0);
  return [...found];
}
function transitions(page) {            // all gestures/system events on any variant of `page`
  const rows = [];
  for (const s of Object.keys(G.screens).concat(Object.keys(out).filter(k => isScreen(k) && !(k in G.screens)))) {
    if (BASE(s) !== page) continue;
    for (const e of out[s] || []) {
      if (e.kind === 'REGISTER' || e.kind === 'ENTERS' || e.kind === 'FRAMEWORK') continue;
      if (e.needs_state && !state.setup) continue;
      rows.push({e, variant: s, next: nextScreens(e)});
    }
  }
  const seen = new Set(), res = [];
  rows.forEach(r => { const k = r.variant + r.e.id; if (!seen.has(k)) { seen.add(k); res.push(r); } });
  // a click on the action bar goes through onOptionsItemSelected, which then switches on the menu id: show one row per menu item
  const expanded = [];
  res.forEach(r => {
    if (!/onOptionsItemSelected$/.test(BASE(r.e.dst))) { expanded.push(r); return; }
    const subs = (out[r.e.dst] || []).filter(c => c.kind !== 'REGISTER' && !(c.needs_state && !state.setup));
    if (!subs.length) { expanded.push(r); return; }
    subs.forEach(c => {
      const m = /(android\.R\.id\.home|action_\w+)/.exec(c.trigger); const id = m ? m[1] : c.dst;
      const label = id === 'android.R.id.home' ? 'Up arrow (top-left)' : id.replace('action_', '').replace('_', ' ');
      expanded.push({e: c, variant: r.variant, next: nextScreens(c), label: 'Menu item: ' + label, via: r.e});
    });
  });
  return expanded;
}

// ---------- shared rendering -------------------------------------------------------------------
const nodeBox = n => el('span', {class: 'node ' + (isScreen(n) ? 'screen' : (['DEAD', 'UNREACHABLE'].includes(G.verdict[BASE(n)]) ? 'dead' : '')), text: n});
function edgeCard(e) {
  const bits = [el('span', {class: 'badge k-' + e.kind, text: e.kind}), e.trigger];
  const d = el('div', {class: 'arrow'}, el('div', {}, bits));
  if (e.gui && e.gui.length) d.append(el('div', {class: 'gui', text: 'GUI: ' + e.gui.join(' ; ')}));
  if (e.guard) d.append(el('div', {class: 'guard', text: 'Guard: ' + e.guard}));
  if (e.needs_state) d.append(el('div', {class: 'warn', text: '⚠ Needs prior state: ' + e.needs_state}));
  const det = [];
  if (e.reads && e.reads.length) det.push('Reads: ' + e.reads.join(', '));
  if (e.writes && e.writes.length) det.push('Writes: ' + e.writes.join(', '));
  if (e.note) det.push('Note: ' + e.note);
  if (det.length) d.append(el('details', {}, el('summary', {text: 'Channels / note'}), el('div', {class: 'muted', text: det.join(' | ')})));
  return d;
}
const go = (tab, key) => { state.tab = tab; if (tab === 'page') state.page = key; if (tab === 'meth') state.meth = key; render(); };

// ---------- tab: page map -------------------------------------------------------------------
function renderMap(pane) {
  const pages = [...new Set(Object.keys(G.screens).map(BASE))].filter(p => state.bg || !isNoise(p));
  const adj = {}, label = {};
  pages.forEach(p => adj[p] = {});
  pages.forEach(p => transitions(p).forEach(r => r.next.forEach(n => {
    if (n === p || !pages.includes(n)) return; (adj[p][n] = adj[p][n] || []).push((r.label || r.e.gui[0] || r.e.trigger).slice(0, 60));
  })));
  // layered by BFS depth from S_ENTRY
  const depth = {'S_ENTRY': 0}, q = ['S_ENTRY'];
  while (q.length) { const n = q.shift(); for (const m in adj[n] || {}) if (!(m in depth)) { depth[m] = depth[n] + 1; q.push(m); } }
  const maxd = Math.max(...Object.values(depth));
  pages.forEach(p => { if (!(p in depth)) depth[p] = maxd + 1; });
  const cols = {}; pages.forEach(p => (cols[depth[p]] = cols[depth[p]] || []).push(p));
  const W = 200, H = 62, X0 = 20, Y0 = 20, GX = 70, GY = 22, pos = {};
  let maxrows = 0;
  Object.keys(cols).forEach(c => { cols[c].forEach((p, i) => pos[p] = {x: X0 + c * (W + GX), y: Y0 + i * (H + GY)}); maxrows = Math.max(maxrows, cols[c].length); });
  const width = X0 * 2 + (Object.keys(cols).length) * (W + GX), height = Y0 * 2 + maxrows * (H + GY);
  const svg = svgel('svg', {viewBox: `0 0 ${width} ${height}`, width, height, style: 'max-width:none'});
  svg.append(svgel('defs', {}, svgel('marker', {id: 'ah', viewBox: '0 0 8 8', refX: 7, refY: 4, markerWidth: 7, markerHeight: 7, orient: 'auto'}, svgel('path', {d: 'M0 0L8 4L0 8z', fill: 'currentColor', style: 'color:var(--mut)'}))));
  const edgeEls = [];
  pages.forEach(a => Object.keys(adj[a]).forEach(b => {
    const A = pos[a], B = pos[b]; if (!A || !B) return;
    const fwd = depth[b] > depth[a]; let d;
    if (fwd) { const x1 = A.x + W, y1 = A.y + H / 2, x2 = B.x, y2 = B.y + H / 2; d = `M${x1} ${y1} C${x1 + 40} ${y1} ${x2 - 40} ${y2} ${x2} ${y2}`; }
    else { const x1 = A.x + W / 2, y1 = A.y, x2 = B.x + W / 2, y2 = B.y; const up = Math.min(y1, y2) - 30 - 8 * Math.abs(depth[a] - depth[b]); d = `M${x1} ${y1} C${x1} ${up} ${x2} ${up} ${x2} ${y2}`; }
    const p = svgel('path', {d, class: 'medge', 'marker-end': 'url(#ah)', 'data-a': a, 'data-b': b}, svgel('title', {}, `${a} → ${b}\n` + [...new Set(adj[a][b])].join('\n')));
    edgeEls.push(p); svg.append(p);
  }));
  pages.forEach(p => {
    const P = pos[p], g = svgel('g', {class: 'mapnode' + (p === state.page ? ' on' : ''), onclick: () => go('page', p)},
      svgel('title', {}, G.screens[p] || p), svgel('rect', {x: P.x, y: P.y, width: W, height: H, rx: 8}),
      svgel('text', {x: P.x + 10, y: P.y + 24, 'font-weight': 600}, p.replace('S_', '')),
      svgel('text', {x: P.x + 10, y: P.y + 44}, (G.screens[p] || '').split(/[:：(（,]/)[0].slice(0, 20)));
    g.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => edgeEls.forEach(e => e.classList.toggle('hl', e.dataset.a === p || e.dataset.b === p)));
    g.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => edgeEls.forEach(e => e.classList.remove('hl')));
    svg.append(g);
  });
  pane.append(el('h2', {text: 'Page map'}), el('div', {class: 'muted', text: 'Each box is a page or UI state. An arrow means: after some user gesture or system event (through its callback chain) the user lands on the next page. Hover a box to highlight its incoming and outgoing arrows, hover an arrow to see which gestures cause it, click a box to open its page view. Columns run left to right by number of steps from the start page; arrows pointing back arc above.'}), el('div', {class: 'mapwrap'}, svg));
}

// ---------- tab: page -> next pages -------------------------------------------------------------------
function renderPage(pane) {
  const p = state.page, rows = transitions(p);
  pane.append(el('h2', {text: p}), el('div', {class: 'muted', text: G.screens[p] || ''}));
  const incoming = [];
  Object.keys(G.screens).map(BASE).filter((v, i, a) => a.indexOf(v) === i).forEach(q => transitions(q).forEach(r => { if (q !== p && r.next.includes(p)) incoming.push({from: q, r}); }));
  pane.append(el('h3', {text: 'How to get here'}));
  const inSet = {}; incoming.forEach(x => (inSet[x.from] = inSet[x.from] || []).push(x.r.label || x.r.e.gui[0] || x.r.e.trigger));
  pane.append(el('div', {}, Object.keys(inSet).length ? Object.keys(inSet).map(f => el('span', {class: 'chip', title: [...new Set(inSet[f])].join('\n'), onclick: () => go('page', f), text: f})) : el('span', {class: 'muted', text: '(none, or only via "needs prior state" edges)'})));
  pane.append(el('h3', {text: `What can happen on this page (${rows.length})`}));
  const tbl = el('table', {}, el('tr', {}, el('th', {text: 'Gesture / event'}), el('th', {text: 'Callback method'}), el('th', {text: 'Then lands on'}), el('th', {text: 'Guard / details'})));
  rows.forEach(r => {
    const e = r.e, vtag = r.variant !== p && r.variant.includes('@') ? el('span', {class: 'muted', text: ' [' + r.variant.split('@')[1] + ']'}) : null;
    tbl.append(el('tr', {},
      el('td', {}, el('span', {class: 'badge k-' + (r.via ? 'GUI' : e.kind), text: r.via ? 'GUI' : e.kind}), (r.label || e.gui[0] || e.trigger), vtag, (!r.via && e.gui.length > 1) ? el('div', {class: 'muted', text: e.gui.slice(1).join(' ; ')}) : null, r.via ? el('div', {class: 'muted', text: 'via ' + r.via.dst}) : null),
      el('td', {}, el('a', {href: '#', onclick: ev => { ev.preventDefault(); go('meth', BASE(e.dst)); }, text: e.dst})),
      el('td', {}, r.next.length ? r.next.map(n => el('span', {class: 'chip', onclick: () => go('page', n), text: n})) : el('span', {class: 'muted', text: '(stays on this page / no page change)'})),
      el('td', {}, el('div', {class: 'guard', text: e.guard || ''}), el('details', {}, el('summary', {text: 'Trigger details'}), el('div', {class: 'muted', text: e.trigger + (e.note ? ' | ' + e.note : '')})))));
  });
  pane.append(tbl);
}

// ---------- tab: method -> trigger path -------------------------------------------------------------------
function renderMeth(pane) {
  const m = state.meth, prev = bfs(), vs = Object.keys(prev).filter(n => BASE(n) === m);
  const v = G.verdict[m];
  const L = (G.labels || {})[m] || {}, kinds = a => (a || []).map(g => g.kinds.join('+')).join(', ') || 'none';
  const why = L.unreachable ? L.unreachable.kind + ': ' + L.unreachable.text : ((G.dead || {})[m] || (G.framework_only || {})[m] || '');
  pane.append(el('h2', {text: m}), el('div', {class: 'muted', text: 'Verdict: ' + v + (why ? ' — ' + why : '')}));
  if (G.labels && v === 'REACHABLE') pane.append(el('div', {class: 'muted', text: 'ENV: ' + kinds(L.env) + ' | STATE: ' + kinds(L.state) + ' | edge classes on the best route: ' + ((L.route_edge_classes || []).join(', ') || 'none') + ' | explicit/standard-only route: ' + (L.explicit_standard_route ? 'yes' : 'no') + ' | confirmed by: ' + L.confirmed_by + (L.source_ref ? ' (' + L.source_ref + ')' : '') + ' | path: ' + L.path_status}));
  if (!vs.length) {
    const noway = v === 'DEAD' || v === 'FRAMEWORK_ONLY' || v === 'UNREACHABLE';
    pane.append(el('div', {class: 'card warn', text: noway ? 'No incoming edges: see the verdict above (unreachable).' : (state.setup ? 'The graph has no path from the start page to this method.' : 'No path unless "needs prior state" edges are included; tick the box above and look again.')}));
  }
  // one candidate route per distinct incoming edge, shortest first
  const cands = [];
  (inn[m] || []).forEach(e => {
    if (e.kind === 'REGISTER' || !(e.src in prev)) return;
    if (e.needs_state && !state.setup) return;
    cands.push(pathTo(e.src, prev).concat([e]));
  });
  cands.sort((a, b) => a.length - b.length);
  const uniq = []; const seenSig = new Set();
  cands.forEach(p => { const s = p[p.length - 1].id; if (!seenSig.has(s)) { seenSig.add(s); uniq.push(p); } });
  if (uniq.length) {
    pane.append(el('h3', {text: `Path from the start to here (shortest: ${uniq[0].length} steps; ${uniq.length} distinct entry edge${uniq.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}; the 3 shortest are shown)`}));
    uniq.slice(0, 3).forEach((p, i) => {
      const box = el('div', {class: 'card'}, el('div', {class: 'muted', text: 'Path ' + (i + 1) + ' · ' + p.length + ' steps'}));
      box.append(nodeBox(p[0].src));
      p.forEach(e => { box.append(edgeCard(e), nodeBox(e.dst)); });
      pane.append(box);
    });
  }
  const ins = (inn[m] || []);
  pane.append(el('h3', {text: `All incoming edges (${ins.length}): every known way to trigger it`}));
  ins.forEach(e => pane.append(el('div', {class: 'card'}, el('span', {class: 'node', text: e.src}), edgeCard(e))));
  const outs = Object.keys(out).filter(k => BASE(k) === m).flatMap(k => out[k]).filter(e => e.kind !== 'REGISTER');
  pane.append(el('h3', {text: `What this method triggers next (${outs.length})`}));
  outs.forEach(e => pane.append(el('div', {class: 'card'}, edgeCard(e), nodeBox(e.dst))));
}

// ---------- sidebar & shell -------------------------------------------------------------------
function renderSide(side) {
  const search = el('input', {type: 'search', placeholder: state.tab === 'meth' ? 'Filter methods…' : 'Filter pages…', value: state.q});
  search.addEventListener('input', () => { state.q = search.value; renderSideList(side, list); });
  const list = el('div', {});
  side.append(search, list); renderSideList(side, list);
}
function renderSideList(side, list) {
  list.textContent = '';
  const q = state.q.toLowerCase();
  if (state.tab === 'meth') {
    const byc = {};
    G.methods.filter(m => m.toLowerCase().includes(q)).forEach(m => (byc[m.split('.')[0]] = byc[m.split('.')[0]] || []).push(m));
    Object.keys(byc).forEach(c => {
      list.append(el('div', {class: 'grp', text: c}));
      byc[c].forEach(m => list.append(el('div', {class: 'item' + (m === state.meth ? ' on' : ''), onclick: () => go('meth', m)}, el('span', {class: 'dot ' + G.verdict[m]}), m.slice(c.length + 1))));
    });
  } else {
    [...new Set(Object.keys(G.screens).map(BASE))].filter(p => p.toLowerCase().includes(q)).forEach(p =>
      list.append(el('div', {class: 'item' + (p === state.page ? ' on' : ''), onclick: () => go('page', p), text: p})));
  }
}
function render() {
  if (G.title) { document.getElementById('title').textContent = G.title; document.title = G.title; }
  document.getElementById('stats').textContent = `${G.methods.length} methods · ${Object.keys(G.screens).length} pages · ${G.edges.length} edges`;
  ['map', 'page', 'meth'].forEach(t => document.getElementById('t-' + t).classList.toggle('on', state.tab === t));
  const side = document.getElementById('side'), pane = document.getElementById('pane');
  side.textContent = ''; pane.textContent = '';

  ({map: renderMap, page: renderPage, meth: renderMeth})[state.tab](pane);
}

document.getElementById('t-meth').onclick = () => { state.tab = 'meth'; state.q = ''; render(); };

FILE	tools/write_summaries.py	10299	0033b14faef5dfbb1546b0ed1dd6402e39c50bd9ad8aeffd38cfdebbcd063e76	utf8
"""Write graphs/SUMMARY.md and handoff/FINAL_SUMMARY.md with numbers recomputed from graphs/*/out. Run from work/ after the builds.
Usage: python3 -I tools/write_summaries.py"""
import collections, csv, json, subprocess

cal = json.load(open('tools/tests/calibration.json'))
cal_da = ', '.join('%.1f%% (%s)' % (v['dead_vs_alive'], k) for k, v in cal.items())
cal_ex = ', '.join('%.1f%% (%s)' % (v['exact'], k) for k, v in cal.items())

table = subprocess.run(['python3', '-I', 'tools/summary_table.py'], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout


def frontier_count(app, node):
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app)))
    return sum(1 for r in rows if r.get('frontier_first_unrun') == node)


def hits(app):
    rows = [r for r in csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app)) if r['monkey_hit'] != '']
    h = sum(1 for r in rows if r['monkey_hit'] == '1' or r['ape_hit'] == '1')
    return h, len(rows)


mm_h, mm_n = hits('MMEX')
ae_h, ae_n = hits('Aegis')
init_home = frontier_count('MMEX', 'MainActivity.initHomeFragment')

index = '''# Trigger graphs of the 13 Java-only apps

Each folder `graphs/<App>/out/` holds the same set of files as `share/` has for Currency: `<prefix>_trigger_graph.json` (pages, methods, verdicts, edges with evidence labels), `methods.csv` (one row per method: verdict, steps from launch, taps on the best path, path, baseline hits), `README.md` (numbers, how much to trust it, limits), `<prefix>_trigger_graph.html` (optional viewer) and `build_report.txt` (validation output). **The JSON and the CSV are the data.**

## Two ways of building

- **Hand-written** (Currency, Diary, hash-checker, openlauncher, Omni-Notes): the edge definitions were written by reading the source; state gates (`needs_state`) and environment gates (`env`) come from a human reading the code. Diary was made by Kiro with older tools.
- **Generated** (Vanilla-Music, AnyMemo, Markor, Aegis, AmazeFileManager, AnkiDroid, MMEX, LibreTorrent): `tools/gen_edges.py` derives entry points, lifecycle, callbacks, lambdas, intents, XML-instantiated classes and library glue (ButterKnife, EventBus, data binding, Dagger, Glide, Room, ...) from the bytecode, the manifest and `res/` XML; the per-app `graph_def.py` only adds a few gates; `review_notes.md` records what was reviewed by hand. Run on the five hand-written apps (`tools/calibrate.py`), the generator agrees with the hand-written graphs on **@CALDA@ of the methods for dead-versus-alive** and on @CALEX@ of the exact verdicts; the gap is `needs_state` (a human reads "needs a note / a folder / a setting" out of the code). So in the generated graphs `REACHABLE` means "no modelled setup", not "no setup".
- The generated graphs add five columns to `methods.csv` (`frontier_from`, `frontier_first_unrun`, `frontier_edge_kind`, `frontier_edge_evidence`, `steps_after_frontier`): for a method the baseline never ran, the edge where the chain from code that did run first enters code that never ran, i.e. the branch or event that blocked the baseline. Each generated README lists the ten frontier nodes that unlock the most never-run methods.

## Numbers (recomputed from the files by `tools/summary_table.py`)

''' + table + '''
"never run: dead / env / setup / clean" splits the methods that neither APE nor Monkey ran: dead or framework-only, reachable only through an outside environment, reachable after in-app setup, reachable with no modelled setup (the clearest target list for the paper). "baseline-run" counts the methods that ran in at least one of the two 1-hour API 27 runs. "contradictions" are dead or framework-only methods that ran anyway (must be 0).

## Rebuild

`python3 -I tools/build_graph.py graphs/<App> <prefix>` (from `work/`), then for generated apps `python3 -I tools/gen_readme.py <App> <prefix>` and `python3 -I tools/write_summaries.py`. `tools/check_gen.sh` re-runs the generator on the two hand-written apps and prints the agreement. The recipe, the tool facts and the rules learned are in `handoff/HANDOFF.md`.
'''
index = index.replace('@CALDA@', cal_da).replace('@CALEX@', cal_ex)
open('graphs/SUMMARY.md', 'w').write(index)

final = '''# Final summary: trigger graphs for the 13 Java-only apps (overnight run, 2026-10-24/25)

**Status: all 13 apps have `graphs/<App>/out/` with the graph JSON, `methods.csv`, `README.md` and the optional HTML viewer.** Index and rebuild commands: `graphs/SUMMARY.md`. Five graphs were written by hand earlier (Currency, Diary by Kiro, hash-checker, openlauncher, Omni-Notes); the other eight (Vanilla-Music, AnyMemo, Markor, Aegis, AmazeFileManager, AnkiDroid, MMEX, LibreTorrent) were produced overnight by a **generator** (`tools/gen_edges.py`) plus a short per-app review, because hand-writing took about 2.5 hours and 450k tokens per app and the eight apps hold 25.5k methods.

## Numbers (recomputed by `tools/summary_table.py`)

''' + table + '''
## What the generated graphs are, and are not

- Built from the bytecode call graph, `AndroidManifest.xml` and `res/` XML: entry points, lifecycle, callbacks of constructed classes, lambdas, intents, XML-instantiated classes, activity / permission results, `Handler` messages, and glue for ButterKnife, EventBus, data binding, Dagger / Hilt, Glide, Room, Icepick, Parceler, ORMLite, reflection by method name. Methods that nothing reaches are `DEAD` (automatic, `AUTO_DEAD`).
- **Validation.** 0 errors, 0 warnings and **0 contradictions with the baseline coverage** (no DEAD method ran in the 1-hour APE / Monkey runs) in all 8; every contradiction found during development was a missing generator rule and was fixed. Run on the five hand-written apps the generator agrees on @CALDA@ of the methods for dead-versus-alive, and on @CALEX@ of the exact verdicts. The five hand-written graphs were rebuilt with the changed tools and came out identical.
- **Limit that matters most:** `REACHABLE` in a generated graph means "no modelled setup". State gates beyond list rows (data that must exist, settings, files) are only modelled where `review_notes.md` / `graph_def.py` of the app lists them, so the split REACHABLE / REACHABLE_AFTER_SETUP is less reliable than in the hand-written graphs. Callbacks are reachable once an instance is constructed; Handler messages, EventBus subscribers and Dagger objects are linked from every send / post / graph-build site (INFERRED).
- **New in the generated CSVs:** the `frontier_*` columns say, for each never-run reachable method, where the chain from code that did run first enters code that never ran (a branch not taken, or an event nobody triggered). Each README lists the ten frontier nodes that unlock the most never-run methods.

## Findings about the baseline (from the hit counts)

- **MMEX: only @MMH@ of @MMN@ observable methods ran.** The hits stop at the tutorial, `SelectDatabaseActivity`, the password screen and the settings; no method of the packages `account`, `transactions`, `investment`, `reports`, `recurring`, `search`, `fragment`, `adapter` ran and `HomeFragment` was never created. `MainActivity.onCreate` ran but returns early when `showPrerequisite()` is true (the tutorial is still needed, or no valid database is selected: it starts `TutorialActivity` / `SelectDatabaseActivity` and calls `finish()`), so `initHomeFragment` never ran and @INIT@ never-run methods sit behind it. The MMEX graph gates those packages behind that prerequisite (read from `MainActivity.java`, stated in its README).
- **Aegis: @AEH@ of @AEN@.** The baseline got past the first-run setup and showed the vault list, but the vault stayed empty (`EntryAdapter.onBindViewHolder` never ran), and it never opened `EditEntryActivity` (add / edit an entry), the importers of the other authenticator apps, the backup preferences, or the unlock screen (`AuthActivity`).
- **LibreTorrent:** no method of the torrent detail screens ran (apparently no torrent was added; the fixtures have no `.torrent`).
- **Fixtures:** Vanilla-Music had 12 mp3 + 3 wav (so no fixture for the Opus / Ogg / FLAC / MP4 tag parsers); the Download folder has 19 files (`archive.zip`, `table.csv`, `todo.txt`, `tasks.org`, `notes.md`, `journal.md`, `sample_deck.apkg`, `sample.pdf`, `page.html`, `config.xml`, `data.json`, `events.ics`, `contacts.vcf`, six images), so csv, todo.txt and AnkiDroid-import files existed, but there is no fixture for 7z / gz / bz2 archives, apk or db files, Mnemosyne / SuperMemo files, `.jenc` notes or torrents; Markor's `Documents` fixture folder is missing.
- From earlier in the session: `merged.ec` omits the launch dump `00000.ec` (first-launch code can show `monkey_hit = 0` although it ran); one dump per run is unreadable; APE seems to wipe app data mid-run in Aegis, Markor and hash-checker; Yang's `share/methods.csv` drops the final tap in 4 rows whose path ends in `*.onOptionsItemSelected`.

## Open questions for Yang (unchanged, still unanswered)

1. Is device verification (emulator) required for each app, and for which edges?
2. Baseline tool: the draft says Monkey, `tool_run_set_38.csv` says APE.
3. The missing `fixtures/Documents` folder (Markor).
4. How were Aegis and MMEX set up on the baseline (coverage 25% and 6%)?
5. The draft counts 41 apps; here there are 13 Java-only apps.
6. Are generated graphs with class-level review acceptable as ground truth, or should selected apps be hand-curated further (the biggest gains: Aegis, MMEX, AnkiDroid, LibreTorrent state gates)?

## If more time is spent

- Hand-curate the `needs_state` gates of the biggest never-run clusters (each README lists them; `review_notes.md` says what is assumed) and re-run `tools/check_gen.sh`.
- Rebuild Diary with the current tools (Kiro's graph used older ones; its inputs `method_list.json` / `auto_calls.json` are not in `graphs/Diary/`).
- Verify the `FW-MEMORY` rules and the frontier edges on an emulator.
'''

FILE	handoff/examples/README.md	913	2f483853b352f8cba056d56f638b58316661987804a4768ac3ee9fbe2c8fa3f8	utf8
Worked examples of the helper scripts used for hash-checker and Omni-Notes (run from `work/`, copy them out and adjust the paths first):

- `make_vlog_hash_checker.py`, `make_vlog_omni_notes.py`: write `graphs/<App>/verification.csv` from the final build. Every note is asserted against the graph (`has_edge`) so the log cannot say something the graph does not carry. The Omni one also rebuilds the first graph (`SCR/omni_old`: the archived v1 definition built with the current builder) to tell which first-graph routes had the first-resume shortcut; the path `SCR` is a scratch directory, change it.
- `croute.py <App> <method>...`: compact route printer (kind, class, trigger, guard, ENV, STATE, evidence) with the enclosing `if` / `for` / `catch` lines of each bytecode-cited call.
- `src.py <App> File.java:from-to ...`: prints source lines of the app by file name (flavor aware, uses `tools/show_route.py`).

FILE	handoff/examples/croute.py	3175	6cd00dcfc69079c7ca2b7783faa627ae052456d51e984f49c1455356981485cb	utf8
import csv, glob, json, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, 'tools')
import show_route
app = sys.argv[1]
g = json.load(open(glob.glob('graphs/%s/out/*_trigger_graph.json' % app)[0]))
E = {x['id']: x for x in g['edges']}
rows = {r['method']: r for r in csv.DictReader(open('graphs/%s/out/methods.csv' % app, encoding='utf-8'))}
idx = show_route.source_index(app)
fl = show_route.built_flavors(app)
cache = {}
CTRL = re.compile(r'^\s*(\}\s*)?(else\b|if\s*\(|for\s*\(|while\s*\(|do\b|switch\s*\(|case\b|default:|try\b|catch\b|synchronized)|\?\s*$|->\s*\{\s*$')
DECL = re.compile(r'^\s*(public|private|protected|static|final|abstract|@Override|void|[A-Za-z_<>\[\],. ]+)\s+[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*\s*\([^;]*\)\s*(throws [\w., ]+)?\s*\{\s*$')


def lines_of(fname):
    p = show_route.find(idx, fname, fl)
    if not p:
        return None
    if p not in cache:
        cache[p] = open(p, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace').read().split('\n')
    return cache[p]


def guards(fname, n):
    L = lines_of(fname)
    if not L or not (1 <= n <= len(L)):
        return []
    ind = lambda s: len(s) - len(s.lstrip())
    cur = ind(L[n - 1])
    out = []
    i = n - 2
    while i >= 0:
        s = L[i]
        if not s.strip() or s.strip().startswith(('//', '*', '/*')):
            i -= 1
            continue
        if ind(s) < cur:
            t = s.strip()
            if DECL.match(s) or re.match(r'^\s*(public|private|protected)\b', s):
                break
            if CTRL.search(s):
                txt = '%d: %s' % (i + 1, t[:90])
                if re.match(r'^\}?\s*else\b', t) and not re.match(r'^\}?\s*else\s+if', t):
                    # pair the else with the if of the same indent
                    for j in range(i - 1, -1, -1):
                        u = L[j]
                        if u.strip() and ind(u) == ind(s) and re.match(r'^\s*(\}\s*else\s+)?if\s*\(', u) and not re.match(r'^\s*\}\s*else', u):
                            txt = '%d: NOT(%s) %s' % (j + 1, L[j].strip()[:80], '')
                            break
                out.append(txt)
            cur = ind(s)
        i -= 1
    return list(reversed(out))


seen = set()
for m in sys.argv[2:]:
    r = rows.get(m)
    if not r:
        print('MISSING', m); continue
    print('##', m, r['verdict'], 'env=', r['env_kinds'] or '-', 'state=', r['state_kinds'] or '-', 'steps=', len(r['route_edge_ids'].split()))
    for i, eid in enumerate(r['route_edge_ids'].split(), 1):
        x = E[eid]
        if eid in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(eid)
        extra = []
        if x['guard']: extra.append('G:' + x['guard'][:60])
        if x['env']: extra.append('ENV:' + x['env'][:50])
        if x['needs_state']: extra.append('ST:' + x['needs_state'][:60])
        print('  %2d %s/%s %s -> %s | %s %s | %s' % (i, x['kind'], x['edge_class'][:3], x['src'][:46], x['dst'][:46], x['trigger'][:60], ' '.join(extra), x['evidence'][:75].replace('\n', ' ')))
        mm = re.search(r'SRC ([\w./$-]+\.java):(\d+)(?:-\d+)? \(bytecode', x['evidence'] or '')
        if mm:
            gs = guards(mm.group(1), int(mm.group(2)))
            if gs:
                print('        in: ' + ' || '.join(gs))

def eid(src, dst, contains=None):
    c = [x for x in g['edges'] if x['src'] == src and x['dst'] == dst and (contains is None or contains in (x['evidence'] or ''))]
    assert c, (src, dst, contains)
    return c[0]['id']
seen = set()
def edge(i, res, ref, note):
    if i in seen:
        return
    seen.add(i)
    out.append(['edge', i, res, 'SOURCE', ref, '', note])

SC = ('ERROR FOUND: the first-graph route reached the list through the shortcut edges "MainActivity.onResume -> ListFragment.onResume / MainActivity.init / the list page", taken right after the process starts. '
      'On a cold start isPasswordAccepted is false, so onResume calls checkPassword, which calls init(), which adds the ListFragment once (MainActivity.java:111-118, 138-156, 178-182); the shortcuts skip those steps and are only valid after an earlier resume. '

def route_edges(m, graph_rows, graph_edges):
    return [graph_edges[i] for i in graph_rows[m]['route_edge_ids'].split() if i in graph_edges]


SCPAIRS = {('MainActivity.onResume', 'ListFragment.onResume'), ('MainActivity.onResume', 'MainActivity.init')}


def had_shortcut(m):
    for x in route_edges(m, oldrows, OE):
        if (x['src'], x['dst']) in SCPAIRS or (x['src'] == 'MainActivity.onResume' and x['dst'].startswith('S_')):
            return True
    return False


def has_edge(src, dst, **kw):
    """assert the final graph carries a gate that a note below talks about"""
    for x in g['edges']:
        if x['src'] == src and x['dst'] == dst:
            for k, v in kw.items():
                if v not in (x.get(k) or ''):
                    break
            else:
                return x['id']
    raise AssertionError((src, dst, kw))


P = {}
def p(m, res, ref, note, sc=True):
    if sc:
        assert had_shortcut(m), m
        note = SC + ' ' + note
    P[m] = (res, ref, note)

FILE	handoff/examples/route_print_template.py	8010	ba9523dafbfc94f46ca0a563d28792ba621d089ceb42abc7a363b55909ebc473	utf8
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# Route printer template: one copy per app. Set the constants below for the app.
# Run from the work folder:  python3 -I graphs/<App>/scratch/route_print.py
#
# For each of the 30 methods in scratch/kiro_sample_30_final.txt it reads the
# route (route_edge_ids) from out/methods.csv and the edges from
# out/anymemo_trigger_graph.json, and for every step prints:
#   step number, edge id, source -> target, class, guard, needs_state, env,
#   and the FULL evidence text.
# For every citation in the evidence -- INCLUDING ranges after a comma
# (e.g. "ui/FileBrowserFragment.java:594, 480") -- it prints each cited range
# from the source with line numbers.
#   App citations  (SRC ...)    resolve under APP_BASE.
#   Framework      (FW-SRC ...) resolve under FW_BASE; "framework not printed"
#                               if the file is absent.
# One output file per method is written to scratch/route_print/<method>.txt
# with '$' replaced by '_'.

import csv
import json
import os
import re

WORK = os.getcwd()
GDIR = os.path.join(WORK, "graphs", "AnyMemo")
SAMPLE = os.path.join(GDIR, "scratch", "kiro_sample_30_final.txt")
METHODS_CSV = os.path.join(GDIR, "out", "methods.csv")
GRAPH_JSON = os.path.join(GDIR, "out", "anymemo_trigger_graph.json")
OUT_DIR = os.path.join(GDIR, "scratch", "route_print")

APP_BASE = os.path.join(
    WORK,
    "final_java_only", "AnyMemo", "app", "AnyMemo-#10.12.2",
    "app", "src", "main", "java", "org", "liberty", "android", "fantastischmemo",
)
# Some citations give a bare class file (e.g. "AMApplication.java:56-60") whose
# real path is under a sub-package (common/), and AndroidManifest.xml lives at
# the module root (app/src/main/).  Index the whole app module by basename and
# by "/<suffix>" so both forms resolve to the real file.
APP_MODULE = os.path.join(
    WORK, "final_java_only", "AnyMemo", "app", "AnyMemo-#10.12.2", "app", "src", "main",
)
FW_BASE = os.path.join(WORK, "fw_src", "android-8.1.0_r1")

_APP_INDEX = None


def app_index():
    global _APP_INDEX
    if _APP_INDEX is None:
        idx = {}
        for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(APP_MODULE):
            for fn in files:
                full = os.path.join(root, fn)
                idx.setdefault(fn, []).append(full)
        _APP_INDEX = idx
    return _APP_INDEX


def resolve_app(path):
    """Resolve an app citation path to a real file, or None.

    Tries: direct join under the fantastischmemo package base; then basename
    lookup in the app module index (prefer a unique match, else one whose tail
    equals the citation path)."""
    direct = os.path.join(APP_BASE, path)
    if os.path.exists(direct):
        return direct
    base = os.path.basename(path)
    cands = app_index().get(base, [])
    if not cands:
        return None
    if len(cands) == 1:
        return cands[0]
    # multiple same-named files: prefer one whose path ends with the citation
    norm = path.replace("\\", "/")
    for c in cands:
        if c.replace("\\", "/").endswith("/" + norm) or c.replace("\\", "/").endswith("/" + base):
            if c.replace("\\", "/").endswith("/" + norm):
                return c
    return cands[0]

# A citation = a path ending in .java or .xml, then ':', then one or more
# comma-separated ranges (num or num-num).  Whitespace after commas allowed.
CITE = re.compile(
    r"(?P<path>[\w./$#-]+\.(?:java|xml)):"
    r"(?P<ranges>\d+(?:-\d+)?(?:\s*,\s*\d+(?:-\d+)?)*)"
)
# Prefix markers tell us app (SRC) vs framework (FW-SRC).
MARKER = re.compile(r"\bFW-SRC\b|\bSRC\b")


def load_methods():
    rows = {}
    with open(METHODS_CSV, newline="") as fh:
        for row in csv.DictReader(fh):
            rows[row["method"]] = row
    return rows


def load_edges():
    g = json.load(open(GRAPH_JSON))
    return {e["id"]: e for e in g["edges"]}


def sample_methods():
    out = []
    with open(SAMPLE) as fh:
        for i, line in enumerate(fh):
            line = line.rstrip("\n")
            if i == 0 or not line.strip():
                continue
            out.append(line.split()[-1])
    return out


def citations_with_kind(evidence):
    """Yield (kind, path, [(a,b), ...]) for each citation, kind is 'app' or 'fw'.

    The citation inherits the kind of the nearest preceding SRC / FW-SRC marker.
    """
    # record marker positions and kinds
    markers = [(m.start(), m.group(0)) for m in MARKER.finditer(evidence)]

    def kind_at(pos):
        kind = "app"  # default to app when no marker precedes
        for mpos, mtxt in markers:
            if mpos <= pos:
                kind = "fw" if mtxt == "FW-SRC" else "app"
            else:
                break
        return kind

    for m in CITE.finditer(evidence):
        path = m.group("path")
        ranges = []
        for part in m.group("ranges").split(","):
            part = part.strip()
            if "-" in part:
                a, b = part.split("-", 1)
                ranges.append((int(a), int(b)))
            else:
                n = int(part)
                ranges.append((n, n))
        yield kind_at(m.start()), path, ranges


def print_range(lines, a, b):
    out = []
    for n in range(a, b + 1):
        if 1 <= n <= len(lines):
            out.append("    %5d: %s" % (n, lines[n - 1].rstrip("\n")))
        else:
            out.append("    %5d: <line out of range (file has %d lines)>" % (n, len(lines)))
    return out


def emit_citation(w, kind, path, ranges):
    if kind == "fw":
        full = os.path.join(FW_BASE, path)
        label = "FW-SRC"
    else:
        full = resolve_app(path)
        label = "SRC"
    rng = ", ".join("%d-%d" % (a, b) if a != b else "%d" % a for a, b in ranges)
    w("  cite [%s] %s:%s" % (label, path, rng))
    if full is None or not os.path.exists(full):
        if kind == "fw":
            w("    framework not printed")
        else:
            w("    SOURCE FILE NOT FOUND for citation: %s" % path)
        return False
    if kind != "fw" and os.path.realpath(full) != os.path.realpath(os.path.join(APP_BASE, path)):
        w("    (resolved to %s)" % os.path.relpath(full, WORK))
    with open(full, errors="replace") as fh:
        lines = fh.readlines()
    for a, b in ranges:
        for ln in print_range(lines, a, b):
            w(ln)
    return True


def main():
    os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    methods = sample_methods()
    mrows = load_methods()
    edges = load_edges()

    for method in methods:
        safe = method.replace("$", "_")
        path_out = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, safe + ".txt")
        buf = []
        w = buf.append
        row = mrows.get(method)
        w("METHOD: %s" % method)
        if row is None:
            w("  (not found in methods.csv)")
            open(path_out, "w").write("\n".join(buf) + "\n")
            continue
        w("verdict: %s   steps_from_launch: %s" % (row["verdict"], row["steps_from_launch"]))
        eids = row["route_edge_ids"].split()
        w("route steps: %d edges" % len(eids))
        w("=" * 70)
        for i, eid in enumerate(eids, 1):
            e = edges.get(eid)
            w("")
            w("STEP %d  edge %s" % (i, eid))
            if e is None:
                w("  (edge id not found in graph json)")
                continue
            w("  %s -> %s" % (e.get("src"), e.get("dst")))
            w("  class: %s   kind: %s" % (e.get("edge_class"), e.get("kind")))
            w("  guard: %s" % e.get("guard"))
            w("  needs_state: %s" % e.get("needs_state"))
            w("  env: %s" % e.get("env"))
            ev = e.get("evidence") or ""
            w("  evidence: %s" % ev)
            cits = list(citations_with_kind(ev))
            if not cits:
                w("  (no file:line citations in evidence)")
            for kind, p, ranges in cits:
                emit_citation(w, kind, p, ranges)
        open(path_out, "w").write("\n".join(buf) + "\n")
        print("wrote %s (%d steps)" % (path_out, len(eids)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE	handoff/connector_kit/make_bundles.py	5404	6b23ccef919a87936912bbe7892dd0d5b37bef775367994af1a09669551093f9	utf8
"""Pack the files the tools need into text bundles cut into parts below the GitHub connector's ~1 MB limit.

Usage (from the repo root): python3 -I handoff/connector_kit/make_bundles.py <out_dir> [part_bytes]
Writes <out_dir>/<bundle>/<bundle>.partNNN.txt and <out_dir>/MANIFEST.json.
Bundles: common (tools, handoff/examples, handoff/connector_kit, fw_src) and one per app (graph inputs + app sources).
Bundle format: a header line "GTRBUNDLE 1", then per file a line "FILE\t<path>\t<size>\t<sha256>\t<utf8|b64>",
the content (raw UTF-8, or base64 for files that are not valid UTF-8), and a newline; the last line is "END".
Unpack with handoff/connector_kit/unpack.py.
"""
import base64
import glob
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys

APPS = ['Currency', 'Diary', 'hash-checker', 'Omni-Notes', 'openlauncher', 'Vanilla-Music', 'Markor', 'AnyMemo',
        'Aegis', 'AmazeFileManager', 'AnkiDroid', 'MMEX', 'LibreTorrent']
GRAPH_FILES = ['graph_def.py', 'method_list.json', 'auto_calls.json', 'calls_all.json', 'verification.csv',
               'make_verification.py', 'readme_notes.md', 'LESSONS.md', 'AGENT_NOTES.md']
SRC_EXT = ('.java', '.kt', '.xml', '.gradle', '.pro', '.properties', '.txt', '.json')
MARKER = ('Marker only. tools/show_route.py built_flavors() reads the name of this folder to know which product flavor\n'
          'the dataset APK was built from; the class files themselves are not in the repo.\n')
SKIP_DIRS = {'build', 'androidTest', 'test', '.git', 'JacocoInstrument', 'report', 'monkey', 'ape', 'combined'}


def common_files():
    out = []
    for f in sorted(os.listdir('tools')):
        p = os.path.join('tools', f)
        if os.path.isfile(p) and f.endswith(('.py', '.sh', '.html')) and 'backup' not in f:
            out.append(p)
    for d in ('handoff/examples', 'handoff/connector_kit', 'handoff/ci'):
        for f in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
            p = os.path.join(d, f)
            if os.path.isfile(p) and not f.endswith('.pyc'):
                out.append(p)
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk('fw_src'):
        dn.sort()
        for f in sorted(fn):
            out.append(os.path.join(dp, f))
    return out


def app_files(app):
    g = os.path.join('graphs', app)
    out = [os.path.join(g, f) for f in GRAPH_FILES if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(g, f))]
    for sub in ('graph_def_mod', 'parts' if app == 'LibreTorrent' else None,
                'scratch/merge-ready' if app == 'LibreTorrent' else None):
        if sub and os.path.isdir(os.path.join(g, sub)):
            out += [os.path.join(g, sub, f) for f in sorted(os.listdir(os.path.join(g, sub)))
                    if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(g, sub, f))]
    if app == 'Markor':
        out += [os.path.join(g, 'scratch', f) for f in ('hier.json', 'textcount.json', 'dotcount.json', 'route_guards.json')]
    if app == 'LibreTorrent':
        for f in ('gates.md',):
            if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(g, 'scratch', f)):
                out.append(os.path.join(g, 'scratch', f))
    root = os.path.join('final_java_only', app, 'app')
    for d in sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(root, '*', 'app', 'build', 'intermediates', 'javac', '*Debug'))):
        out.append((os.path.join(d, 'FLAVOR_MARKER.txt'), MARKER.encode('utf-8')))
    for f in sorted(os.listdir(root)):
        p = os.path.join(root, f)
        if os.path.isfile(p) and f.endswith(('.txt', '.json')):
            out.append(p)
    for dp, dn, fn in os.walk(root):
        dn[:] = sorted(d for d in dn if d not in SKIP_DIRS)
        if dp == root:
            continue
        for f in sorted(fn):
            if f.endswith(SRC_EXT):
                out.append(os.path.join(dp, f))
    return out


def pack(paths):
    chunks = [b'GTRBUNDLE 1\n']
    for p in paths:
        if isinstance(p, tuple):
            p, data = p
        else:
            data = open(p, 'rb').read()
        try:
            data.decode('utf-8')
            enc, body = 'utf8', data
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            enc, body = 'b64', base64.b64encode(data)
        head = 'FILE\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\n' % (p, len(data), hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), enc)
        chunks += [head.encode('utf-8'), body, b'\n']
    chunks.append(b'END\n')
    return b''.join(chunks)


def main(argv):
    out_dir = argv[1]
    part = int(argv[2]) if len(argv) > 2 else 700000
    manifest = {'format': 'GTRBUNDLE 1', 'part_bytes': part, 'bundles': {}}
    sets = [('common', common_files())] + [(a, app_files(a)) for a in APPS]
    for name, paths in sets:
        blob = pack(paths)
        d = os.path.join(out_dir, name)
        os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
        parts = []
        for i in range(0, len(blob), part):
            piece = blob[i:i + part]
            fn = '%s.part%03d.txt' % (name, i // part + 1)
            open(os.path.join(d, fn), 'wb').write(piece)
            parts.append({'file': '%s/%s' % (name, fn), 'bytes': len(piece), 'sha256': hashlib.sha256(piece).hexdigest()})
        manifest['bundles'][name] = {'files': len(paths), 'bytes': len(blob),
                                     'sha256': hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest(), 'parts': parts}
        print('%-17s %5d files %6.1f MB %3d parts' % (name, len(paths), len(blob) / 1e6, len(parts)))
    json.dump(manifest, open(os.path.join(out_dir, 'MANIFEST.json'), 'w'), indent=1)
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

FILE	handoff/connector_kit/split_graph_def.py	4246	4f3ffa1184f812d5328a8ce5973289d3b6aa2f119085935a940aee248489b632	utf8
"""Split a large graph_def.py into modules below the GitHub connector's ~1 MB limit.

Usage (from the repo root): python3 -I handoff/connector_kit/split_graph_def.py graphs/<App> [max_bytes]

The cut points are top-level statement boundaries (found with ast), so no statement is split.
The first chunk stays in graph_def.py (it holds TITLE, STRICT, USE_AUTO and the other settings);
the rest go to graphs/<App>/graph_def_mod/NN.py. A loader appended to graph_def.py runs the
modules in name order, in the same namespace, exactly as if they were still one file.
If graph_def_mod/ already exists, its modules are joined back first, so the script can be re-run
whenever a module grows past max_bytes. Check after every split: the build output
(out/methods.csv, out/<prefix>_trigger_graph.json) must be byte-identical to the build before the split.
"""
import ast
import os
import sys

LOADER_MARK = '# ---- graph_def_mod loader (handoff/connector_kit/split_graph_def.py)'
LOADER = LOADER_MARK + '''
# The rest of this graph is in graph_def_mod/NN.py (each file below 1 MB, so the GitHub connector can read and write it).
# The modules run in name order, in this same namespace, exactly as if they were one file. GDIR is set by tools/build_graph.py.
import os as _gdmod_os
for _gdmod_name in sorted(_gdmod_os.listdir(_gdmod_os.path.join(GDIR, 'graph_def_mod'))):
    if _gdmod_name.endswith('.py'):
        _gdmod_path = _gdmod_os.path.join(GDIR, 'graph_def_mod', _gdmod_name)
        exec(compile(open(_gdmod_path, encoding='utf-8').read(), _gdmod_path, 'exec'))
del _gdmod_os, _gdmod_name, _gdmod_path
'''


def joined_source(gdir):
    main = open(os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def.py'), encoding='utf-8').read()
    if LOADER_MARK not in main:
        return main
    head = main.split(LOADER_MARK)[0]
    mdir = os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def_mod')
    parts = [open(os.path.join(mdir, n), encoding='utf-8').read() for n in sorted(os.listdir(mdir)) if n.endswith('.py')]
    return head + ''.join(parts)


def main(argv):
    gdir = argv[1]
    limit = int(argv[2]) if len(argv) > 2 else 600000
    src = joined_source(gdir)
    lines = src.splitlines(keepends=True)
    tree = ast.parse(src)
    starts = [n.lineno for n in tree.body]
    # include comments/blank lines that precede a statement in that statement's chunk
    bounds = []
    for i, st in enumerate(tree.body):
        begin = 1 if i == 0 else tree.body[i - 1].end_lineno + 1
        bounds.append((begin, st.end_lineno))
    tail_begin = tree.body[-1].end_lineno + 1 if tree.body else 1
    chunks, cur, size = [], [], 0
    for begin, end in bounds:
        text = ''.join(lines[begin - 1:end])
        if cur and size + len(text.encode('utf-8')) > limit:
            chunks.append(''.join(cur))
            cur, size = [], 0
        cur.append(text)
        size += len(text.encode('utf-8'))
    cur.append(''.join(lines[tail_begin - 1:]))
    chunks.append(''.join(cur))
    for c in chunks:
        if len(c.encode('utf-8')) > 900000:
            raise SystemExit('a single top-level statement is larger than 900 KB; split it by hand')
    mdir = os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def_mod')
    if len(chunks) == 1:
        open(os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def.py'), 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(chunks[0])
        if os.path.isdir(mdir):
            for n in os.listdir(mdir):
                os.remove(os.path.join(mdir, n))
            os.rmdir(mdir)
        print('%s: one chunk (%d bytes), no modules needed' % (gdir, len(chunks[0].encode('utf-8'))))
        return 0
    os.makedirs(mdir, exist_ok=True)
    for n in os.listdir(mdir):
        os.remove(os.path.join(mdir, n))
    head = chunks[0]
    if not head.endswith('\n'):
        head += '\n'
    open(os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def.py'), 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(head + '\n' + LOADER)
    for i, c in enumerate(chunks[1:], 1):
        open(os.path.join(mdir, '%02d.py' % i), 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(c)
    print('%s: graph_def.py %d bytes + %d modules: %s' % (
        gdir, os.path.getsize(os.path.join(gdir, 'graph_def.py')), len(chunks) - 1,
        ', '.join('%02d.py %d' % (i, len(c.encode('utf-8'))) for i, c in enumerate(chunks[1:], 1))))
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

FILE	handoff/connector_kit/unpack.py	3167	159fd32361cf950038c64346ae7dea2223c8ac6593fbbc9861505c76c4565643	utf8
"""Rebuild a working tree from the bundle parts in handoff/workspace_bundle/ (standard library only).

Usage: python3 -I unpack.py <bundle_dir> <target_dir> [bundle ...]
  <bundle_dir>  local folder holding MANIFEST.json and the <bundle>/<bundle>.partNNN.txt files, fetched
                byte for byte from branch work-2026-10-31, handoff/workspace_bundle/
  <target_dir>  where the repo-relative files are written (use your working copy root)
  bundle ...    optional: only these bundles (default: all in the manifest)

Every part, every whole bundle and every file is checked against its SHA-256. Any mismatch stops the run
with the name of the bad part or file: fetch that part again, then rerun.
"""
import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys


def main(argv):
    bdir, target = argv[1], argv[2]
    man = json.load(open(os.path.join(bdir, 'MANIFEST.json')))
    names = argv[3:] or list(man['bundles'])
    total = 0
    for name in names:
        b = man['bundles'][name]
        blob = bytearray()
        for p in b['parts']:
            path = os.path.join(bdir, p['file'])
            if not os.path.isfile(path):
                raise SystemExit('MISSING part %s' % p['file'])
            data = open(path, 'rb').read()
            if len(data) != p['bytes'] or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != p['sha256']:
                raise SystemExit('BAD part %s (size %d, expected %d): fetch it again' % (p['file'], len(data), p['bytes']))
            blob += data
        if hashlib.sha256(bytes(blob)).hexdigest() != b['sha256']:
            raise SystemExit('BAD bundle %s after joining its parts' % name)
        pos = blob.index(b'\n') + 1
        if bytes(blob[:pos]) != b'GTRBUNDLE 1\n':
            raise SystemExit('bundle %s: unknown format' % name)
        n = 0
        while True:
            end = blob.index(b'\n', pos)
            line = bytes(blob[pos:end]).decode('utf-8')
            pos = end + 1
            if line == 'END':
                break
            tag, path, size, sha, enc = line.split('\t')
            if tag != 'FILE' or path.startswith('/') or '..' in path.split('/'):
                raise SystemExit('bundle %s: bad entry %r' % (name, line))
            size = int(size)
            if enc == 'utf8':
                data = bytes(blob[pos:pos + size])
                pos += size + 1
            else:
                end = blob.index(b'\n', pos)
                data = base64.b64decode(bytes(blob[pos:end]))
                pos = end + 1
            if len(data) != size or hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise SystemExit('BAD file %s in bundle %s' % (path, name))
            out = os.path.join(target, path)
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(out), exist_ok=True)
            open(out, 'wb').write(data)
            n += 1
        if n != b['files']:
            raise SystemExit('bundle %s: %d files written, manifest says %d' % (name, n, b['files']))
        total += n
        print('%-17s OK  %5d files' % (name, n))
    print('all bundles OK: %d files written under %s' % (total, target))
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv))

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Rebuild the trigger graphs and run the eight checks for the apps named as arguments ("all" or none = every app).
# Run from the repo root. Writes graphs/<App>/out/ (build outputs and README) and graphs/<App>/scratch/ci_latest/
# (1_build.txt ... 8b_scan_callers.txt and SUMMARY.txt). Used by .github/workflows/rebuild-graphs.yml; runs the same
# on any machine with python3 (no packages needed).
set -u
apps_all="Currency Diary hash-checker Omni-Notes openlauncher Vanilla-Music Markor AnyMemo Aegis AmazeFileManager AnkiDroid MMEX LibreTorrent"
prefix() {
  case "$1" in
    Currency) echo currency ;; Diary) echo diary ;; hash-checker) echo hash-checker ;; Omni-Notes) echo omni-notes ;;
    openlauncher) echo openlauncher ;; Vanilla-Music) echo vanilla ;; Markor) echo markor ;; AnyMemo) echo anymemo ;;
    Aegis) echo aegis ;; AmazeFileManager) echo amazefilemanager ;; AnkiDroid) echo ankidroid ;; MMEX) echo mmex ;;
    LibreTorrent) echo libretorrent ;; *) echo "" ;;
  esac
}
apps="$*"
if [ -z "$apps" ] || [ "$apps" = "all" ]; then apps="$apps_all"; fi
for a in $apps; do
  x=$(prefix "$a")
  if [ -z "$x" ]; then echo "unknown app: $a"; continue; fi
  o="graphs/$a/scratch/ci_latest"
  rm -rf "$o"; mkdir -p "$o"
  # the tools read the first graphs/<App>/out/*_trigger_graph.json they find: remove outputs of other prefixes first
  for f in graphs/$a/out/*_trigger_graph.json graphs/$a/out/*_trigger_graph.html; do
    [ -e "$f" ] || continue
    case "$(basename "$f")" in "${x}_trigger_graph.json"|"${x}_trigger_graph.html") ;; *) rm -f "$f"; echo "removed stale $f" ;; esac
  done
  python3 -I tools/build_graph.py "graphs/$a" "$x" > "$o/1_build.txt" 2>&1; echo "exit $?" >> "$o/1_build.txt"
  python3 -I tools/gen_readme_v2.py "$a" > "$o/gen_readme.txt" 2>&1
  python3 -I tools/check_citations.py "$a" > "$o/2_citations.txt" 2>&1
  python3 -I tools/check_routes.py "$a" > "$o/3_routes.txt" 2>&1
  python3 -I tools/check_bytecode_lines.py "$a" > "$o/4_bytecode.txt" 2>&1
  files="graphs/$a/graph_def.py"
  [ -d "graphs/$a/graph_def_mod" ] && files="$files $(ls graphs/$a/graph_def_mod/*.py)"
  [ "$a" = "LibreTorrent" ] && [ -d graphs/$a/parts ] && files="$files $(ls graphs/$a/parts/*.py 2>/dev/null)"
  {
    echo "DEAD|FRAMEWORK_ONLY lines: $(cat $files | grep -c 'DEAD\|FRAMEWORK_ONLY')"
    echo "FW-MEMORY lines: $(cat $files | grep -c 'FW-MEMORY')"
    echo "PENDING lines: $(cat $files | grep -c 'PENDING')"
    echo "STRICT = True line in graph_def.py: $(grep -c '^STRICT = True' graphs/$a/graph_def.py)"
    echo "files searched: $files"
  } > "$o/5_7_greps.txt"
  python3 -I tools/scan_unreachable.py "$a" --all > "$o/8a_scan_all.txt" 2>&1
  python3 -I tools/scan_unreachable.py "$a" --callers > "$o/8b_scan_callers.txt" 2>&1
  {
    echo "app $a | commit $(git rev-parse --short HEAD 2>/dev/null) | $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')"
    grep -E '^(methods|verdicts|unreachable by kind|labels confirmed by|verification (label|edge|path)|errors|warnings|exit)' "$o/1_build.txt"
    echo "check 2: $(grep -E '^citations' "$o/2_citations.txt" || tail -1 "$o/2_citations.txt")"
    echo "check 3: $(tail -1 "$o/3_routes.txt")"
    echo "check 4: $(grep -E 'where the cited line does not show the callee' "$o/4_bytecode.txt" || echo 'no summary line (see 4_bytecode.txt; a traceback means the check crashed)')"
    head -4 "$o/5_7_greps.txt" | sed 's/^/check 5-7: /'
    echo "check 8 (--callers): $(tail -1 "$o/8b_scan_callers.txt")"
    if [ -f "graphs/$a/verification.csv" ]; then
      echo "verification.csv rows: path $(grep -c '^path,' graphs/$a/verification.csv; true), DEVICE $(grep -c ',DEVICE,' graphs/$a/verification.csv; true)"
    else
      echo "verification.csv: missing"
    fi
  } > "$o/SUMMARY.txt"
  echo "== $a"; cat "$o/SUMMARY.txt"
done
